### [1] [Hypothesis-awkward: Property-Based Testing Strategies for Awkward Array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31820)
*Tai Sakuma,Ianna Osborne,Peter Elmer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Hypothesis-awkward 是一个为 Awkward Array 提供 Hypothesis 策略的库，用于属性测试以自动生成复杂嵌套数组数据，已在持续集成中发现 37 个 Awkward Array 缺陷和 1 个 Hypothesis 缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Awkward Array 表达复杂嵌套、变长、混合类型数据，传统单元测试仅覆盖少量预定义样本，难以探索庞大的组合空间边缘情况。

Method: 利用 Hypothesis 属性测试框架，实现生成几乎所有可能 Awkward Array 的主策略，支持控制布局、数据类型、缺失值、掩码等属性。

Result: 策略已集成到 Awkward Array 的持续集成中，覆盖部分功能并已发现 37 个 Awkward Array 缺陷和 1 个 Hypothesis 自身缺陷，显著提升测试可靠性。

Conclusion: Hypothesis-awkward 通过自动化生成全面测试数据，有效补充传统测试，增强 Awkward Array 及其生态工具的鲁棒性，推动属性测试在复杂数据结构中的应用。

Abstract: Hypothesis-awkward is a collection of Hypothesis strategies for Awkward Array. Awkward Array can represent a wide variety of nested, variable-length, mixed-type data. Many tools that process Awkward Arrays are widely used and actively developed. The unit test cases of many of these tools list predefined input samples. In practice, such samples can cover only a small portion of the vast combinatorial space of Awkward Array instances. Hypothesis, a Python property-based testing library, generates test data that can make test cases fail and automatically explores edge cases. The main strategy of Hypothesis-awkward generates nearly all possible Awkward Arrays, with options to control the layout, data types, missing values, masks, and other array attributes. Property-based tests written with these strategies have begun to run in the continuous integration of Awkward Array; to date, they cover a small part of Awkward Array and have found 37 bugs in it, as well as one in Hypothesis itself. These strategies make the tests of Awkward Array and of the tools that use it more reliable.

</details>


### [2] [Verification as an Architectural Layer for LLM Agents: A V-Model Design, and a Pilot Study of Its Deterministic Core](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31937)
*Ali Afoud,Jie JW Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于V模型的验证机制，将LLM智能体中策略选择、动作选择、格式化和结果评估四项职责拆分为不同层次，每个层次配有专用验证器，并由确定性控制器强制执行判决。只有验证结果写入内存，从而实现对故障源头的精确定位。验证器由零成本确定性“闸门”和可选LLM“法官”组成，可独立衡量其贡献和成本。实验证明，验证器能显著改善智能体的终止行为，而无验证的配置则完全失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于ReAct的LLM智能体将所有职责集中在单一模型中，没有外部机制来拒绝不充分的输出，导致无法进展的智能体一直运行直至被外部预算终止。研究者希望通过引入软件工程中的V模型，采用分层验证机制，实现对智能体输出的精细控制和故障定位。

Method: 设计了V模型架构：自顶向下的规范层次（需求、规格、单元/验收、集成），每层配备专用验证器，包括确定性“闸门”（零成本）和可选LLM“法官”。确定性控制器强制执行每个验证层的判决，只有验证结果写入内存。实验证实了单元级和验收级验证器对MuSiQue四层基座（8B参数）的有效性，在相同执行者、工具集和评分器的条件下，对五个验证配置进行了47次运行。

Result: 在47次运行中，无验证配置无法回答任何问题，均因步骤上限或令牌限制而终止。启用验证的配置（无规划器）回答了8个问题，其余问题被拒绝。确定性闸门贡献了9次修正中的8次，且不增加成本。引入规划器后验证效果下降。结果显示验证器能显著改善智能体的终止行为，而非大规模准确性。

Conclusion: 验证层架构能精确定位故障，改善智能体的终止决策，并为未来的扩展铺平道路，包括计划中对集成级验证器的验证。提出了一个为期12个月的计划，以完成全架构实施和评估。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents built on the ReAct pattern concentrate four responsibilities in one model: selecting a strategy, choosing each action, formatting it, and judging whether the result is adequate. Nothing outside the generative loop can reject its output, so an agent that cannot make progress does not report failure; it runs until an external budget stops it. We propose treating verification as an architectural layer by adapting the V-model from software engineering: specification levels descend from requirements to individual steps, each level is paired with a dedicated verifier, a deterministic controller enforces every verdict, and only verification outcomes write to memory, so a rejection localizes the level that introduced the fault and an agent halts by declining rather than by exhaustion. Each verifier separates a zero-cost deterministic \emph{gate} from an optional LLM \emph{judge}, so the contribution and cost of each can be measured independently. We report a pilot implementing the acceptance- and unit-level verifier pairs, comparing five configurations that share one executor, tool set, and scorer and differ only in verification, on the four-hop stratum of MuSiQue with an 8B-parameter backbone. Across 47 executions, the two unverified configurations answered none of ten questions, every run ending at a step cap or provider token limit; the verified configuration without a planner answered eight and abstained on the rest. Deterministic gates produced eight of the nine observed corrections at zero marginal cost, and planning degraded performance once verification was present. These results characterize termination behavior, not accuracy at scale; we outline a twelve-month plan to complete and evaluate the full architecture, including the integration-level pair the pilot omits.

</details>


### [3] [Methodological Harness in Agentic Software Engineering: An Empirical Study on Mining Software Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32014)
*Jessica Díaz,Jorge Pérez,Sergio Gil-Borrás*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文实证研究了代理软件工程中方法论 harness 的八个机制在 GitHub 仓库中的采用情况，发现机制主要以孤立形式存在，而非集成系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理软件工程需要协调和治理机制，提出的框架包含八个机制，但缺乏在仓库规模上的实证验证。

Method: 使用 AIDev 数据集 116,211 个 GitHub 仓库，分层抽样 5,435 个仓库（按星标衡量可见性）。RQ1 检测七个可观察机制的制品及引入时间；RQ2 对 150 个仓库的规则文件进行定性编码。

Result: 总体至少一种机制的普及率 21.7%，高可见度仓库为 65.3%。多机制配置罕见。规则文件几乎总是指导代理和陈述规范，而持久共享知识、可执行规范、结构化咨询和渐进自主仅在少数出现。

Conclusion: 该 harness 可被实证观察，但主要通过孤立机制而非框架提出的集成系统。

Abstract: Context. Agentic software engineering requires mechanisms to coordinate and govern agents' work. The framework motivating this study proposes a methodological harness with eight mechanisms: context engineering, persistent shared knowledge, executable specifications, N-version mindset and parallel agents, normative specifications, structured consultation, evidence-based acceptance, and graduated autonomy. These are realized through artifacts such as rule or context files, specifications, and architectural decision records, but the framework had not been empirically validated at repository scale. Objective. We analyze to what extent and how this harness is observable in repositories with agentic activity. RQ1 characterizes adoption through artifact prevalence, breadth, co-occurrence, and temporal evolution; RQ2 examines rule files - a key observable artifact and persistent source of agent instructions - to assess how their content reflects the proposed mechanisms. Method. From the AIDev dataset of 116,211 GitHub repositories, we analyzed 5,435 using a design stratified by visibility, measured through stars as an indicator of popularity and/or reputation. For RQ1, we detected and quantified artifacts and introduction dates for the seven observable mechanisms. For RQ2, we qualitatively coded rule files from 150 repositories. Results. Population prevalence of at least one mechanism is 21.7%, versus 65.3% among the most visible repositories. Multi-mechanism configurations are rare. Where rule files exist, they almost always guide the agent and state norms, while persistent shared knowledge, executable specifications, structured consultation, and graduated autonomy appear only in a minority. Conclusions. The harness is empirically observable, but mainly through isolated mechanisms rather than the integrated system proposed by the framework.

</details>


### [4] [CG-Diff: Organizing Code Changes Around Call Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32057)
*Bimal Raj Gyawali,Devamardeep Hayatpur,Nadia Polikarpova*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 现有代码审查工具按文件展示改动，但改动常以调用图为更好组织，研究发现40% PR中超过半数函数在调用图内且跨文件，提出CG-Diffs把调用图分解为小图，实现网页界面，用户研究显示帮助定位并提供更有意义的导航，但也有重复节点和全局改动不明显的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有审查工具按文件展示改动，缺乏对函数调用关系的组织，导致新贡献者或审阅 unfamiliar LLM PR 困难。

Method: 实证分析 GitHub PR，发现调用图跨文件的比例，提出 CG-Diffs 将调用图分解为更小的子图，并实现网页界面，进行 within-subject 用户研究对比 GitHub 原始界面。

Result: 研究表明 CG-Diffs 能帮助用户更好定位并提供更有意义的导航结构，但出现函数节点重复导致 disorienting，且全局改动（如 imports）不易显现。

Conclusion: 利用调用图组织代码审查有助于新贡献者和 LLM 生成 PR 的审阅，展示出潜在价值，但需要改进以解决重复节点和全局改动的可视化问题。

Abstract: Tools for code review present code changes file-by-file. We argue that, oftentimes, changes can be better organized around a call graph of the changed code. From an empirical study of GitHub pull requests (PRs) in-the-wild, we find that (1) in around 40% of the PRs, more than half of the changed functions (and methods) are connected by call graphs, and (2) necessary callees are often located in different files from their callers. Based on these findings, we develop the notion of CG-Diffs, subgraphs derived by decomposing a call graph of the changed code into smaller and more navigable directed graphs. We then implement a web-based interface for viewing PRs that restructures the PR around these CG-Diffs. Through a within-subject study comparing our interface against GitHub's PR view, we find that CG-Diffs help orient participants and provide them with more meaningful structures to navigate the PR. We also found several limitations: function nodes repeated across multiple CG-Diffs can be disorienting, and changes not contained in a function (e.g. globals, imports) are not as immediately apparent. Our study shows promise in using call graphs to help contextualize and navigate unfamiliar codebases, which may benefit new contributors to open source, and reviewers of unfamiliar LLM-generated PRs.

</details>


### [5] [IChart2Code: Benchmarking Multimodal Large Language Models for Interactive Chart Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32413)
*Xu Zhang,Hongzhang Zheng,Zhili Huang,Yaoyi Wang,Ling Xu,Sheng Huang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出了交互式图表代码生成基准 IChart2Code 和轨迹引导的双智能体框架 TRAIL。IChart2Code 包含377个任务和1209条交互需求，并设计了基于浏览器的多维评估协议；TRAIL 框架通过执行轨迹诊断和结构化反馈，显著提升了多模态大模型在交互式图表代码生成上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图表代码生成基准主要关注静态输出，缺乏对用户交互规范、浏览器执行以及交互后状态变化验证的有效任务表示和评估协议，无法全面衡量模型生成交互式图表代码的能力。

Method: 1) 构建了 IChart2Code 基准，提供参考截图、局部数据和自然语言交互需求作为输入，目标为可执行的HTML/JS代码。2) 设计了基于浏览器的评估协议，包含可执行性门控以及数据保真度、静态视觉正确性、交互正确性三个维度，并使用 rubric 引导的 MLLM 进行评判。3) 提出 TRAIL 双智能体框架，其中 Inspector 智能体生成任务特定的检查项，在浏览器中执行并收集轨迹，诊断失败原因并生成结构化的修复反馈。

Result: MLLM 评判器与人工标注对比的 item 级 F1 分数达到 0.8844。在四个 MLLM 上的实验表明，TRAIL 框架相比直接提示，在四个评估维度上分别提升了 7.89、4.98、3.45 和 4.47 个百分点。

Conclusion: IChart2Code 为交互式图表代码生成提供了可靠的评估基准，而 TRAIL 框架通过引入动态执行轨迹和双智能体反馈机制，有效解决了直接生成中的交互逻辑与视觉对齐问题，显著提升了代码生成质量。

Abstract: Interactive chart code generation requires models to reproduce a reference chart's appearance and underlying data and correctly implement the state changes triggered by specified user interactions. Existing chart-to-code benchmarks focus on static outputs and lack task representations or evaluation protocols for interaction specification, browser execution, and post-interaction verification. We introduce IChart2Code, a benchmark comprising 377 tasks across 20 chart forms and 13 data families, with 1209 interaction requirements in six families. Each task provides a reference screenshot, task-local data, and natural-language interaction requirements, with executable HTML/JavaScript code as the target output. We further develop a browser-based evaluation protocol with an Executability gate and three rubric-guided dimensions: Data Fidelity, Static Visual Correctness, and Interaction Correctness. The protocol tests runtime viability, consistency with task-local data, fidelity of the initial rendering to the reference screenshot, and interaction-induced state changes in a sandboxed browser. A rubric-guided MLLM judge evaluates task-specific items for the three scored dimensions using the collected browser observations and achieves an overall item-level F1 score of 0.8844 against adjudicated human labels. We also propose TRAIL, a trajectory-guided dual-agent framework for interactive chart code generation. An Inspector derives task-specific inspection checks, executes them in the browser, and uses the resulting trajectories to diagnose failures and produce structured repair feedback. Averaged across four MLLMs, TRAIL improves the four evaluation dimensions over direct prompting by 7.89, 4.98, 3.45, and 4.47 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [6] [How AI Changes DevOps Performance: A Mechanism-Based Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32414)
*Mamdouh Alenezi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过离散事件模拟和实验设计，分析了AI生成代码（AGC）和自主代理AI（AGT）对DORA性能指标的影响。发现AGC增加了失败率、重构时间和恢复时间，而AGT显著提升了部署频率、领导时间和恢复时间，但稳定性效应取决于自动化CI修复机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有证据混杂且多为横截面研究，常将AGC与AGT混淆。本研究旨在厘清两者对DORA关键指标的独立影响，并建立包含四个能力调节者的模型。

Method: 通过证据审计、构建双构造模型（含四个能力调节器）、离散事件仿真以及六种实验设计（包括因子设计、Shapley分解、敏感性分析过600参数抽样、固定需求稳健性测试和5000支队伍的阶梯采用面板与反事实）进行分析。

Result: AGC在所有能力层级均增加了失败率、重构时间和恢复时间；当AI增加变更量时，AGC仅在审查容量充足时缩短领导时间（-13%），而在审查饱和时延长；在低能力团队中，AGC的部署频率收益完全来自未计划重构。AGT显著改善了部署频率（+31%至+34%）、领导时间（-8%至-22%）和恢复时间（-29%至-38%）。AGT的稳定性效应依赖于自动化CI修复掩盖缺陷，逃逸缺陷在高能力团队中增加。能力调节了AGC的绝对失败率惩罚（7.9点vs.1.5点）但未改变其相对惩罚（+34%vs.+84%）。两向固定效应低估了采用效应8-16%，检测稳定效应需约50支队伍，能力调节需约400支队伍。

Conclusion: DORA指标对AI的响应涉及排队、批处理和监督机制，而不仅仅是代码质量。研究提出了测量规则、代理修复守门线和样本量指导的实地验证协议。

Abstract: Context. AI tools for software development have progressed from code completion to autonomous agents, yet evidence on their effects on DORA performance is mixed, mostly cross-sectional, and often conflates AI-generated code (AGC) with agentic AI (AGT). Objective. We examine how AGC and AGT, separately and jointly, affect deployment frequency, lead time, change failure rate, recovery time, and deployment rework rate. Method. We audit the evidence, formalise a two-construct model with four capability moderators, and implement a discrete-event simulation of a delivery pipeline. Six experiments include a factorial design, Shapley decompositions, sensitivity analysis over 600 parameter draws, a fixed-demand robustness test, and a staggered-adoption panel of 5,000 simulated teams with counterfactuals. Results. AGC increased failure rate, rework, and recovery time in both capability profiles. When AI increased change volume, AGC shortened lead time only where review capacity was spare (-13%) and lengthened it once review saturated; in the low-capability team, its deployment-frequency gain came entirely from unplanned rework. AGT improved deployment frequency (+31% to +34%), lead time (-8% to -22%), and recovery time (-29% to -38%), but its stability effect depended on autonomous CI repair masking defects, and escaped defects increased in the high-capability team. Capability reduced AGC's absolute failure-rate penalty (7.9 vs. 1.5 points) but not its relative penalty (+34% vs. +84%). Two-way fixed effects underestimated adoption effects by 8-16%; detecting the modelled stability effect required about 50 teams, and its capability moderation about 400. Conclusions. Within the model, DORA metrics respond to AI through queueing, batching, and oversight, not only code quality. We derive measurement rules, guardrails for agentic remediation, and a sample-size-informed field-validation protocol.

</details>


### [7] [Beyond the Model: Demystifying Harness Effects in Software Engineering Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32459)
*Haichuan Hu,Quanjun Zhang,Shengcheng Yu,Zhifei Chen,Tianyu Luo,Chunrong Fang,Zhenyu Chen,Liang Xiao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 轻量模块化框架NanoHarness显著提升SEAgent性能，强调Harness设计对LLM代理效果的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统认为模型性能决定代理表现，但Harness设计影响交互、执行和验证，且不同模型、任务下作用未明，需要系统研究。

Method: 评估mini-SWE-agent和OpenCode两种Harness与十个Qwen、DeepSeek模型在三个基准（SWE-bench Pro、ProgramBench、GitTaskBench），并构建NanoHarness，分析五个组件（工具注册表、上下文压缩、显式规划、子代理、懒惰技能），测量性能提升。

Result: Harness效果取决于模型能力和任务类型；复杂Harness对模型提升的边际收益递减，对复杂开放式任务有帮助；结构化工具使用和任务特定子代理最稳健，上下文压缩和通用子代理可能有害；NanoHarness在Qwen3.7-Max上提升7.37%，DeepSeek-V4-Pro提升6.21%，接近产品级Harness收益。

Conclusion: Harness设计是影响SE-Agent性能的关键因素，需作为首要考虑，并为构建更高效、高效能的编程代理提供指导。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents are increasingly used for software engineering tasks, yet their performance is not determined by the base model alone. The agent harness substantially shapes how SE agents interact with repositories, execute actions, and validate solutions. However, the role of harness design remains insufficiently understood, especially across different models, tasks, and harness components. In this paper, we present a systematic empirical study of harness effects in SE agents. We first evaluate two representative harnesses, mini-SWE-agent and OpenCode, with ten models from two prominent open-weight model families, Qwen and DeepSeek, on three benchmarks: SWE-bench Pro, ProgramBench, and GitTaskBench. We then construct NanoHarness, a lightweight modular harness built on top of mini-SWE-agent, and use it to analyze five representative harness components: tool registry, context compression, explicit planning, subagents, and lazy skills. Experimental results show that harness effectiveness depends jointly on model capability and task type. Complex harnesses provide diminishing marginal gains on SWE-style issue repair as model capability improves, but can benefit stronger models on more complex and open-ended repository-level tasks. Component-level analysis on ProgramBench further shows that structured tool use and task-specific subagents provide the most stable improvements, while context compression and general subagents can hurt repository-generation performance. When combined, NanoHarness improves over mini-SWE-agent by 7.37 and 6.21 percentage points on Qwen3.7-Max and DeepSeek-V4-Pro, respectively, recovering most of the gains of product-level harnesses. These findings highlight harness design as a first-class factor in SE-agent performance and provide insights for building more effective and efficient coding agents.

</details>


### [8] [Evolution of Deprecated APIs and Their Replacements in Python Libraries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32618)
*Gangqiang He,Guanping Xiao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究分析了Python库中弃用API与替代API的关系，发现替代局部性、参数差异、版本间排名变化及生命周期事件对迁移有重要影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究分别考察弃用、替代识别和客户端迁移，缺乏跨版本和生命周期状态下弃用API与替代API关系的系统理解。

Method: 从33个Python库构建了830个维护者指定的映射，涵盖类、函数、方法；对比定义，基于相似度跟踪两种迁移场景下的排名，通过源码检查和逐版本执行识别生命周期事件。

Result: 同模块替代在类、函数、方法映射中分别占39.1%、55.6%、79.9%；多数映射无参数变化，但函数映射变化更频繁多样；排名随版本和代码表示变化，两种方法仅在约60%情况下趋势一致；其他候选影响在49.0%关键版本中占主导或混合；弃用后56.4%映射保留源定义，68.6%保留可执行原始调用；在753个至少有一个结束事件的映射中，25.2%为源优先或调用优先而非同一版本，其中77.4%为源优先。

Conclusion: 弃用API与替代API关系取决于API粒度、发布上下文、代码表示、候选竞争和生命周期状态，结果支持演化感知的替代API推荐和自动化API迁移。

Abstract: Context: API deprecation is common in library evolution, but migration requires finding replacements and adapting calls. Prior studies examine deprecation, replacement identification, and client migration separately, leaving deprecated API-replacement API relationships across releases and lifecycle states insufficiently understood. Objective: We examine replacement locality, parameter-interface differences, changes in replacement rankings across versions, and post-deprecation lifecycles from source-definition and original-invocation perspectives. Method: We construct 830 maintainer-specified mappings from 33 Python libraries, covering classes, functions, and methods. We compare definitions, track similarity-based rankings under two migration scenarios, and identify lifecycle events through source inspection and release-by-release execution. Results: Same-module replacements account for 39.1%, 55.6%, and 79.9% of class, function, and method mappings, respectively. Most mappings have no parameter changes, but function mappings show more frequent and diverse changes. Rankings vary across releases and code representations, and the applicable methods agree on the overall trend in only about 60% of cases. Other-candidate effects are dominant or mixed in 49.0% of key versions. After deprecation, 56.4% of mappings retain the source definition and 68.6% retain an executable original invocation. Among 753 mappings with at least one observed ending event, 25.2% are source-first or invocation-first rather than same-release. Of these cases, 77.4% are source-first. Conclusions: Deprecated API-replacement API relationships depend on API granularity, release context, code representation, candidate competition, and lifecycle state. These results support evolution-aware replacement API recommendation and automated API migration.

</details>


### [9] [AsynCodeBench: Benchmarking Collaboration of Asynchronous Multi-Agent Systems in Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32662)
*Kaituo Zhang,Zhen Xiong,Zhimeng Jiang,Mingyu Zhong,Zhouyuan Yuan,Zhecheng Li,Bowen Lin,Chia-Yuan Chang,Mingzhi Hu,Huazheng Wang,Ying Lin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AsynCodeBench introduces a dependencyⶏcentric benchmark for evaluating asynchronous multi‑agent coding. It defines 19 real‑world tasks with explicit directed dependency graphs and executable Dependency Checkers, enabling two novel collaboration metrics: Asynchronous Dependency Pass Rate (ADPR) and Dependency Resolution Step (DRS). Experiments show a clear gap between coding ability and collaboration; better code generation does not guarantee effective coordination, and task‑level scores can diverge from dependency‑level performance. Analysis reveals that successful coordination often occurs via concentrated “hopping window” bursts rather than gradual progress.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current multi‑agent coding benchmarks evaluate systems solely on task outcomes inherited from single‑agent settings, conflating individual coding skill with cross‑agent coordination. There is a need for a direct, dependency‑centric measure of collaboration in asynchronous multi‑agent software engineering.

Method: Developed AsynCodeBench: a benchmark comprising 19 real‑world repository tasks, each represented by an explicit directed dependency graph and executable Dependency Checkers. Introduced two complementary collaboration metrics—Asynchronous Dependency Pass Rate (ADPR) and Dependency Resolution Step (DRS). Conducted extensive experiments across model families, scales, and generations, and performed dependency‑trajectory analysis to identify coordination patterns.

Result: Results reveal a substantial gap between coding performance and collaboration capability; improvements in code generation do not consistently translate to better cross‑agent dependency satisfaction. Task‑level metrics often diverge from dependency‑level measures. Dependency‑trajectory analysis uncovers a “hopping window” pattern where many dependencies are resolved in concentrated bursts rather than gradually.

Conclusion: AsynCodeBench provides a focused, dependency‑centric evaluation framework for asynchronous multi‑agent software engineering. It highlights the need to optimize both coding and collaborative coordination separately, and suggests that effective multi‑agent collaboration is characterized by periodic, bursty dependency resolution rather than steady incremental progress.

Abstract: Multi-agent coding has emerged as an increasingly active direction in software engineering, where complex development tasks are decomposed across multiple specialized agents working on different parts of the problem. Despite the shift from individual problem solving to distributed collaboration, multi-agent systems still lack a direct measure of collaboration and are largely evaluated through task-level outcomes inherited from single-agent coding, conflating individual coding capability with cross-agent coordination. We introduce AsynCodeBench, a dependency-centric benchmark for asynchronous multi-agent software engineering that represents each task with an explicit dependency graph and executable Dependency Checkers. Through this dependency-tracking process, we propose two complementary measures: Asynchronous Dependency Pass Rate (ADPR), which measures how many cross-agent dependencies are ultimately satisfied, and Dependency Resolution Step (DRS), which measures when each dependency first becomes satisfied during execution. AsynCodeBench comprises 19 tasks from real-world repositories, exposing 52 directed dependencies as explicit units for evaluating cross-agent collaboration. Experiments across model families, scales, and generations reveal a clear gap between coding and collaboration capability: improvements in coding performance do not necessarily translate into stronger collaboration, and task-level metrics can diverge substantially from dependency-level collaboration measures. Dependency-trajectory analysis further reveals that successful coordination often emerges not gradually, but through concentrated bursts in which many dependencies become resolved over a short portion of the execution trajectory, a pattern we term a hopping window.

</details>


### [10] [Social3Source: Connecting Social Provenance, Societal Qualities, and Social Purpose on an Open-Source Software Foundation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32823)
*Jordi Cabot*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Social3Source概念，强调软件开发需超越开源发布，融入社会价值，关注开发过程、社会品质和服务目的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管软件创造和部署速度空前加快，但缺乏社会贡献导向，需将社会考量融入软件开发。

Method: 提出Social3Source概念及其原则，探讨如何将社会因素整合到开源软件开发中。

Result: 构筑Social3Source理论框架，为社会化开源软件开发提供初始思路。

Conclusion: 软件发展应以造福社会为终极目标，确保证开发的软件具有实际社会价值。

Abstract: The speed at which software can be created and deployed today has increased dramatically. But what is the point of all this software if it does not contribute to society? The ultimate goal of building software should be bettering human lives and society. And this goes beyond publishing the software as open source; it requires considering how it is developed, the societal qualities it embodies, and the purposes it serves. This is what I call Social3Source. This paper presents the concept, principles, and initial discussion for the implementation of Social3Source, illustrating how it integrates social considerations into open-source software development. We have never generated as much software as we do today. Let's make sure it counts.

</details>


### [11] [What Does a Skill Actually Do? Estimands and Evaluation Validity for Tool and Skill Use in LLM Agents: A Critical Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33153)
*Shuyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于大型语言模型（LLM）智能体工具与技能库评估的批判性综述。通过引入基于估计量（estimand）的评估框架，从六个维度系统分析了现有研究的设计差异与假设，旨在澄清“提升”效果背后的不同设计选择与识别假设，提升评估的严谨性与可比性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM智能体广泛集成外部工具和技能库，现有文献中关于其“性能提升”的报告存在概念混淆。不同的评估指标（如检索召回率、成功率变化、预算约束下的增益等）对应着不同的研究问题和假设，缺乏统一的澄清框架。

Method: 采用批判性综述方法，结合估计量理论，构建了包含六个维度（处理对比、目标人群、结果、预算约束、汇总度量、识别假设）的分析框架。通过分析13项核心实证研究、方法论工作及分析性示例（toy examples），对不同的评估设计进行解构和对比。

Result: 明确了若干关键区别：任务配对不等于识别调用效应；配对增益衡量的是协议不一致性而非恶化任务占比；模块部署总效应与预算约束效率是不同问题。同时比较了 curated 技能与检索器替换、触发子集与全任务结果等设计。

Conclusion: 提出了一个报告清单和示例，要求研究者明确报告其评估设计的具体维度和假设。这有助于减少概念混淆，提高不同研究之间实证结果的可比性和可靠性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly draw on external tools and reusable skills selected at run time from libraries that hold thousands of entries. Reports that a retriever, router, or skill library "improves" an agent may refer to retrieval recall, the success change from enabling a library, a paired contrast restricted to triggered tasks, or a gain under an approximate budget constraint. This critical review asks what each design compares and under which assumptions. Building on estimand-based approaches to agent evaluation, we describe tool and skill designs along six axes: treatment contrast, target population, outcome, budget constraint, summary measure, and identification assumptions. Thirteen core empirical studies anchor the evidence synthesis, supplemented by related methodological work and design-level reading of the wider literature. Our contribution is to make explicit distinctions that some source authors already acknowledge through a decomposition of trigger-conditioned pairing, analytic counterexamples, and comparisons across studies. Pairing on the task does not by itself identify an invocation effect; paired gain and regression counts measure protocol-specific discordance rather than the share of tasks whose expected outcomes worsen; and total effects of module deployment answer a different question from budget-constrained efficiency. We compare curated skill provision with retriever replacement, triggered subsets with all-task outcomes, and observed cost reductions with budget-constrained comparisons. A reporting checklist and worked examples connect these distinctions to information that studies can report. The review runs no new experiments; empirical results come from the cited studies, and numerical toy examples are analytical illustrations.

</details>


### [12] [WideSWE: Can Coding Agents Coordinate Changes Across Repositories?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33382)
*Baoyi Wang,Xingliang Wang,Jinyang Wu,Keming Wu,Chen Zhi,Jianwei Yin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: WideSWE introduces a new benchmark of 120 real-world cross‑repository tasks (60 bug fixes, 60 features) mined from 103 software ecosystems, evaluating coding agents beyond single‑repo scenarios. The study finds task success ranging from 10.8 % to 42.5 % across seven agent configurations, with Codex CLI paired with GPT‑5.6‑sol achieving the highest rate. Joint execution of multiple repositories outperforms isolated work, highlighting the need for agents to coordinate across repos.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing coding‑agent evaluations focus on single‑codebase tasks, yet many real‑world changes involve coordinated modifications across several repositories. This limitation hampers realistic assessment of agent capabilities, prompting the creation of a benchmark that reflects cross‑repository development patterns.

Method: The authors mined issue‑PR pairs from 103 open‑source ecosystems, extracting 120 representative tasks. They crafted natural‑language prompts, designed hidden test suites that allow diverse correct implementations while preserving required behavior, and evaluated seven agent configurations under both independent per‑repo execution and joint execution modes.

Result: Performance spans 10.83 % to 42.50 % full task success. The best configuration (Codex CLI + GPT‑5.6‑sol) reaches 42.5 %. Analysis of failure trajectories shows common issues: missing necessary changes, incomplete implementation, or incorrect modifications. Joint execution recovers many omitted steps and corrects prior failed attempts more effectively than independent execution.

Conclusion: WideSWE reveals significant gaps in current coding agents when handling cross‑repository work. Joint planning and information sharing across repos improve outcomes, suggesting future agents should integrate multi‑repo context and coordination mechanisms.

Abstract: Coding-agent evaluation has progressed from resolving individual issues to carrying out long-horizon development, yet task completion is still largely assessed within a single codebase. In software ecosystems, many features and bug fixes require coordinated changes across multiple repositories. We introduce WideSWE to evaluate coding agents on such cross-repository tasks. Mining and reviewing changes across 103 software ecosystems yields 120 real-world tasks, balanced between 60 bug fixes and 60 features. We derive prompts from related issues and pull requests. We systematically review and adapt hidden tests to support diverse correct implementations while preserving required behavior and regression checks. Across seven agent configurations, full task success ranges from 10.83% to 42.50%, with the configuration pairing Codex CLI with GPT-5.6-sol achieving the highest rate. Trajectories show agents failing to identify necessary changes, recognizing changes but leaving them unfinished, or modifying the required repositories without fully satisfying the request. To examine whether working on one repository at a time can alleviate these difficulties, we compare it with joint execution under identical prompts. Independent execution mainly recovers omitted work and is less effective at correcting previously attempted but unsuccessful implementations. Joint execution can use information from related repositories to guide implementation and verification. Code is available at https://github.com/ZJU-ACES-ISE/WideSWE.

</details>


### [13] [Graph-Guided Repository Environment Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33429)
*Jianying Pan,John Zhang,Hongyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Graph2Env 是一种基于依赖图的代理方法，用于构建可靠的代码执行环境。它通过持续反馈和状态追踪来优化环境构建过程，在 200 个 Python 仓库的基准测试中实现了 81.0% 的环境构建成功率和 59.3% 的环境设置成功率，显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码代理越来越依赖执行来验证解决方案，但构建可靠的执行环境具有挑战性，因为执行要求分散在仓库组件中，并可能仅在执行过程中才变得清晰。现有的基于交互的方法无法有效地跟踪当前构建状态，包括发现的需求、满足和未满足的先决条件以及它们的依赖关系。

Method: Graph2Env 使用 DepGraph（一个类型化的依赖图）来明确表示仓库执行所需的环境需求及其依赖关系和状态。该方法利用 DepGraph 指导环境构建，并根据执行反馈不断完善，同时将成功修复保存在可重放的构建程序中。最终生成的产物在新环境中被应用以验证可重复构建的能力。

Result: 在 200 个来自 RATBench 和 EnvBench 的 Python 仓库基准测试中，Graph2Env 实现了 81.0% 的环境构建成功率（EBSR）和 59.3% 的环境设置成功率（ESSR），分别比最强基线高出 9.5 和 9.0 个百分点。

Conclusion: Graph2Env 通过将依赖图与执行反馈结合，为编码代理提供了一个可靠且可重现的执行环境构建框架。其在多个基准测试中的卓越表现表明，该方法有效解决了分布式环境状态追踪问题，并显著优于现有的静态依赖推断工具和专用环境构建系统。

Abstract: Coding agents now increasingly rely on execution to validate their solutions, making the construction of reliable execution environments a critical enabling capability. However, repository environment construction is challenging because execution requirements are fragmented across repository artifacts and may only become apparent during execution. Existing agent-based approaches address this problem through iterative interaction, but information about the current construction state, including discovered requirements, satisfied and unresolved prerequisites, and their dependencies, can remain distributed across the interaction history. We present Graph2Env, an agent-based approach centered on DepGraph, a typed dependency graph that explicitly represents the environment requirements needed for repository execution, their dependency relations, and their states. Graph2Env uses DepGraph to guide environment construction and continuously refines it with execution feedback, while persisting successful repairs into a replayable construction procedure. The resulting artifacts are then applied in a fresh environment to verify that the constructed environment can be reproduced. We evaluate Graph2Env on a benchmark of 200 Python repositories drawn from RATBench and EnvBench, against a static dependency-inference baseline (pipreqs), three specialized environment-construction systems (Repo2Run, RAT, and SetupX), and two general-purpose coding agents (SWE-agent and Claude Code). Graph2Env achieves an 81.0% Environment Build Success Rate (EBSR) and a 59.3% Environment Setup Success Rate (ESSR), outperforming the strongest baseline by 9.5 and 9.0 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [14] [Path2Spec: Path-Aware Specification Generation via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33438)
*Dan Huang,Zhensu Sun,Huihui Huang,Jinfeng Jiang,Xiaofei Xie,Yintong Huo,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 Path2Spec 框架，通过路径级规约生成与分解重试策略，显著提升 LLM 生成形式化规约的质量与覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 规约生成方法虽验证通过率高，但 34.6% 规约未捕捉程序独特行为，原因在于将程序视为整体导致约束过于粗糙。

Method: Path2Spec 采用分治策略：提取所有执行路径，为每条路径生成专用规约并合并；复杂程序则按逻辑分支递归分解为子程序，生成规约后再合并。

Result: 在 SG-Bench (120 程序) 上达 87.5% 通过率，优于 SpecGen 66.7%；在 SV-COMP (265 程序) 上达 83.0%，优于 44.2%。人工评估确认语义对齐更精准。

Conclusion: Path2Spec 通过细粒度路径推理与分解重试，有效解决了规约质量隐患，实现了更高质量的自动化形式化规约生成。

Abstract: Formal specifications are critical for program verification, comprehension, and maintenance. However, manually writing them is costly and difficult to scale. Recent studies have shown that Large Language Models (LLMs) are promising for automated specification generation, but existing methods suffer from quality issues. We analyze a state-of-the-art approach and find that at least 34.6% of successfully verified specifications actually fail to meaningfully capture the program's distinct behavior, which is a quality issue not captured by metrics that only measure verification success. We further found that a major factor contributing to such hidden quality issues stems from the design of existing methods: these methods treat a program as a single unit, resulting in overly general, coarse-grained constraints. To this end, we introduce Path2Spec, a divide-and-conquer framework that addresses these limitations through systematic path-based reasoning. Path2Spec leverages LLMs to extract all execution paths from an input program, generates path-specific specifications for each, and merges them into a comprehensive overall specification. For complex programs where path-based generation struggles, Path2Spec employs a decompose-then-retry strategy that recursively breaks a program into smaller subprograms based on logical branches, generates specifications for each, and merges them back. We evaluate Path2Spec on two public benchmarks: SG-Bench (120 programs) and SV-COMP (265 programs). Results show that Path2Spec can outperform the state-of-the-art baseline SpecGen: 87.5% versus 66.7% on SG-Bench, and 83.0% versus 44.2% on SV-COMP. Human evaluation further validates that Path2Spec generates higher-quality specifications with precise semantic alignment to the code.

</details>


### [15] [Maat: Independent Deterministic Contract-Based Governance for Multi-Agent LLM Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34017)
*Uliana Elina*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Maat——一种确定性运行时治理层，用于在LLM多智能体系统中验证智能体间的交接是否符合工作流程契约（anchor），而不依赖语言模型。实验在6个领域工作流程中进行，共522次试验，注入数据级缺陷并使用七项确定性检查。结果显示，在部分工作流程中治理组得分提升2.9%-26.5%，但审计发现评分工具存在偏差；手工审查94次停止行为发现37%为虚假警报，由校验器缺陷引起，导致治理组在4/6工作流程中表现更差。研究表明确定性交接验证对契约可表达缺陷有效，但校验器配置和停止归因需额外测试，不具备普适性正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统存在可靠性问题：一智(agent错误可能被下游智能体接受为上下文并在工作流程中传播。现有 safeguard 多依赖概率性的 judge，无法确定性拦截违反契约的缺陷。作者希望探索一种确定性方法，在不引入语言模型的情况下验证智能体间交接是否符合预定义工作流程契约，从而早期拦截缺陷。

Method: 提出Maat治理层：运行时根据版本化工作流程契约(anchor)验证智能体间交接，校验逻辑确定，无语言模型参与。实验在6个受控领域工作流程中进行(含6-15个智能体)，共522次对试，其中注入数据级缺陷。使用七项确定性检查 rubric评估，比较治理组与未治理组。额外进行停止归因分析(early halt是否真实缺陷)、模型调用成本分析、手工审查94次停止行为。

Result: V1结果显示治理组在全部6个工作流程中得分提升(2.9%-26.5%)。但发布后审计指出3个基准 scorer 将提前停止误认为已防止缺陷。仅在治理组完成或因确认缺陷而提前停止的配对试验中，5个工作流程 rubric得分上升7.7%-29.1%，软件开发工作流程持平；存在归因性提前停止时模型调用成本下降17%-53%。手工审查显示94次停止中35次为虚假警报(37%)，由校验器缺陷非模型行为所致；若将这些停止计为失败，则治理组在4/6工作流程中低于未治理组。

Conclusion: 确定性交接校验对契约可表达缺陷有效，但其自身的校验器配置与停止归因必须被测试；结果仅证明该方法在特定场景下的有效性，而非普适性正确性、语言模型无关的效果或幻觉检测能力。Maat作为辅助性确定性治理层值得实施，但不应作为唯一可靠性保障。

Abstract: Large-language-model multi-agent systems (LLM-MAS) introduce a characteristic reliability problem: an error produced by one agent can be accepted as context by downstream agents and propagate across the workflow. Many proposed safeguards rely on learned or LLM-based judges whose verdicts are themselves probabilistic; we ask whether a deterministic layer can instead stop contract-detectable handoff defects. We present Maat, a runtime governance layer that validates agent-to-agent handoffs against a versioned workflow contract, or anchor, with no language model in the validation or scoring path. We evaluate it in six controlled domain workflows (6-15 agents, 522 trials) with injected data-level defects and a deterministic seven-check rubric. Version 1 reported gains in all six workflows (2.9-26.5%). A post-publication audit found that three benchmark scorers credited any early halt as a prevented defect. On paired trials where the governed run completed or halted on a finding attributable to a verified defect, the rubric score changes by +7.7% to +29.1% in five workflows and is flat in software development; model-call cost falls 17-53% where attributable halts occur early. A hand review of all 94 governed-arm halts found 35 false alarms (37%), caused by validator defects rather than model behaviour; counting those halts as failed work, the governed arm scores below the ungoverned arm in four of six workflows. The results support deterministic handoff validation for contract-expressible defects and show that validator configuration and halt attribution must themselves be tested; they do not establish universal correctness, hallucination detection, or model-independent effectiveness.

</details>


### [16] [Neuro-Symbolic Indirect-Call Analysis under Opaque Pointers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33547)
*Kaixuan Li,Bozhi Wu,Jian Zhang,Peixin Wang,Ting Su,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Facet is a type-based indirect call analysis for C that reconstructs dispatch relations over LLVM's opaque (type-erased) pointers by recovering structure field identities and associated functions, achieving higher recall and better precision than prior methods on 14 C programs, and enabling deep bug detection in real-world software.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Resolving indirect calls is critical for accurate call-graph construction in C. Previous scalable type-based analyses (like MLTA) relied on LLVM IR pointee types, which fail when a single pointee type misrepresents memory layout and are fully broken under LLVM 17's removal of pointee types in favor of opaque pointers. This loss of type information removes the key used by field-sensitive analyses to match indirect calls to candidate functions.

Method: Facet reconstructs the dispatch relation over opaque IR without requiring end-to-end value-flow paths. It first identifies the structure field from which an indirect call loads its function pointer, then separately recovers all functions assigned to that field through initializers, stores, and aggregate copies. These are joined by field identity. Facet applies distinct evidence rules to classify and refine proposed call-graph edges (addition/removal) and records assumptions for each refinement. A lightweight symbolic bound restricts candidate selection, with an LLM resolving only residual edge cases. The analysis produces two graphs: a recall-preserving call graph and a refined, more precise version.

Result: Evaluated on 14 C programs, Facet reduced mean target-set size from 25.9 (baseline) to 5.2 and raised observed recall from 0.79 to 0.99. Field identities recovered by Facet matched typed IR at 98.1% of jointly resolved indirect-call sites. When applied to bug detection in production C software (e.g., nginx, Linux kernel), the refined call graph found 17 deep bugs, three latent for over a decade; 12 are confirmed.

Conclusion: Facet overcomes the limitations of type-based indirect-call resolution under LLVM's opaque pointer model by reconstructing dispatch relations through field-identity recovery and principled edge refinement, significantly improving both precision and recall of call graphs and proving effective for finding deep, long-dormant bugs in real-world codebases.

Abstract: Resolving indirect calls is central to call-graph construction for C. Scalable type-based analyses such as MLTA use type information in LLVM IR to associate indirect calls with functions assigned to the corresponding structure fields. However, a single pointee type often misrepresents the memory a pointer addresses, and LLVM 17 removed pointee types in favor of opaque pointers. Therefore, field-sensitive analyses lose their matching key. Recovering the erased types restores the matching key but still misses the relation that the type encoded: which functions the program assigns to the field. We present Facet, to our knowledge the first analysis that reconstructs this dispatch relation over opaque IR. Facet identifies the structure field from which an indirect call loads its function pointer. It separately recovers the functions assigned to that field through initializers, stores, and aggregate copies. It then joins the two by field identity, without requiring an end-to-end value-flow path. Facet classifies proposed call-graph changes under distinct evidence rules for edge addition and removal and records the assumption behind each refinement. An LLM decides only the residual cases among symbolically bounded candidates. One analysis yields both a recall-preserving call graph and a refined call graph. On 14 C programs, Facet reduces the mean target-set size from 25.9 to 5.2 and raises observed recall from 0.79 to 0.99. Its recovered field identities agree with typed IR at 98.1% of jointly resolved sites. Applied to bug detection, the refined call graph found 17 deep bugs in C software from nginx to the Linux kernel, three of them latent for over a decade; 12 are confirmed.

</details>


### [17] [Beyond the Prompt: Linking What Developers Ask, Do, and Understand with Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33712)
*Yunhan Qiao,Summit Haque,Christopher Hundhausen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出Say, Do, Understand工作流，对开发者与AI代理的交互进行全流程分析，基于10名开发者使用GitHub Copilot的观察研究，发现四类人格并揭示性能与理解的不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的提示、屏幕活动和任务成功指标只能部分反映人机交互，缺乏对开发者书面输入、实际操作和事后解释的综合评估。

Method: 工作流包括Capture、Prepare、Analyze、Integrate、Interpret五个阶段，使用提示代码本、屏幕活动编码方案以及解释和解决方案评分标准，在10名经验丰富的开发者使用GitHub Copilot于陌生代码库的观察中实施。

Result: 将任务表现与理解交叉得到四类人格；八名开发者两项指标一致，两名出现分歧——一名通过测试但难以解释，另一名解释良好但测试失败；主要模式为频繁要求复查的开发者用时最少自行测试，且结果仅具描述性，未能普遍推广。

Conclusion: 该工作流可供教育者、行业实践者和研究人员用于适应和评估软件工程中的人机交互，为未来研究提供结构化框架。

Abstract: Coding agents can now change code for developers, who describe goals, supply context, and respond to the agent's work. Yet prompts, screen activity, and task success each tell only part of this story. We present Say, Do, Understand, an end-to-end workflow for analyzing what developers write to an agent, what they do while it works, and what they can explain afterwards. The workflow has five stages (Capture, Prepare, Analyze, Integrate, and Interpret) and three instruments: a prompt codebook, a scheme for coding screen-recorded activities and events, and separate rubrics for explaining the process and the solution. We applied it in an observational study of ten experienced developers who used GitHub Copilot on an unfamiliar codebase. Crossing task performance with understanding produced four personas. The two measures agreed for eight developers but split for two: one passed most tests but could not explain the solution, and another passed few tests but explained it well. In this sample, the personas that most often asked the agent to check its work spent the least time testing on their own. These patterns are descriptive and do not generalize beyond the sample. We recommend the workflow to computing educators, industry practitioners, and researchers to adapt and evaluate human--AI communication in software engineering.

</details>


### [18] [RAISE: Reinforcing Access Control Policy Synthesis in LLMs via Symbolic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33796)
*Yingming Zhou,Adarsh Vatsa,William Eiers*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了RAISE框架和CedarInstruct数据集，用于训练可形式化验证的Cedar策略合成。通过两阶段训练（SFT+RL），特别是RAISE-OC变体，在语义成功率上超越零样本GPT-6 Astra和Claude Opus。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言访问控制要求将权限、约束和例外进行仔细推理，但前沿LLM往往生成违反授权语义的策略。现有方法缺乏对形式化验证的支持，难以确保合规性。

Method: 构建CedarInstruct数据集（5800个场景，44个领域，1408个来自单一合成组织），包含已验证目标策略和执行计划。引入RAISE框架：第一阶段使用形式化验证的监督微调（SFT），第二阶段使用强化学习（RL）利用验证器信号。实验比较了不同RL实例化，其中RAISE-OC仅有一种能显著改进SFT结果。

Result: SFT在模型已掌握授权逻辑时效果良好；后续RL如何利用验证器信息比输入量更重要。只有RAISE-OC在SFT基础上产生显著提升。RAISE-OC在 held-out 场景中相较于零样本GPT-6 Astra和Claude Opus 分别提高了13.33% 和 16.26 个百分点的语义成功率，并可迁移到独立构建的CedarBench。

Conclusion: RAISE-OC证明了结合形式化验证与强化学习的双阶段训练方法在政策合成中有效，能显著提升语义正确性，展现出超越现有基线的潜力。

Abstract: Translating natural-language access-control requirements into policies requires careful reasoning about permissions, constraints, and exceptions, and even frontier LLMs often produce policies that violate the intended authorization semantics. We construct CedarInstruct, to our knowledge the first dataset that supports both training and semantic evaluation for formally verifiable Cedar policy synthesis. It contains 5,800 scenarios across 44 domains and 1,408 representing a single synthetic organization, each with a verified target policy and an executable verification plan. On this data we introduce RAISE, which trains policy synthesizers from formal verification in two stages, verified supervised fine-tuning (SFT) followed by a reinforcement learning (RL) stage that learns from verifier signal. We find that SFT succeeds largely by letting models express authorization logic they already have, since untrained models rarely write valid Cedar but often reason correctly when they do. After SFT, how the verifier's information is used matters more than how much of it is used. Of six RL instantiations that consume progressively richer verifier signal, only RAISE-OC improves meaningfully on SFT; it turns failed checks and symbolic counterexamples into guided exploration and learns from the result with off-context GRPO. With about 5.4K verified scenarios and LoRA fine-tuning, RAISE-OC trains Qwen3.5-9B to surpass zero-shot GPT-6 Astra and Claude Opus 5 by 13.33 and 16.26 percentage points in semantic success on held-out scenarios, and training transfers to the independently constructed CedarBench.

</details>


### [19] [Identical Runs, Different Results: Benchmarking AI Coding Agents on Open-Weight Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33812)
*Eduardo Ariño de la Rubia,Szilard Pafka*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究发现，同一编码智能体在相同任务上的重复运行会产生显著分数波动；单次或少数运行难以可靠比较不同智能体或模型，应将智能体与模型作为“配对”在多次运行中评估，并同步报告质量与合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体在基准测试中的重复运行分数不稳定，而团队在实际使用智能体完成自身任务时，需要判断这种波动意味着什么：是智能体能力差异不可靠，还是需要更多重复、更大模型，还是要结合合规结果做决策。

Method: 研究在一个机器学习任务上进行密集复制：让编码智能体改进用于预测航班延误的XGBoost分类器训练代码，并使用智能体从未见过的留出集评分。共进行584次运行，比较6个智能体与6个开源模型端点；固定设置下运行6种智能体-模型配对各52次，并对其中3种配对使用同族更大模型重复测试。同时分析数据规则违反、模型在后续年份航班上的泛化表现以及按官方价格计算的运行成本。

Result: 同一配对的重复运行差异大于不同配对之间的差异，因此用少数运行排名智能体并不可靠，区分观察到的智能体差异需要每个配对数十至上百次运行。更大模型整体得分更高，但提升小于一次运行的标准差，且其中一种智能体带来的提升超过其他智能体两倍以上。少于1/20的运行违反任务数据规则，但这些违规运行往往得分最高；先剔除违规运行，再从少数合规结果中保留最优结果，能可靠改善最终交付模型。将交付模型用于更晚年份的航班数据时，相比初始代码仅保留约三分之一的提升。成本方面，在相同模型下两个智能体之间的官方价格成本差异超过20倍，主要来自提示缓存。

Conclusion: 编码智能体与基础模型不应只按“单一模型”或“少量运行”比较，而应作为智能体-模型配对，在重复尝试中评估，并同时报告质量分数和合规表现。该工作还提供数据、代码和所有交付程序，强调可复现性、重复测试、合规筛选与成本分析的重要性。

Abstract: Repeated runs of the same coding agent are known to give different benchmark scores. We ask what that variation means for a team running an agent on its own task, by intensive replication on one machine-learning task: an agent improves the training code of an XGBoost classifier for airline delays, and a holdout it never sees scores the result. Across 584 runs, we compare six agents on six open-weight model endpoints, run six agent-model pairings 52 times each under fixed settings, and repeat three of them on a larger model from the same family. Identical runs of one pairing varied more than the pairings differed from one another, so comparisons of a few runs ranked them unreliably; resolving the agent differences we observed would take tens to more than a hundred runs of each. Runs on the larger model scored clearly higher, but by less than one run-to-run standard deviation, and the gap was more than twice as large with one agent as with the others. Fewer than one run in twenty broke the task's data rules, but those runs held the highest scores. Rejecting those runs first and keeping the best compliant result among a few attempts reliably improved the delivered model, even though a few runs could not rank the agents. On flights from a later year, the delivered models kept only a third of their gain over the starting code. At list prices, cost differed more than twentyfold between two agents on the same model, mostly through the prompt cache. Agents and models should be evaluated as pairings, over repeated attempts, with compliance reported beside quality. Data, code and every delivered program: https://github.com/earino/identical-runs-different-results

</details>


### [20] [Counterfactual Rollout Replay: Forkable Environments as Free Process Rewards for Software Engineering Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33875)
*Yuanhao Li,Hongbo Wang,Xuhong Chen,Yiming Cao,Xunzhu Tang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CRR is a training-time method using forkable environments to compute counterfactual returns at selected decision points, improving SWE-bench pass@1 over outcome-only RL.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Outcome-only RL provides only terminal signals, lacking step-level guidance for software engineering agents. CRR addresses this by generating step-level return contrasts without human process labels or learned reward models.

Method: CRR selects decision points, restores states, samples alternative actions, rolls forward under the policy, and replaces advantages at selected steps with the difference between realized and counterfactual terminal returns. Uses a 14B policy with forkable executable environments.

Result: On SWE-bench Verified, CRR achieves 41.7% pass@1 versus 36.7% for outcome-only GRPO in equal-wall-clock comparison (5.0-point gain including fork overhead). Also improves on SWE-bench Live and SWE-rebench, and combines with process-reward and trajectory-search methods.

Conclusion: CRR provides cost-free step-level supervision via environment forks, yielding consistent pass@1 improvements, but is limited to environments with reliable, affordable state restoration and fails with stochastic continuations or expensive/imperfect replay.

Abstract: Outcome-only reinforcement learning gives software engineering (SWE) agents a terminal success signal but little direct guidance about intermediate decisions. We introduce Counterfactual Rollout Replay (CRR), a training-time procedure that uses forkable executable environments to obtain step-level return contrasts. CRR selects a small set of decision points, restores each state, samples an alternative action, and rolls the branch forward under the policy. It retains the realised training trajectory and replaces the advantage at selected steps with the difference between its terminal return and the sampled counterfactual return. The method needs no human process labels or learned process reward model; free refers to those supervision costs, not replay compute. With a 14B policy, CRR improves pass@1 on SWE-bench Verified, SWE-bench Live, and SWE-rebench, and combines with process-reward and trajectory-search methods. On SWE-bench Verified, an equal-wall-clock comparison on the same hardware yields 41.7% versus 36.7% for extended outcome-only GRPO, a 5.0-point gain with fork overhead included. These results apply to environments with affordable, reliable state restoration; stochastic continuations and expensive or imperfect replay remain limitations.

</details>


### [21] [Green AI: Cost of LLM-Based Code Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33918)
*Negar Alizadeh,Nishant Saurabh,Fernando Castor*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: This study examines the trade-off between accuracy and energy consumption in LLM-based code completion across different workloads and model configurations, finding that smaller/quantized models offer good energy efficiency with comparable accuracy.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Energy cost of inference in LLM-based code completion remains underexplored, particularly under large-context workloads and across programming languages. There is a need for energy-aware deployment strategies to enable sustainable AI-assisted software development.

Method: The study evaluates 25 open-weight LLMs on two workloads: repository-level next-line completion (RepoBench) and fill-in-the-middle (FIM) code completion (Python, Java, Rust on McEval). It analyzes the influence of input tokens, output tokens, model size, and their interactions on energy consumption using correlation analysis and cluster-robust linear regression.

Result: Energy consumption drivers vary by task structure: in RepoBench, input context size and its interaction with model scale are primary factors; in McEval, output generation and active parameter count have the strongest influence. Output generation is more energy-intensive per token than prompt processing. Smaller and heavily quantized models frequently achieve Pareto-optimal trade-offs, matching larger FP16 models' accuracy while using significantly less energy. Increasing model size or context length does not guarantee proportional quality gains, and quantization can substantially improve energy efficiency with minimal accuracy loss.

Conclusion: The findings demonstrate that energy-aware deployment strategies are crucial for sustainable AI-assisted software development. Using smaller, quantized models provides excellent accuracy-energy trade-offs, making them preferable choices for local deployment where energy efficiency matters.

Abstract: Code completion is one of the most widely used applications of large language models (LLMs) in software development. Open-weight LLMs are increasingly adopted for locally deployed code completion systems, partly due to privacy concerns. Despite advances in LLM accuracy, the energy cost of inference remains underexplored, particularly under large-context workloads and across programming languages. This study investigates the trade-off between accuracy and energy consumption in LLM-based code completion and how workload characteristics, context size, and model scale influence inference energy usage. We evaluate 25 open-weight LLMs on two workloads: repository-level next-line completion with varying context sizes on RepoBench, and fill-in-the-middle (FIM) code completion across Python, Java, and Rust on McEval. We analyze the influence of input tokens, output tokens, model size, and their interactions on energy consumption using correlation analysis and cluster-robust linear regression. Our findings show that the dominant drivers of energy consumption depend strongly on task structure. In RepoBench, energy consumption is primarily influenced by input context size and its interaction with model scale, whereas in McEval, output generation and its interaction with active parameter count dominate. Output generation is more energy-intensive per token than prompt processing. Across both benchmarks, smaller and heavily quantized models frequently achieve Pareto-optimal trade-offs, often providing accuracy comparable to larger FP16 models while consuming substantially less energy. Increasing model size or context length does not necessarily lead to proportionally better completion quality, while quantization can substantially improve energy efficiency with limited accuracy degradation. These findings support more energy-aware deployment strategies for sustainable AI-assisted software development.

</details>


### [22] [JET: Judge-Guided Evolution at Test Time for Agent Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34126)
*Yao Long Teng,Jiayi Cai,Bo An*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本论文提出 Judge‑Guided Evolution at Test Time (JET)，一种在源任务上进化可执行判���器并将其转移用于指导目标任务程序进化的方法。JET 能够在没有目标评估器访问权或模型权重更新的情况下提供评分和诊断反馈，而无需这些操作。在未见过 WebShop 任务上，JET 相较于固定评判标准指导取得了显著优势：冷启动时平均奖励高出约 13 % ， 预热启动时高出约 4 % ， 冷启动时精确成功的相对提升为 36 % 。PushT 的精确判别控制（其中判别器通过观察重建评分规则）显示，在没有判别器错误的前提下，程序搜索成为性能瓶颈。分析结果表明，进化后的代码中包含有用的奖励预测逻辑，并且更好的最终选择无法单独解释所取得的进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当需要在测试时进化代理程序（即如何使用工具、处理观察结果和应对失败）时，尽管这有助于适应性，但在缺乏真实奖励的情况下难以决定保留哪些变化。尽管执行跟踪提供了代理行为方面的证据，但解释这些证据需要一个在任务和候选程序不断变化时依然有效的判别器。

Method: JET 首先在源任务上训练一个可执行的判别器，该判别器使用标记化的源轨迹进行训练。然后冻结该判别器并将其转移到目标任务上，以指导目标任务程序进化。在目标进化期间，判别器评估候选程序并提供诊断反馈，而不会更新模型权重或访问目标评估器。通过这种方式，判别器可以解释地为程序选择提供指导，即使在任务转移的情况下也是如此。

Result: 在未见过 WebShop 任务上，JET 表现优于固定评判标准指导：冷启动时平均奖励高出约 13 % ， 预热启动时高出约 4 % ， 冷启动时精确成功的相对提升为 36 % 。PushT 的精确判别控制显示，在没有判别器错误的前提下，程序搜索成为性能瓶颈。

Conclusion: 可执行判别器的转移（JET）为在评估器保持不变的任务转移情况下进行程序适应提供了一种有希望的方法，能够实现实质性的性能提升，并证明了可解释、可转移的判别器在无需访问目标奖励的情况下指导程序进化的价值。

Abstract: An agent's executable program governs how it uses tools, processes observations, and responds to failures. Evolving this program at test time can help adaptation, but deciding which changes to retain is difficult when true rewards are unavailable. Execution traces provide evidence of agent behavior, yet interpreting that evidence requires a judge that remains useful as tasks and candidate programs change. We introduce Judge-Guided Evolution at Test Time (JET), which evolves an executable judge on labeled source trajectories, then freezes and transfers it to guide target-side program evolution. The judge supplies scores and diagnostic feedback without target evaluator access or model-weight updates. On unseen WebShop tasks, JET achieves approximately 13% higher mean reward than fixed-rubric guidance when evolution begins from an unevolved program (cold start) and 4% higher when it begins from one already optimized on source tasks (warm start), with a 36% relative improvement in cold-start exact success. An exact-judge control on PushT, where the judge reconstructs the scoring rule from observations, shows that without judge error, program search becomes the bottleneck. Analyses identify useful reward-prediction logic in the evolved code and show that better final selection alone cannot explain the gains. These results support executable judge transfer for program adaptation under evaluator-preserving task shifts.

</details>


### [23] [The Construction of an Empirical Dataset of Incomplete Software Changes from Open Source Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34317)
*Savira Ramadhanty,Profir-Petru Pârţachi,Yoshiya Ishida,Takashi Kobayashi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过构建基于真实开源软件的缺失变更数据集，重新评估LCExtractor协同变化规则提取方法，发现89.4%的遗漏变更涉及5个或更少的文件，并优化了排序标准和提交数量的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件开发中组件修改可能传播到整个系统，开发者常因疏忽遗漏相关变更（45.7%），现有方法多基于人工构造的不完整变更数据，与实际场景不符，需要更真实的数据来评估方法有效性。

Method: 从开源软件收集包含诱导bug及其修复信息的issue跟踪平台数据，通过挖掘这些不完整变更构建新数据集，然后在该数据集上重新评估LCExtractor方法，确定最优排序标准并分析使用提交数量的影响。

Result: 在新数据集上重新评估LCExtractor后，识别出最佳排序标准，并量化了所用提交数量对结果的影响。具体而言，89.4%的遗漏变更涉及5个或更少的文件。

Conclusion: 通过构建真实世界数据集，本研究验证了LCExtractor在实际场景中的有效性，并提供了改进建议以提高协同变化规则提取的准确性。

Abstract: During software development, a modification to a software component may propagate across the system, requiring precise identification and correct revision of all affected components. This is a complex task, and developers often (45.7%) miss related changes. To address this, several methods have been developed to extract co-change rules from files that are frequently changed together in the revision history. However, previous research evaluated the methods using artificially created incomplete changes, which may not be representative of real-world data. To solve this problem, we construct a dataset by mining incomplete changes from a collection of open-source software, using information about induced bugs and their respective fixes from an issue tracking platform. We also analyze the characteristics of incomplete changes using this constructed dataset and found that 89.4% of missed changes involved five or fewer files. Finally, we re-evaluate LCExtractor, an existing co-change rule extraction method, on our constructed dataset, and we identify the optimal sorting criterion and the impact of the number of used commits.

</details>


### [24] [The Last Mile Is the File: OfficeEditBench for Preservation-Aware Office Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34469)
*Zhiwen Wu,Chengxu Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 OfficeEditBench 基准，评估在编辑 Office 文档时仅执行必要更新并保护未授权内容的能力，发现虽然文件交付成功率高，但所有输出均未满足完整契约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编辑 Office 文件时需要同时传播必需更新和保留受保护状态，过少导致依赖不一致，过多则擅自修改用户未授权内容，亟需衡量这种“变更范围维护”能力的评估手段。

Method: 构建包含 170 个任务的 OfficeEditBench，涵盖电子表格、演示文稿和文档，任务契约明确要求的更新、受保护状态、原生结构及交互需求；在 WorkBuddy、Doubao 和 Codex 三系统的 510 个归档结果上进行评估，区分文件交付、目标完成和验证器定义的接受度。

Result: 硬件包有效交付率在 92%–100% 之间，但没有任何选定输出能完全满足任务契约；案例分析表明局部正确性不足：更新值可能丢失生成公式、修改规则可能无法触及相关结论、新截止日期可能遗漏保留的前置条件。

Conclusion: OfficeEditBench 通过将工件层面检查与文档的持续可维护性联系起来，提供了一个在完成必需更改的同时保留原有工作逻辑与范围的测试台，为改进变更范围维护算法奠定基础。

Abstract: A small Office edit creates two obligations: propagate every required update and leave protected state untouched. Updating too little leaves dependencies inconsistent; updating too much changes content the user did not authorize. We introduce OfficeEditBench, a 170-task benchmark for change-scoped maintenance of spreadsheets, presentations, and documents. Task contracts specify required updates, protected state, native structures, and applicable interaction requirements. Across 510 archived task-system outcomes from WorkBuddy, Doubao, and Codex, we distinguish file delivery, target completion, and verifier-defined acceptance. Hard package-valid delivery ranges from 92% to 100%, yet no selected output satisfies the complete contract. Case analysis highlights why local correctness is insufficient: an updated value can lose its generating formula, a revised rule can fail to reach related conclusions, and a new deadline can omit a retained prerequisite. These mechanisms connect artifact-level checks to the continued maintainability of Office files. We analyze maintenance failures while distinguishing frozen automatic verdicts from human acceptability. OfficeEditBench provides a testbed for completing required changes while preserving the logic and scope of existing work.

</details>


### [25] [From Noisy Telemetry to Actionable Warnings: GPU Failure Prediction in Industrial Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34473)
*Yongqian Sun,Run Zhu,Wenwei Gu,Mengyao Li,Shenglin Zhang,Guanjin Wang,Yang Zhang,Xin Wu,Linlin Han,Feng Wang,Xiaozhou Liu,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究者提出了Falcon框架，通过结合缺失感知的时间特征、 peer-relative 特征和故障特定学习器选择，实现了GPU故障预测的显著提升，在测试集上达到最高F1分数（70.6%），且生产部署验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU集群是AI服务的关键基础设施，但在生产环境中准确且可操作的GPU故障预测仍然是一个挑战。研究发现了三个主要障碍：负载混淆的指标、异质故障前兆以及窗口级别预测与可操作警报之间的差距。

Method: Falcon框架结合了缺失感知的时间特征、peer-relative特征、故障特定学习器选择，以及基于阈值、持续性和冷却机制的事件策略。它通过将缺失数据处理、利用同一类别其他节点的信息来捕获故障前兆，并针对不同故障类型选择最合适的学习器进行建模。

Result: Falcon在四个基线中取得最高F1分数，在最佳故障类型上达到70.6%的F1分数。检测到的案例具有中位领先时间为17.34-35.57小时。在生产部署中，Falcon经过校准以提高精度，反映了假阳性成本。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，针对故障的建模能够从嘈杂的生产GPU指标中获得更早的预警，证明了Falcon框架在实际应用中的有效性。

Abstract: GPU clusters are critical infrastructure for AI services, but accurate and actionable GPU failure prediction remains a problem in production settings. We study ticket-linked telemetry from a ByteDance GPU cluster and identify three obstacles: workload-confounded telemetry, heterogeneous fault precursors, and the gap between window-level predictions and actionable alerts. These findings motivate Falcon, a fault-specific warning framework combining missingness-aware temporal and peer-relative features, fault-specific learner selection, and an event policy based on thresholding, persistence, and cooldown. On the test set, Falcon achieves the highest F1 among four baselines and reaches 70.6% F1 on the best-performing fault type. Detected cases provide median lead times of 17.34-35.57 hours. We further report a production deployment, where Falcon is calibrated toward high-precision alerts to reflect false-positive costs. Together, these results show that fault-specific modeling improves early warning from noisy production GPU telemetry.

</details>


### [26] [CLAD: Constrained Abstract Domain for Neural Network Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34628)
*Hai Duong,Thanh Le,ThanhVu Nguyen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了约束拉格朗日抽象域（CLAD），一种能够处理由多个凸约束定义的输入区域的神经网络验证方法，通过拉格朗日松弛和投影原始-对偶算法求解紧上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抽象域仅支持简单的Lp‑norm球，实际约束往往是Lp球与其他凸条件的组合，导致过估严重，验证失败或产生伪反例。

Method: CLAD将每个凸约束引入拉格朗日乘子，将原始的最大最小问题转化为可通过投影梯度法交替更新输入变量和乘子求解的形式，支持任意具有子梯度的凸约束（可通过自动求导获得）。

Result: 在四个卷积网络上的1,944个实验中，CLAD在标准无约束L∞性能上与GCPCROWN相当；在L2‑球约束性能上验证多60%的实例，总体验证多22%。

Conclusion: 通过将凸约束纳入拉格朗日框架，CLAD显著提升了抽象的紧度和验证效率，能够处理更现实的复杂输入区域。

Abstract: Neural network verification (NNV) formally verifies that a network satisfies a specified property for all inputs within a defined region. Modern NNV tools employ abstract domains to compute a sound over-approximation of the network's behavior from the given input region, thus the tightness of these abstractions essentially determines efficiency. A long line of increasingly precise domains has been developed, but they all describe the valid input region in the same restrictive way, e.g., an Lp-norm ball. A practical input region is rarely a simple Lp ball, but rather a combination Lp ball with additional constraints. Verifying a network over such a region with existing abstraction produces a loose over-approximation, which results in either failing to verify a property or spurious counterexamples. We introduce Constrained Lagrangian Abstract Domain (CLAD), a new abstract domain that computes a sound over-approximation of neural networks over input regions defined by a combination of convex constraints. CLAD propagates these constraints and tightens bounds over the true feasible region. However, bounding a neuron over the intersection of these constraints has no closed-form solution, so CLAD relaxes each constraint into the objective with a Lagrange multiplier and solves the resulting max-min problem with a projected primal-dual method, alternating a projected gradient step on the input with a multiplier update. CLAD supports any convex constraint with a subgradient, e.g., from automatic differentiation. We evaluate CLAD on 1,944 instances across four convolutional networks with motion-blur structured perturbations with halfspace or L2-ball constraints. On standard unconstrained Linf property, CLAD verifies as many instances as GCPCROWN at a similar runtime. On constrained properties, CLAD verifies 60\% more instances than GCPCROWN on L2-ball properties, and 22% more in total.

</details>


### [27] [Codoku: Renewable Program-Reasoning Challenges for Frontier Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34661)
*Cong Li,Hao Sun,Zenan Li,Zhendong Su*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了Codoku（代码苏密）作为一个可再生的程序推理基准，要求求解器填充部分程序中的类型化单元以满足全局静态和动态约束，如控制流图和执行路径。通过语义重构生成新谜题，确保每个谜题都有可证明可解性的证据。评估五个前沿模型在300个谜题上的表现，发现小谜题挑战开源模型，而专有模型仅解决大部分大谜题之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的程序推理基准存在两个关键假设：首先，假设代理可以通过执行程序来恢复答案；其次，固定任务集容易受到污染影响且难以更新。这些限制使得传统方法无法有效应对快速进步的编程代理。

Method: Codoku将问题转化为填充部分程序中类型化单元的任务，以满足全局静态约束（如控制流图）和动态约束（如执行路径）。通过语义重构从头合成谜题，每个谜题包含一个可证明可解性的证据。评估时使用五个前沿模型在固定工具预算下处理300个谜题，通过编码代理进行测试。

Result: 小规模谜题已经挑战了开源模型，而专有模型仅能解决大部分大规模谜题之一。这表明Codoku提供了一个可持续的程序推理测试平台，可以跟上编程代理的快速发展。

Conclusion: Codoku成功构建了一个可再生的程序推理基准，克服了现有基准的局限性，为评估和改进编程代理提供了新的标准。

Abstract: Existing program-reasoning benchmarks ask large language models to predict a program's behavior on a given input. Coding agents break two assumptions on which these benchmarks rest: an agent can recover the answer by executing the program instead of reasoning about it, and fixed task sets drawn from existing programs are increasingly exposed to contamination, yet costly to renew. We introduce Codoku (code sudoku), a renewable benchmark in which a solver fills typed cells in a partial program to satisfy global static and dynamic constraints, such as a prescribed control-flow graph and execution path. Because a partial program cannot be executed and valid fillings are sparse in an exponentially large space of interdependent choices, neither tool use nor enumeration can substitute for program reasoning. Puzzles are synthesized from scratch via semantic reification, so fresh puzzles of controllable complexity can be generated on demand, each with a witness that guarantees solvability. We evaluate five frontier models on 300 puzzles through a coding agent free to use any tool within a fixed budget. Small puzzles already challenge open-weight models, whereas even proprietary models solve only about half of the large ones. Codoku thus offers a renewable testbed for program reasoning that can keep pace with rapidly improving coding agents. GitHub: https://github.com/connglli/Codoku.

</details>


### [28] [When Ambiguity Meets Atypicality: Dual-Perspective Test Input Prioritization for DNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34703)
*Haoran Li,Shihai Wang,Bin Liu,Jialuo Chen,Wenjing Zhu,Yu Liu,Tengfei Shi,Shudi Guo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了DuFP（Dual perspective Feature space Prioritization）方法，结合决策模糊性和分布偏离度两个视角的KNN密度估计来对深度神经网络进行测试输入优先级排序，显著提升了故障诱导输入的识别效率并优于现有SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络（DNNs）在实际应用中虽然取得了显著进展，但其固有的脆弱性引发了对可靠性和安全性的关注。DNN测试已成为确保软件可靠性的重要实践，而测试输入优先级排序对于早期故障检测和降低标注成本至关重要。然而，准确识别导致故障的输入仍然具有挑战性。传统上，决策模糊性和分布偏离度是两种主要的特征，但单独使用任一视角都会引入盲点。

Method: 本文提出了DuFP（Dual perspective Feature space Prioritization），一种基于类条件密度估计的KNN密度驱动的测试输入优先级排序方法。该框架通过构建混合不确定性分数将两者整合：首先利用类条件密度估计结果计算预测正确性，分别定义决策模糊性（反映决策不确定性）和分布偏离度（量化数据分布异常程度）。然后，通过融合这两个分数构建混合不确定性分数来指导最终的优先级排序。

Result: 实验在图像和文本数据集上评估了清洁、腐坏和对抗场景下的优先级排序和选择任务。结果表明，DuFP有效且高效地优先排序了导致故障的输入，并优于现有状态为最先进的方法。

Conclusion: DuFP方法通过联合考虑两类视角，为深度神经网络提供了一种鲁棒的测试输入优先级排序策略，有效解决了单一视角带来的局限性，展示出在各种场景下对故障诱导输入的高效识别能力。

Abstract: While Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) have achieved remarkable progress in cutting-edge domains, their inherent brittleness has become a growing concern. To ensure the reliability and safety of DNN-enabled software, DNN testing has emerged as an indispensable practice. Within this context, test input prioritization is essential for early fault detection and reducing labeling costs. However, it remains challenging to accurately identify failure-inducing inputs. Although decision ambiguity and distributional atypicality are two widely adopted perspectives for characterizing inter-class competition and intra-class typicality respectively, relying on either perspective in isolation inevitably introduces blind spots. In this paper, we propose DuFP (Dual perspective Feature space Prioritization), a KNN density-based test input prioritization approach for DNNs that jointly incorporates both inter-class and intra-class perspectives. The prioritization framework of DuFP is built upon class-conditional density estimation. Based on the estimation results, prediction correctness is characterized by an ambiguity score and an atypicality score, with the former reflecting decision ambiguity and the latter quantifying distributional atypicality. A hybrid uncertainty score is then constructed by integrating both scores to guide the final prioritization. We evaluate DuFP on prioritization and selection tasks across image and text datasets under clean, corrupted, and adversarial scenarios. Experimental results demonstrate that DuFP effectively and efficiently prioritizes fault-inducing inputs and outperforms state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [29] [Multi-SWT-Bench: A Multilingual Benchmark for Reproduction Test Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34752)
*Kazuki Kusama,Sota Nakashima,Haruka Tokumasu,Masanari Kondo,Lingming Zhang,Yasutaka Kamei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 介绍了一个多语言再现测试生成基准 MULTI-SWT-BENCH，包含 8 种编程语言共 1963 个实例，并用多种方法评估了 LLMs 在跨语言上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准按单一编程语言构建，无法实现跨多种编程生态的统一评估；同时缺乏多语言维度上的系统性研究。

Method: 构建 MULTI-SWT-BENCH 基准，涵盖 Python、Java、TypeScript、JavaScript、Go、Rust、C、C++ 等 8 种语言；使用 MSWE-agent、MOpenHands、Codex、Claude Code 等四种方法进行实验并分析结果。

Result: 所有方法和模型在 Python 上的成功率高于整体平均成功率；C++ 表现最差；问题在于语言特定的测试惯例和跨语言隐式设置推断等难点。

Conclusion: 强调多语言评估的重要性，并为开发通用再现测试生成方法提供了可行方向，以更好地跨软件生态系统捕获问题特定行为。

Abstract: Reproduction test generation translates a natural-language issue description into executable tests that fail on the original code and pass after the issue is resolved, providing executable evidence for verifying candidate patches. Existing benchmarks are constructed for individual programming languages, preventing a unified evaluation across diverse programming ecosystems. To address this limitation, we introduce MULTI-SWT-BENCH, a multilingual benchmark for reproduction test generation consisting of 1,963 instances across eight programming languages: Python, Java, TypeScript, JavaScript, Go, Rust, C, and C++. Using this benchmark, we conduct an empirical study of state-of-the-art LLMs with four representative methods (MSWE-agent, MOpenHands, Codex, and Claude Code) and perform a failure analysis across programming languages. Our evaluation reveals a systematic language gap. Across every evaluated method and LLM, the success rate on Python exceeds the aggregate success rate across all languages, while C++ exhibits particularly low success rates. Our failure analysis identifies both language-specific challenges arising from repository testing conventions and cross-language challenges in inferring implicit setup requirements and preserving the target behavior through iterative revisions. These findings demonstrate the importance of multilingual evaluation and provide actionable directions for developing reproduction test generation methods that generalize across software ecosystems and reliably capture issue-specific behavior.

</details>


### [30] [TraceLib: System-Call Bitmap Feedback Mechanism for Language-Agnostic Web Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34778)
*I Putu Arya Dharmaadi,Elias Athanasopoulos,Fatih Turkmen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出TraceLib，一种基于系统调用的语言无关Web模糊测试反馈机制，通过转换系统调用为AFL-like位图来替代应用级别的语言特定插桩，实现跨语言Web应用的覆盖引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的覆盖反馈依赖特定语言和运行时的应用级别插桩，难以维护且成本高，特别是在多语言和多运行时的现代Web应用场景下，需要一种语言无关的系统调用层面的反馈机制。

Method: TraceLib观察系统调用转换，结合有限参数哈希丰富关键转换，并生成65536位置的AFL-like位图，从而使任何Web fuzzer无需查询应用代码即可判断请求是否增加新的位图位置。将其集成到WebFuzz中并与包括Native gray-box、black-box及Xiao et al.的N-gram比较方法进行对比评估。

Result: 在16个Web应用(8个PHP及Node.js、Ruby、Java、Go、Python等8个其他语言)中评估。TraceLib projected方法在全部8个PHP应用上优于black-box，在Joomla、Drupal、PrestaShop、Bagisto等4个应用上优于Native。服务器端额外延迟约1毫秒。

Conclusion: 紧凑的系统调用反馈作为运行时无关的覆盖指导代理是有效的，TraceLib实现了语言无关的Web模糊测试反馈，兼具低性能开销与良好的覆盖发现能力。

Abstract: Coverage feedback is an important source of guidance for fuzzing. However, obtaining such feedback normally requires application-level instrumentation that is specific to the language and runtime of the application. Given that modern web applications span multiple languages and runtimes, this application-level instrumentation is costly to implement and maintain. Therefore, we present TraceLib, a system-call feedback mechanism for enabling language-agnostic web fuzzing. Our proposed approach observes the transitions of system calls, enriches selected transitions with bounded argument hashes, and converts them into a 65,536-position AFL-like bitmap. By using the generated bitmap, any web fuzzer can decide whether to retain requests that add previously unseen bitmap positions without consulting application code coverage.
  We integrate TraceLib into WebFuzz and evaluate it under five WebFuzz feedback modes: the two proposed TraceLib variants (one over every traced system call and one projected onto monitored file paths and recognized SQL buffers), the N-gram comparator adapted from Xiao et al. representing the most recent work to our knowledge, WebFuzz's Native grey-box feedback, and black-box fuzzing without feedback. We evaluate TraceLib on sixteen web applications under test (WUTs): eight PHP applications and eight further applications spanning Node.js, Ruby, Java, Go, and Python to demonstrate platform portability. The results show that our proposed TraceLib projected exceeds black-box on all eight PHP WUTs while exceeding Native on four: Joomla, Drupal, PrestaShop, and Bagisto. In addition, measured on an identical replayed request workload, the tracer costs approximately one millisecond of server-side latency per request. These results indicate that compact system-call feedback is a useful runtime-independent proxy for coverage guidance.

</details>


### [31] [Certified Compilation in the TELEPERM XS Nuclear Safety I&C Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34871)
*Alexandre Berard,Richard B. Kreckel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Framatome 的 TELEPERM XS 数字 I&C 平台使用 CompCert 认证编译器进行形式化编译，但在某些常见生成代码模式中发现运行时性能不佳。通过形式化方法改进编译器，同时缩小其可信计算基础 (TCB)，从而在保持安全论证的同时进一步增强其安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提升安全仪表与控制系统的可靠性和性能，需要在保持形式化验证强度的同时优化编译器生成的目标代码。
