Method: 采用形式化方法分析 CompCert 编译器产生的目标代码，识别子优秀的重复代码模式，并在编译器内部进行形式化验证的改进，以减少 TCB 并提升运行时效率。

Result: 改进后的编译器消除了目标代码中的性能瓶颈，同时其可信计算基础进一步缩小，安全论证得到了加强而不仅仅是维持。

Conclusion: 形式化方法不仅能够保证编译器的正确性，还能在不增加可信基础的前提下优化生成代码，从而提升核电厂安全 I&C 系统的整体安全性和性能。

Abstract: The large safety instrumentation & control (I&C) systems in civil nuclear power plants (NPPs) are mainly safe-shutdown systems (reactor protection) or limitation and control systems. Framatome's established TELEPERM XS (TXS Core) product family is a digital I&C system platform to cover all these applications. We illustrate the role of verification in the different stages of the software production toolchain, focus on the formal compilation process, and discuss the contribution of the CompCert certified compiler to the safety case of the product. Scrutinizing the object code produced by this compiler has exhibited suboptimal run-time performance in a certain simple but recurring generated code pattern. We explain how formal methods allow us to address this issue in the compiler while simultaneously reducing its trusted computing base (TCB), thereby strengthening the safety case rather than merely preserving it.

</details>


### [32] [Verifying Graceful Degradation in a Distributed Malware-Detection System with SPIN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34873)
*Andrei Aldea,Dumitru-Bogdan Prelipcean*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文在Promela中建模了Bitdefender的端点恶意软件检测管道的故障降级链，用LTL形式化六条安全/活性属性，并使用SPIN模型检查器验证其在并发扫描场景下的正确性，确保永不产生误报、始终达成唯一判决且无死锁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端点检测系统在服务器故障时需通过降级机制保持安全，然而此类故障处理逻辑鲜有形式化验证，可能导致误报或阻塞。本研究旨在通过模型检查提供强硬性正确性保证，填补这一空白。

Method: 采用Promela构建抽象的端点决策管道模型，包含首要分析服务器超时后回退到旧版协议服务器、进一步回退到本地减签扫描的完整降级链。基于假设检测签名的健全性，以线性时序逻辑(LTL)形式化六条安全与活性属性（无误报、单一判决、有序降级、终止等），利用SPIN模型检查器进行穷尽状态空间探究，并分析随并发扫描数和端点数的状态空间增长。

Result: 所有六条LTL属性在模型上均非空真成立：降级机制永不会将良性文件判为恶意（无误报）；即便超时响应迟到，系统也能恰好产生一个判决；检测强度仅按照预定顺序弱化；系统始终能够终止并执行决策，无死锁。状态空间随并发扫描呈指数增长，但在这些参数范围内仍可在可接受时间内完成验证。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了形式化建模与模型检查在生产安全系统故障处理逻辑中的应用价值，能够提供对降级链行为的严格保证，为类似的分布式 endpoint 安全架构提供可复用的验证方法论。

Abstract: Modern endpoint malware detection is distributed: a lightweight agent on each endpoint collects features from a scanned file or process, sends them to a remote server for analysis, and then enforces the returned verdict locally by blocking, quarantining, or disinfecting. Because the endpoint acts on the verdict, the distributed machinery surrounding detection must never turn a transient server failure into a wrong action. We present a formal model, in Promela, of the endpoint decision pipeline of such a system, abstracted from a production architecture at Bitdefender. The model captures the system's graceful-degradation fallback chain: when the primary analysis server times out, the endpoint falls back to an older legacy-protocol server, and failing that to a reduced-signature local scan, before enforcing a verdict. Assuming detection signatures are sound, we specify six safety and liveness properties in linear temporal logic (LTL) and verify them exhaustively with the SPIN model checker. We prove that the fallback machinery never causes a false positive (an enforcement action against a benign file), commits to exactly one verdict per scan even when timed-out responses arrive late, weakens detection strength only in an explicit and ordered way, and always terminates in an enforcement decision, so the pipeline is deadlock-free. Each property is checked to hold non-vacuously, and we report how the state space grows with concurrent scans and endpoints. The work shows how model checking can give strong correctness guarantees for the failure-handling logic of a production security system, a layer that has received little direct formal attention.

</details>


### [33] [SmartMemory: Detecting On-chain-off-chain Communication Inconsistency for Smart Contract via Memory-based Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34983)
*Zeqin Liao,Yuhong Nan,Henglong Liang,Zixu Gao,Lianyu Hu,Yuqiang Sun,Zhijie Zhong,Xiaoyu Ma,Zibin Zheng,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文将跨链/链下通信中的资产表现不一致性定义为OFCI（OFC Inconsistency），作为OFC应用漏洞的根本原因，提出了首个基于记忆代理的SmartMemory框架，通过统一业务语义表示和记忆知识迁移，实现OFCI的自动检测，并在48个DApps的数据集上获得80.68%精确率和87.65%召回率，以及36个 previously unknown OFCI的发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对OFC应用漏洞的分析碎片化，未能提供统一视角，导致已知模式外的漏洞易被忽略。亟需从根本原因出发，统一识别OFC中的业务逻辑缺陷。

Method: SmartMemory首先将异构的OFC合约映射为统一的业务语义表示，定位业务逻辑；随后利用记忆代理提取漏洞知识为检测模式和规则，实现知识迁移；最后结合taint analysis验证候选OFCI的可达性、类型和影响。

Result: 在由48个DApps、81个OFCI组成的真实世界数据集上，SmartMemory实现80.68%精确率和87.65%召回率。进一步对325个真实OFC应用的分析中，检测到36个 previously unknown OFCI，全部经确认并由相应方修复。

Conclusion: 本文提出的OFCI概念及SmartMemory框架，填补了OFC安全研究的空白，展示了通过业务语义统一和记忆知识迁移实现漏洞检测的可行性，为跨链/链下通信安全提供了新方法。

Abstract: Smart contracts underpin decentralized finance, where growing demand for on-chain/off-chain communication(OFC) has driven diverse applications such as cross-chain bridges, real-world asset tokenization, and fiat-backed stablecoins. TheOFC-related security incidents in these applications are increasingly frequent, but prior studies address separate vulnerability categories within OFC applications rather than providing a unified view, causing vulnerabilities outside known patterns to be missed.In this paper, we identify OFC inconsistency (OFCI) as a root cause of OFC vulnerabilities, which arises from business-logic flaw and ultimately breaks the equivalence between the on-chain and off-chain asset representations to induce inconsistency.Automatically detecting OFCIs faces two challenges including (1)locating heterogeneous business logic, and (2) transferring existing vulnerability knowledge to identify unseen OFCI instances.
  To this end, we propose SmartMemory, the first framework to leverage a memory-based agent for OFCI detection. To address heterogeneity, SmartMemory maps diverse implementations ofOFC contracts into a canonical business-semantic representation to locate the business logic for OFCI inspection. For knowledge reuse, SmartMemory integrates a memory-based agent to distill vulnerability knowledge from features into patterns and detection rules, enabling knowledge transfer across cases to identify unseenOFCIs. Lastly, SmartMemory performs taint analysis to verify the reachability, type, and impact of each candidate OFCI. We construct the first real-world OFCI dataset comprising 48 DApps with 81 OFCIs for evaluation, on which SmartMemory achieves80.68% precision and 87.65% recall. In addition, through an analysis of 325 real-world OFC applications, SmartMemory detects 36 previously unknown OFCIs, all of which have been confirmed and fixed by corresponding parties.

</details>


### [34] [Making the Invisible Visible: A Framework for Reflective AI Use in Software Engineering Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34997)
*Ali Shakiba,Thomas Chaffey*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 AI Journal 框架，通过执行跟踪和认知审计使首年软件工程教育中学生与生成式 AI 的交互过程可视化，从而评估学生的评价判断和负责任的 AI 辅助实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教师仅能看到最终成果，难以了解学生在学习过程中如何评估、验证和改进 AI 生成的输出，导致难以考察评价判断力和负责任的 AI 使用。

Method: 设计 AI Journal 框架，结合执行跟踪（记录提示、输出、意图、交互上下文）与认知审计（捕捉验证策略、干预决策、置信度判断、关键学习时刻、反思），在首学期软件工程课程中部署。

Result: 初步观察显示学生在验证实践、干预策略和对 AI 辅助工作的感知上存在差异；关键学习时刻常发生在评估语境适用性、可行性和需求一致性时，而非仅识别明显错误。

Conclusion: AI Journal 是一种实用、轻量且模型无关的方法，能使 AI 辅助学习过程可见，将评估重心从产品导向转向评价判断和负责任的 AI 辅助实践。

Abstract: Generative AI (GenAI) is increasingly embedded in software engineering education, supporting activities such as requirements development, design exploration, documentation, and prototyping. However, educators often have visibility only into final artefacts, with limited insight into how students evaluate, verify, and refine AI-generated outputs during the learning process. This creates challenges for assessing evaluative judgement and responsible AI-assisted practice. This paper introduces the AI Journal, a structured reflection framework designed to make student-GenAI interaction visible in first-year software engineering education. The framework combines execution tracking, which records prompts, outputs, intent, and interaction context, with cognitive auditing, which captures verification strategies, intervention decisions, confidence judgements, critical learning moments, and reflections on AI-supported work. Deployed in a first-semester software engineering course, the AI Journal enabled visibility into aspects of student learning not observable through artefact-based assessments alone. Preliminary observations suggested variation in verification practices, intervention strategies, and perceptions of AI-supported work. Critical learning moments frequently occurred when students evaluated contextual suitability, feasibility, and requirements alignment rather than identifying obvious errors. The AI Journal demonstrates a practical, lightweight, and model-agnostic approach for making AI-assisted learning processes visible. By foregrounding verification, intervention, and reflection, it shifts attention from product-focused assessment toward evaluative judgement and responsible AI-assisted practice.

</details>


### [35] [Graph-Based Learning for Multi-Horizon Martian Atmospheric Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35042)
*Gary Myler,James Holmes,Manish Patel,Amel Bennaceur*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了MaGMA框架，利用图结构数据工程方法将OpenMARS重分析场转换为结构化学习对象，用于火星大气预报，在常规年和全球尘暴年中均取得良好性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 火星天气预报对未来探索至关重要，但火星大气行为涉及空间、时间、垂直和尘埃驱动过程，挑战了当前建模和预报方法，需要更有效的预报框架。

Method: 开发了MaGMA（火星图基于多时程大气预报）框架，将局部大气斑块表示为图节点，通过空间邻域、时间连续性、长时间依赖和动态相似的大气状态进行链接。模型整合近期大气历史、工程物理描述符和垂直大气信息，以支持多时程预报。

Result: 在五个未见过的火星年份中评估，包括常规年和全球尘暴年。在常规年，整体R²值约为0.73-0.85；对于尘埃柱预测，超越了经典和深度时间基线在大多数年-时程比较中。全球尘暴年中，尘埃柱预测在较短时程仍保持强劲表现，前两个时程的R²超过0.8，而更广泛的多变量性能有所下降。

Conclusion: 图结构数据工程可以创建可重复且诊断有用的表示，对于行星大气预报具有重要意义，同时也突显了在极端罕见情境下需要更好学习，以及改进垂直大气结构利用的重要性。

Abstract: Martian weather forecasting is important for future exploration, but atmospheric behaviour on Mars combines spatial, temporal, vertical, and dust-driven processes in ways that challenge current modelling and forecasting approaches. This paper introduces MaGMA (Martian Graph-based Multi-horizon Atmospheric Forecasting), a graph-based data engineering framework that transforms OpenMARS reanalysis fields into structured learning objects for Martian atmospheric forecasting. Local atmospheric patches are represented as graph nodes and linked through spatial neighbourhoods, temporal continuity, longer temporal dependencies, and dynamically similar atmospheric states. The model integrates recent atmospheric history, engineered physical descriptors, and vertical atmospheric information to support forecasting across multiple horizons. We evaluate MaGMA across five unseen Martian years, including regular years and a global dust storm year. In regular years, the model achieves overall R^2 values of approximately 0.73-0.85. For dust-column forecasting, it outperforms classical and deep temporal baselines in most year-horizon comparisons. During the global dust storm year, dust-column prediction remains strong at shorter horizons, with R^2 above 0.8 for the first two horizons, while broader multivariate performance declines. The results show that graph-based data engineering can create reusable and diagnostically useful representations for planetary atmospheric forecasting, while highlighting the need for better learning under rare extreme regimes and improved use of vertical atmospheric structure.

</details>


### [36] [Research-Native by Construction: Minimal Nodes, Re-verifiable Workflows, and Compounding Memory for Long-Horizon Scientific Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35182)
*Di Wang,Yu Liu,Bing Cui,Chaoqun Ji,Dongyuan Ni,Jingyu Lu,Kunlei Cui,Pu Qin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AfS (Agent for Science) is a platform for long-horizon scientific work that runs tens of hours across dozens of agent runs with minimal human involvement. It enforces research discipline through mechanically enforced laws rather than relying on model behavior, using mechanisms like unforgeable freezing, hash-chained artifact ledgers, and a two-tier knowledge base to prevent failures like fabrication and skipping.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Most existing agent-based scientific systems are general coding agents that fail by fabricating or skipping steps under pressure. This work aims to build credibility through structural enforcement rather than behavioral compliance.

Method: Research discipline is encoded as mechanically enforced laws across three time horizons: within-run research nodes, project-level inquiry contracts with hash-chained ledgers, and cross-project two-tier knowledge bases with rewriting-based promotion. Each mechanism is documented with its invariant, prevented failure, realization, and cost.

Result: The system description covers node contracts, write-path gates, two-tier memory, and runtime substrate. Three failure traces and two closed campaigns illustrate how the mechanisms catch real failures.

Conclusion: AfS shifts credibility from model compliance to unrepresentable non-compliant states. A process-integrity suite for quantitative comparison is under construction, but current measurements only cover operating costs.

Abstract: We describe AfS (Agent for Science), a platform built for long-horizon scientific work, where a project runs for tens of hours across dozens of agent runs with a human present only occasionally. Most agents for science are general coding agents with a skills folder attached, and they inherit that lineage's failure mode: under pressure to finish, they fabricate, skip, or smooth over. Our design rests on one claim: most of the credibility of machine-made research can be moved from asking the model to behave to making the non-compliant state unrepresentable. We encode research discipline as mechanically enforced laws (commitment before measurement; unforgeable freezing; reports are not facts; evidence persists but verdicts do not; negative results are first-class; mechanical questions to the framework and semantic judgment to the model), organized around three time horizons: a minimal set of research nodes within a run, an inquiry contract with frozen closure conditions and a hash-chained artifact ledger within a project, and a two-tier knowledge base with promotion by rewriting across projects. This is a system description written under one rule: each mechanism appears in exactly one place, with the invariant it enforces, the failure it prevents, the way it is realized, and the cost it imposes. It covers the node contract, the write-path gates, the two-tier memory, and the runtime substrate. Three traces walk real failure attempts through the mechanisms that catch them, and two closed campaigns are included as worked illustrations rather than as an evaluation. We report no benchmark: a process-integrity suite that would support quantitative comparison is under construction, and what we can measure today is only the operating cost of the machinery.

</details>


### [37] [Is there a future for models in the LLM era?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35281)
*Marco Calamo,Massimo Mecella,Monique Snoeck*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ARTHUR框架，结合模型驱动工程（MDE）与大语言模型（LLM）多智能体技术，重构传统MDE工具生成的遗留Java代码，实验表明MDE仍具重要价值，而非即将退役。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着软件开发深度依赖大语言模型，MDE是否仍有意义成为关键问题。MDE提供可靠性，LLM提供易用性，二者结合可互补各自短板，提升代码生成质量。

Method: 开发名为ARTHUR的LLM驱动多智能体框架，从UML图和文本规范重构遗留Java代码，引入Spring Boot等现代框架支持，并通过基于模型的测试验证代码符合初始规范。在自定义数据集上评估，采用时间、成本、测试通过率、compile@k、pass@k和pass^k等指标。

Result: 初步实验结果显示，ARTHUR能成功重构遗留代码，支持现代框架同时保持规范合规性；各项指标均优于直接从概念模型生成代码的方式，证明MDE结合LLM极具潜力。

Conclusion: MDE远未退役，结合LLM多智能体技术可显著增强其代码生成能力，ARTHUR框架展示了混合方法的可行性与优势。

Abstract: In an era where software development is deeply tied with Large Language Models, does Model Driven Engineering (MDE) still make sense? This raises the question of the extent to which MDE can be successfully combined with an LLM approach to address the downsides of each approach separately. In this paper, we try to answer that question by investigating a central Research Question: Can Agents powered by Large Language Models improve code generated from UML diagrams and text specifications by Model Driven Engineering (MDE) tools? To investigate this problem, we developed a novel LLM-powered Multi-Agentic approach called ARTHUR (Architecture Refactoring Through Hybrid UML Reasoning), a framework that aims at combining the reliability of MDE and the ease of use of LLMs. ARTHUR is designed to refactor legacy Java code produced by traditional, rule-based, MDE code generators from UML diagrams. To asses the answer to our question, we have refactored the legacy code of several projects from our custom dataset crafted for this purpose. \name{} which made it possible to add support for modern frameworks like Spring Boot, while ensuring compliance with Model-Based Testing techniques to verify that the code still corresponds to the initial model's specifications. We then measured the results obtained in terms of time and cost, passing test rate, and \texttt{compile@k}, \texttt{pass@k} and \texttt{pass$^k$} metrics. We also observed the effect of generating code directly from the conceptual model without the refactoring. Our preliminary test results show that MDE could not be more far from retirement, after all.

</details>


### [38] [Do Coding Agents Reuse Existing Code or Reinvent the Wheel?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35357)
*Dongsheng Ma,Sizhe Wang,Xinyi Huang,Zhengren Wang,Yuhan Wang,Luyang Si,Xincheng Wei,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: RepoReuse 是一个多轮基准测试，用于在真实仓库中审计代码复用情况。研究发现，编码代理在迭代开发中往往重复实现相同逻辑，导致冗余累积，但现有评估指标（如通过率）无法捕捉这种问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码代理正在被广泛应用于实时仓库的迭代开发，但现有的评估方法未能回答关键问题：编码代理是否会复用现有代码还是重新发明轮子？每一次重复实现都是冗余累积，而代理生成代码的速度远超人类审查能力，使得这种冗余在无监督情况下积累。需要超越功能正确性来评估代码生成质量。

Method: 提出了 RepoReuse 系统，一个基于 AST 的依赖图、引导证据收集和执行验证任务综合的自动化管道。该系统支持多轮交互，在每个回合揭示需求并累积工作空间，可扩展到新仓库，同时测量重用率、召回率和跨轮次结构冗余。

Result: 在 3000 轮审计中发现，编码代理逐渐停止探索相关仓库代码，即使完整代码已在工作空间中，它们也更少地利用自身历史；到第 5 轮，50.8% 的任务链仍包含重复逻辑；这些缺陷与通过率几乎无关，表明单纯的功能正确性评估不足以检测代码冗余问题。

Conclusion: 评估代码生成必须超越功能正确性，引入对代码复用和结构冗余的专门指标，以识别编码代理产生的隐性冗余问题，确保生成的代码不仅正确，而且高效且符合现有实现。

Abstract: Coding agents are increasingly deployed for iterative development on real repositories, yet existing evaluation barely answers a basic question: \emph{do coding agents reuse existing code or reinvent the wheel?} The question matters: every duplicated implementation is a fix applied twice and agents produce code far faster than humans can audit, so redundancy accumulates unsupervised. Thus, we present \textbf{RepoReuse}, a multi-turn benchmark for auditing code reuse in real repositories, where requirements are revealed turn by turn and the workspace accumulates across turns. It is built by a fully automated pipeline combining AST-based dependency graphs, guided evidence collection, and execution-verified task synthesis, and scales readily to new repositories. Beyond pass rates, we measure the reuse rate together with recall and cross-turn structural redundancy. An audit over 3{,}000 turns shows that agents progressively stop exploring relevant repository code, reuse their own history less even when it is fully in the workspace, and leave duplicated logic in 50.8\% of task chains by turn~5---all while pass rates barely move. Such deficiencies are invisible to pass rates, underscoring the need to evaluate code generation beyond functional correctness.

</details>


### [39] [MCP Error Messages Written for Developers Hurt the Most Capable Agents Most](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35381)
*Xiaonan Xu,Wenjing Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了Model Context Protocol (MCP)服务器的错误消息对智能体的影响。发现许多错误消息要求执行人类操作，但智能体只能调用工具，导致任务恢复率低。通过改进错误消息和提示工程，可以显著提高恢复率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP服务器包装了为人类开发者设计的Web API，其错误消息常指示运行命令或等待，但许多智能体只能调用工具，无法执行这些操作，从而引发问题。

Method: 分析了150个MCP服务器的3,001条错误消息，统计其中包含下一步操作指令的比例。在Berkeley Function Calling Leaderboard任务上测试了五个OpenAI模型，评估错误消息对任务恢复的影响。测试了两种改进方案：为MCP开发者提供命名工具的错误消息，以及为智能体开发者提供删除错误步骤的提示。

Result: 949条错误消息包含操作指令，其中一半依赖服务器无法感知的信息。在凭证过期时，终端命令步骤仅使45%的任务恢复；在速率限制时，等待指令仅使6%恢复。改进后，命名工具的错误消息将凭证过期恢复率提升至84%，速率限制恢复率提升至88%；删除错误步骤的提示将凭证过期恢复率提升至82%。

Conclusion: MCP服务器的错误消息需要针对智能体进行优化，例如明确指定要调用的工具。智能体开发者可以通过提示工程忽略不合理的错误步骤。这些改进能显著提高智能体在遇到错误时的恢复能力。

Abstract: Many Model Context Protocol (MCP) servers wrap web APIs built for human developers, and their error messages tell the reader to run a command, edit a configuration, open a web page or wait. Many agents that read them can only call the server's tools. In 150 widely used MCP servers, 949 of 3,001 error messages tell the caller what to do next, and half of these steps depend on something the server cannot see about the caller. On credential errors, 62 of 67 steps ask for a terminal command, a configuration change or a web page; on rate limits, 20 of 30 say to wait and retry without naming the call to repeat. We tested five OpenAI models that act only through the tools of Berkeley Function Calling Leaderboard tasks, and the agents did what the step said. On expired credentials, a terminal command in the step left 45% of tasks recovered, and the loss it caused grew from 18 points for GPT-5.5 to 69 for GPT-6 Astra. On a rate limit, GitHub's "Wait before retrying." left 6%. We tested two remedies. For MCP developers, naming a server tool in the step raised recovery on expired credentials to 84%, with the login tool in place of the command, and on a rate limit to 88%, with the call to repeat in place of the bare wait. For agent developers, deleting the step with a one-sentence prompt before the model reads it raised recovery on expired credentials to 82%.

</details>

### [40] [Be Careful Who You Trust: Coordination Dynamics under Corrupted Communication in LLM Multi-Agent Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31704)
*Xuanyi Liu,Niall Dalton,Hairi Amin,Xiyuan Yin,Lydia Lim*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本研究调查了大型语言模型在通信可能被篡改时协调的多代理 Stag Hunt 游戏中的表现。通过对多种任务和七种 LLM 进行程序化操作反转（模拟通信腐败），作者发现：(1) 随着腐败程度的增加，诚实的 Stag 选择会减少，但公开成功率的下降主要是由机制导致的；(2) 在严重腐败的情况下，诚实的选择与决策时可用的公开历史密切相关；(3) 预定义的阈值型报告基准与 LLM 的实际决策表现出相似的匹配率，表明 LLM 反映了这些规范。作者得出结论，在评估多代理系统的鲁棒性时，应同时考虑原始选择、公开操作和执行结果，因为通信腐败会严重、可预测地损害互利的合作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然大型语言模型越来越多地被用作自主代理，但它们在公开通信不可靠时保持协调性的能力仍然知之甚少。本研究旨在研究这种情况下的 LLM 协调性，特别是在 Stag Hunt 游戏中，该游戏突显了互利合作的风险收益权衡。

Method: 使用程序化操作反转对齐迭代 N 玩家 Stag Hunt 游戏，模拟对公开对话和所执行动作的篡改。实验中涵盖了不同的群体规模、协调阈值和腐败水平，并纳入了七种不同的 LLM。作者在这些因素组合上进行了系统实验，以观察代理的行为和协调模式。

Result: 三个主要发现：(1) 随着腐败程度的增加，诚实的 Stag 选择会减少，但公开成功率的下降主要是由机制导致的（例如，在 N=5, M=3 的设定中，即使在 80% 的腐败水平下，预反转的 Stag 选择仍保持在约 78%；而公开成功率则降至 12%）。;(2) 在严重腐败的情况下，诚实的选择与决策时可用的公开历史密切相关。;(3) 三个基于公开报告的阈值型基准与 LLM 之间的动作匹配率相似，表明 LLM 的决策在很大程度上描述了这些规范。

Conclusion: 在评估多代理系统的鲁棒性时，必须将原始决策、公开操作和实际执行结果分开。通信腐败会导致互利的合作严重且可预测地恶化，这表明需要设计更具韧性的多代理 LLM 系统，以应对不安全的公开通信。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as interacting agents, but it remains unclear how robust their coordination is when public communication is unreliable. We study this question in iterated $N$-player Stag Hunt games played by homogeneous LLM groups under controlled programmatic action inversion, which changes both the public transcript and the actions used for execution. Across an experimental grid spanning group sizes, coordination thresholds, corruption levels, and seven LLMs, we observe three main patterns. First, honest agents' pre-flip Stag choices decline as corruption increases, but the sharp fall in public success is primarily mechanical. In the focal $N=5,M=3$ setting, pre-flip success remains 78% at 80% corruption, while public success falls to 12%. Second, honest choices are associated with the public history available at decision time, particularly under high corruption. Third, three threshold-style public-report benchmarks yield similar action-match rates to the LLM agents, showing substantial descriptive agreement between LLM decisions and these benchmarks. Overall, our results show that original choices, public actions, and executed outcomes must be separated when evaluating multi-agent robustness, as corrupted communication can severely and predictably degrade mutually beneficial cooperation.

</details>


### [41] [On Evaluating and Improving Conversational Agents in Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32092)

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种评估和改进生产环境中大规模多智能体购物助手的框架，并总结了其使用经验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线评估面临三个障碍：无法重放日志、系统本身存在随机性以及聚合质量分数无法定位具体行为变化。

Method: 通过评估工具生成针对性断言和固定客户情景，利用用户模拟在本地重现行为；重复运行未修改系统建立基线；改进编排器将断言结果转化为假设，实现隔离修改并与基线进行配对百分位自举区间比较。

Result: 生产环境调查表明，断言分析可定位产品轮播位置失败，重复运行可区分真实改进与随机波动；评估审计发现评判器缺乏必要证据以及模型设置未生效。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了评估难题，提供了可操作的改进建议，并支持未来评估的修订，但仍需人工审批。

Abstract: We present a framework for evaluating and improving a large-scale, multi-agent shopping assistant in production, and report lessons from its use. Offline evaluation of such a system faces three obstacles. (i) A logged conversation cannot be replayed against a modified system, because a different response changes every turn that follows. (ii) The unchanged system itself varies from run to run. Its LLM components are stochastic, and in product search the available products, their prices, and the customer's personalization signals change. (iii) Aggregate quality scores combine distinct behaviors, so they show that quality has changed but not which behavior caused the change. Our framework addresses each obstacle in turn. For a reported behavior, an Evaluation Harness generates targeted assertions and a fixed cohort of customer scenarios. It then reproduces the behavior in a local instance of the assistant through grounded user simulation. Instead of replaying the log, the simulator writes new customer turns conditioned on the recorded messages and context. Repeated runs of the unchanged system form a stored baseline. An Improvement Orchestrator turns the assertion results into hypotheses, implements each as an isolated modification, and compares it with the baseline using paired percentile bootstrap intervals over scenario-level differences. When an investigation ends, the harness may propose revisions to future evaluations, subject to human approval and without altering past decisions. We report production investigations with this framework. Assertion profiles showed which positions of a product carousel a failure affected, and repeated runs distinguished a real improvement from run-to-run fluctuation. Audits of the evaluation itself found a judge that lacked the evidence it needed and a model setting that was configured but not applied.

</details>


### [42] [Robust Game-theoretic Motion Planning over Extended Time Horizons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32098)
*Bennet Outland,Vishala Arya*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为 WOLF 的新算法，用于解决具有长时标扰动的非凸博弈运动规划问题。该算法将模型预测控制与基于顺序凸化的开环差分博弈求解器相结合，通过动态地同时优化鲁棒性管（其厚度用于调整共享约束）来确保扰动下的可行性。论文验证了算法在两个具有耦合平移-姿态动态的轨道对抗博弈中的表现，包括隐蔽的共轨道干扰博弈和太阳阻挡博弈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动规划问题通常涉及非凸性和外界干扰，传统的鲁棒方法会在离线阶段固定不确定性描述，可能无法兼顾鲁棒性和性能。此外，在多个代理人之间的竞争性多代理运动规划中，求解博弈均衡通常 computationally 代价高昂。需要开发一种同时考虑鲁棒性和竞争性的高效算法。

Method: 将问题表示为一个部分耦合的广义纳什均衡问题，其中每个代理人的动态仅依赖于其自身的状态和控制，从而使其适合快速求解。WOLF 算法应用模型预测控制 (MPC) 和逐层凸化技术，得到开环差分博弈求解器。该算法将鲁棒性管视为一个动态状态变量，与轨迹同时优化，其厚度用于调整共享耦合约束。推导了足够的条件，确保在所有代理人的误差范围内调整约束的 nominal 轨迹对所有允许的扰动都可行。

Result: WOLF 算法成功解决了两个具有耦合平移-姿态动态的轨道对抗博弈：(1) 在活动检测概率限制下，执行隐蔽的共轨道干扰；(2) 通过减少太阳能来阻止逃逸者的太阳阻挡。该算法能够处理长期时间标，保持鲁棒性和性能，展示了其在真实环境下的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: WOLF 方法提供了一种新的框架，能够同时求解具有长时标扰动的非凸博弈运动规划问题，动态地调整鲁棒性约束，并提供理论上的可行性保证。其应用在轨道对抗博弈中验证了算法的有效性和鲁棒性，为未来具有对抗性和不确定性环境的运动规划和控制提供了有用的工具。

Abstract: This work presents a solution to nonconvex, game-theoretic motion planning problems subject to disturbances over long time horizons. The problem is posed as a partially-decoupled generalized Nash equilibrium problem, in which each agent's dynamics depend only on its own state and control, admitting fast solution methods for competitive multi-agent motion planning. An algorithm, WOLF, is developed that applies receding-horizon model predictive control to an open-loop differential games solver based on sequential convexification. In contrast to robust formulations that fix the uncertainty description offline, the robustness tube here is itself a dynamic state, co-optimized with the trajectory, and its thickness directly sets the tightening of the shared coupling constraints. A sufficient condition is derived under which a nominal trajectory satisfying constraints tightened against all agents' error bounds remains feasible for every admissible disturbance realization. The method is demonstrated on two adversarial on-orbit games with coupled translational-attitude dynamics: a stealthy co-orbital jamming game under an active detection-probability bound, and a sun-blocking game in which an adversary disables an evader by decreasing solar power

</details>

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分层多智能体框架 MUFASA，用于在金融领域进行符号回归，通过分离不同估值视角的智能体、元协调器和基于统计性能的记忆机制，在多国数据集上实现了最优表现并产生可解释的估值方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统符号回归在金融估值中受限，因为金融估值存在多种有效视角、非平稳市场和噪声连续反馈，因而需要一种能够处理这些特性的新方法。

Method: MUFASA 包含三个核心组件：(1) 代表不同估值视角的专门智能体进行 disentangled 方程发现；(2) 元协调器利用市场上下文信息进行层次化推理；(3) 基于准确率、稳定性和尾部风险等统计性能摘要的记忆机制，在噪声反馈中引导学习。

Result: 在多国金融数据集上的实验表明，MUFASA 在估值任务上优于经典金融方法、金融大语言模型以及传统符号回归方法，同时输出的方程具有高可解释性，并公开了演化迭代中的提炼知识和上下文依赖的策略权重。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了分层多智能体框架在处理金融估值这一复杂符号发现问题上的有效性，为未来的金融基本面分析研究提供了可复用的工具和见解。

Abstract: While symbolic regression (SR) has been successfully used in science to discover new equations, its use in financial valuation is hindered by several limitations. Whereas the natural sciences provide objectively correct relationships, financial valuation constitutes a distinct class of symbolic discovery problems, as it admits multiple valid perspectives, operates under non-stationary market conditions, and involves noisy, continuous performance signals. In this work, we propose Multi-Agent Fundamental Analysis with Symbolic Adaptive learning (MUFASA), a hierarchical multi-agent framework for symbolic discovery in finance. MUFASA introduces (1) disentangled equation discovery via specialized agents representing distinct valuation perspectives, (2) a meta-coordinator that performs hierarchical-level reasoning over market context information, and (3) a memory mechanism that reasons over statistical performance summaries (e.g., accuracy, stability, and tail risk) to guide learning under noisy feedback. Experiments across datasets from multiple countries show that MUFASA achieves state-of-the-art performance on the valuation task compared to classical finance methods, financial large language models, and SR approaches, while simultaneously producing interpretable equations, which we share with the community. We also make publicly available the distilled learnings across evolution iterations and context-dependent strategy weights, which might offer useful insights for future research on financial fundamental analysis.

</details>


### [44] [Adaptive and Resilient Dual-Layer Resource Slicing for Hovering Aerial Backhaul Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32798)
*Chuan-Chi Lai,Jen-Hsiang Li*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出RAPO-TD3框架，解决悬浮空中代理辅助回传网络中双层资源切片的自适应与弹性优化，针对eMBB、URLLC、mMTC异构业务，通过双重soft-max投影机制和弹性自适应优先级编排机制，保障URLLC时延，理论证明收敛性，仿真显示性能优于PPO、DDPG等，且在需求激增500%时仍接近理论最优，执行延迟亚毫秒级满足1ms预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决非平稳环境下HAA辅助回传网络中双层资源切片的复杂耦合问题，保障关键URLLC业务的低时延需求，同时适应异构5G/6G业务的动态流量变化。

Method: 提出基于双延迟深度确定性策略梯度的弹性自适应优先级编排增强框架（RAPO-TD3），引入双重soft-max投影机制将连续动作空间映射为物理可行的带宽分配，嵌入RAPO机制保护URLLC时延，并严格证明Lipschitz连续性和Robbins-Monro条件以保证渐近收敛稳定性。

Result: 仿真实验表明，RAPO-TD3在非平稳流量下优于PPO、DDPG和传统求解器；通过RAPO机制，URLLC满足度在500%需求激增时仍接近理论最优；可扩展性评估显示亚毫秒级执行延迟，严格满足1ms URLLC预算。

Conclusion: 该框架为异构5G/6G业务中的双层资源切片提供了自适应、弹性且理论收敛的解决方案，有效应对动态环境并保障关键业务性能。

Abstract: This paper investigates adaptive and resilient dual-layer resource slicing in hovering aerial agent (HAA)-assisted backhaul networks for heterogeneous 5G/6G services, including enhanced mobile broadband (eMBB), ultra-reliable and low-latency communications (URLLC), and massive machine-type communications (mMTC). To address the complex coupling of this dual-layer architecture in non-stationary environments, we propose the resilient adaptive priority orchestration enhanced twin delayed deep deterministic policy gradient (RAPO-TD3) framework. We introduce a novel double soft-max projection mechanism to map the continuous action space into physically feasible bandwidth distributions, ensuring strict constraint adherence. Additionally, a resilient adaptive priority orchestration (RAPO) mechanism is embedded to safeguard mission-critical URLLC latency. Crucially, we establish a rigorous mathematical foundation proving that our framework ensures Lipschitz continuity and satisfies the Robbins-Monro conditions for stable asymptotic convergence. Extensive simulations under non-stationary traffic demonstrate that our RAPO-TD3 framework achieves superior performance relative to PPO, DDPG, and traditional solvers. Notably, via the RAPO mechanism, our approach maintains URLLC satisfaction levels closely approaching theoretical optima even during 500% demand surges. Furthermore, scalability evaluations indicate that sub-millisecond execution latencies strictly satisfy the 1 ms URLLC budget, demonstrating the performance efficacy of our proposed framework.

</details>


### [45] [ORBIT: A Framework for Multi-Agent Safety and Security Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33102)
*Ben Hagag,William L. Anderson,Srija Chakraborty,Christian Schroeder de Witt*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: ORBIT框架提供统一的多智能体安全评估基准，揭示现有防御在跨威胁可转移性上的不足及安全性能权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估缺乏共享基础设施，导致防御难以比较和泛化，且单一威胁或单一智能体评估无法反映真实多智能体环境的风险。

Method: ORBIT基于UK AISI Inspect，可配置通信拓扑、记忆、调度和角色，支持四种威胁类型、四种防御策略及非对抗性失效，涵盖五类场景（浏览、计算、编码、客服、协作分配），实现可重复的实验基准。

Result: 实验发现，针对单一动作的防御在多问题代码任务上可降低攻击成功率约60分，但对协同攻击几乎无效，且没有防御在所有攻击上通用；安全性与性能存在权衡，架构选择显著影响防御效果。

Conclusion: ORBIT为多智能体安全研究提供统一基准，强调迫切需要开发跨威胁可转移的防御机制并考虑架构与防御的交互影响。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems are increasingly deployed for complex, long-horizon tasks or emerge as a natural consequence of agents interacting in the wild. Yet they give rise to significant safety and security risks: the flexible protocols that enable task generalization also expose novel threats, from cascading prompt injection to inter-agent collusion. Progress in defending against these threats has been slowed by a lack of shared empirical infrastructure, which forces bespoke environment development for every new defense and makes standardized comparison impossible. Existing evaluations address isolated threat models or single-agent settings, but none jointly vary attack, defense, and architecture across realistic multi-agent environments. To address this gap, we introduce ORBIT, a configurable evaluation framework for empirical multi-agent safety and security research, built on UK AISI's Inspect. ORBIT lets researchers configure communication topologies, memory, scheduling, and agent roles. It supports four threat types and four defense strategies, as well as non-adversarial failures, with a benchmark suite spanning five scenario families covering browser use, computer use, agentic coding, customer service, and cooperative allocation. Our central finding is a gap in defense transferability across threats: per-action defenses that cut a compromised agent's attack success by 60 points on multi-issue coding give no measurable protection against colluding agents, and none of the defenses we tested generalized over all attacks tested. We further demonstrate security-performance tradeoffs and interactions between architecture and defense effectiveness. We make ORBIT available open-source at https://github.com/wlanderson0/orbit.

</details>


### [46] [TRACE: Governing Memory Validity in Evolving Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33517)
*Wenjun Xiong,Shengtao Zhang,Shangding Gu,Bo Tang,Zhiyu Li,Feiyu Xiong,Ying Wen,Muning Wen*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为 TRACE 的无监督时间记忆准入机制，用于处理代理在共享状态发生变更后重新加入的问题。该方法通过在重返时进行资格判断（而非简单存储/检索）来协调离开检查点与更新之间的关系，并在仅当返回视图覆盖代理的待办任务时才释放有限的有效记忆状态。通过三种代理模型和三个基准测试，TRACE 在保留有效记忆和拒绝陈旧记忆方面表现出色，实现了 92.6-98.3% 的有效信息可用性和 98.4-99.5% 的无效信息拒绝率，显著优于重启和重置等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期协作中的语言模型代理使用持久性内存来保存状态，但当代理重新加入时，如何确定哪些记忆仍然可接受是一个未解决问题。即使记忆内容正确、相关且忠实于来源，但可能因基础数据已变更（如更换酒店）而不应被使用。因此，需要一种能够同时保留有效记忆并剔除过时记忆的方法。

Method: 提出 TRACE——一个训练-free 的时间记忆准入层，将重返视为资格决策过程。其工作原理包括：1) 与离开检查点进行协调；2) 识别并处理显式和隐式失效；3) 仅在返回视图完全覆盖代理的待办任务时才发布有限的返回视图。TRACE 被评估在三个代理模型（Memora、STALE Type II 和新近提出的 ManBench-Return）上进行，返回式测试，每个场景包括一个代理离开，四个队友更新共享状态，然后代理重返。

Result: 实验结果表明：TRACE 在 ManBench-Return 数据集上实现了 92.6-98.3% 的有效信息可用性和 98.4-99.5% 的无效信息拒绝率，与最佳基线的整体准确率相差仅 3.8 个百分点。在 STALE Type II 数据集上，TRACE 的整体性能比 Qwen、Gemini 和 DeepSeek 等强基线分别提升了 22.3、18.5 和 27.5 个百分点，且 Token 使用率仅为后者的约三分之一。此外，一种基于写时合并的策略虽然更准确，但 Token 使用率是 TRACE 的约 3.99 倍。

Conclusion: TRACE 提供了一种新的解决方案，有效解决了长期协作中代理重返时的记忆准入问题，实现了有效记忆的保留和陈旧记忆的有效剔除，在性能和效率方面均优于传统方法。该方法为实现更鲁棒、更具时效性的语言模型代理协作奠定了基础。

Abstract: Persistent memory lets language-model agents carry information across long-running collaborations, but leaves a lifecycle question open: what may a returning agent still act on once the shared state has changed? A memory can be correctly retrieved, relevant to the current task, and faithful to its source, and nonetheless be inadmissible for action: an itinerary saved before a pause still names the hotel the team has since replaced. We formalize this as temporal memory admission and present TRACE, a training-free layer that treats re-entry as an eligibility decision rather than a storage or retrieval operation, reconciling a departure checkpoint against absence-period updates, resolving explicit and implicit invalidation, and releasing a bounded Return View only when it covers the returning role's open obligations. We evaluate TRACE under three actor models on Memora, STALE Type II, and a derived ManBench-Return setting, each recast as return episodes: one agent departs, four teammates change the shared state, and the agent rejoins. What separates methods is not overall accuracy but whether one can retain valid memory and reject stale memory at once, and no single-policy baseline can: Restore (reinstate the departure checkpoint in full) admits stale state, Reset (start the return from an empty memory) discards valid state, each bottoming out at 0% on one of the two. TRACE is the only method high on both, reaching 92.6-98.3% valid-information availability with 98.4-99.5% invalid-information rejection on ManBench-Return, within 3.8 points of the best baseline's overall accuracy. On STALE Type II it improves Overall over the strongest comparison policy by 22.3 (Qwen), 18.5 (Gemini), and 27.5 (DeepSeek) points at roughly 2.3 times their tokens, while a write-time consolidation pipeline is more accurate still at 3.99 times TRACE's.

</details>


### [47] [Population Physics, Population Problems: Safety and Emergence in LLM Societies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33871)
*Adrian de Wynter*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: The authors propose a framework to quantify self-organisation in LLM societies, test it on three multi-agent simulations (Schelling grid, Moltbook, Rogue), and show robust self-organisation and phase‑transition‑like dynamics, especially in open‑ended systems. Even safety‑tuned agents can develop population‑level pathologies driven by coordinated subsets, while two other scenarios (GovSim, ChatEval) do not exhibit self-organisation. The framework offers a lightweight, agent‑agnostic way to detect coordinated collective behavior in deployed LLM systems.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing tools for studying single language models do not scale to multi‑agent LLM societies, yet recent incidents involving autonomous agentic systems make understanding collective behavior critical. A general, agent‑agnostic diagnostic is needed to detect coordinated collective actions in deployed multi‑agent LLM systems.

Method: Developed a measurement framework for self‑organisation in LLM social systems. Applied it to three systems: a Schelling grid, a social network (Moltbook), and a Twitter‑like misinformation simulation (Rogue). Included two control scenarios where self‑organisation should not appear: a commons dilemma (GovSim) and an LLM‑as‑judge deliberation scheme (ChatEval). Analyzed statistical signatures and relaxation dynamics under varying environmental information conditions.

Result: All three primary systems exhibited statistically significant self‑organisation; open‑ended systems (Moltbook, Rogue) showed sharp, phase‑transition‑like dynamics. Population‑level pathologies emerged even with safety‑tuned or monitored LLMs, driven mainly by coordinated activity of a subset of agents. In contrast, no self‑organisation was observed in the GovSim and ChatEval scenarios.

Conclusion: The proposed framework provides a lightweight, agent‑agnostic diagnostic layer for detecting coordinated collective behavior in deployed multi‑agent LLM systems, independent of natural language analysis or model versioning.

Abstract: The collective behaviour of large language model (LLM) societies is not the sum of their individual outputs. It yields statistically distinct, sometimes-unpredictable phenomena, for which the tools we use to study single agents may not scale. Due to recent incidents involving autonomous agentic systems, however, understanding these systems is paramount. For that we introduce a framework for measuring self-organisation in LLM social systems and apply it to three such systems: a Schelling grid, a social network (Moltbook), and a Twitter-like misinformation simulation ('Rogue'). All three exhibit statistically significant self-organisation. Moreover, their relaxation dynamics vary with the environmental information available to the agents, with open-ended systems (Moltbook, Rogue) exhibiting sharp, phase-transition-like dynamics. Further results show that population-level pathologies can emerge even when the LLMs are safety-tuned or monitored, being primarily driven by the coordinated activity of a population subset. We also show when self-organisation does \textit{not} emerge under two additional scenarios (a commons dilemma, GovSim, and a LLM-as-a-judge deliberation scheme, ChatEval). We argue that measuring signatures of this kind offers a lightweight, agent-agnostic diagnostic layer for detecting coordinated collective behaviour in deployed multi-agent systems without relying on natural language or model versioning.

</details>


### [48] [Prospective Interpretation Risk: Principled Communication Control Between LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33885)
*Wanrong Yang,Rehan Deen,Julian Ma,Yuheng Fan,Yaoyu Jin,Taher Jafferjee,Ziquan Liu,Dominik Wojtczak,Yalin Zheng,David Henry Mguni*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本研究提出了前瞻性解释风险（PIR）概念，量化接收器可能误解消息的概率，并通过价值解释信息（VoII）在部署时仅查询当预期收益超过成本时进行接收器信息查询，以减少解释失败率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多代理系统中，发送者与接收者之间的通信存在不确定性，尤其在异构系统中，不同能力的接收器可能从相同消息中重构出不同的任务。传统方法难以估计接收器如何解读消息，因此需要建立模型来评估接收器可能产生的解释风险。

Method: 研究者将问题建模为带有隐藏接收器类型的发送者-接收者问题，定义前瞻性解释风险（PIR）为接收器重构非预期任务的概率。采用黑盒探针关联消息、预期任务和接收器特定重构，使用离线训练时的异构冻结接收器提供监督数据，在部署时利用历史信息生成接收器类型后验分布指导消息修订和选择。引入价值解释信息（VoII），只在预期沟通收益超过成本时查询接收器信息。

Result: 实验显示：解释失败率因接收器而异，差异范围为4-13倍；接收器信息能将PIR校准误差降低68%；PIR引导的修订使解释失败率分别降低44%（相对于原始消息）和40%（相对于通用重写）；VoII在匹配成本下优于信息增益和随机查询，将解释失败率从3.84%降至3.79%，同时仅查询了18.2%的episode。

Conclusion: 该工作首次系统地量化了接收器解释风险，并通过VoII框架实现了高效的接收器信息查询策略，有效降低了LLM代理系统中的解释失败率，为构建更可靠的多代理系统提供了理论基础和实践指导。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agentic systems increasingly rely on models communicating with one another, yet existing uncertainty and multi-agent methods rarely estimate how a particular receiver will interpret a message before it is sent. This matters in heterogeneous systems, where capable receivers can reconstruct different tasks from the same message. We model this as a sender-receiver problem with a latent receiver type and define prospective interpretation risk (PIR): the probability that a receiver reconstructs a task other than intended. Rather than model an LLM's full input-output behaviour, we use black-box probes relating messages, intended tasks, and receiver-specific reconstructions, yielding scalable supervision while separating interpretation from downstream capability failure. Offline, heterogeneous frozen receivers provide supervision for receiver-conditioned risk and the effects of predefined mutable message features. At deployment, history induces a posterior over receiver types, guiding message revision and selection. We introduce value of interpretation information (VoII), querying for receiver information only when its expected communication benefit exceeds its cost. Our theory characterises when receiver information has decision value and bounds such queries. Empirically, interpretation-failure rates vary by 4-13x across receivers. Receiver information reduces PIR calibration error by 68% relative to a receiver-agnostic predictor, largely by correcting receiver-specific risk levels. PIR-guided revision reduces interpretation failure by 44% relative to the original message and 40% relative to a generic rewrite, mostly through a repair that helps every receiver. VoII outperforms information-gain and random querying at matched cost on the interpretation objective it optimises, lowering interpretation failure from 3.84% to 3.79% while querying 18.2% of episodes.

</details>


### [49] [MASTraceBench: Diagnosing Collaboration Gains through Proposal Trajectories in LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34496)
*Yapeng Li,Songze Li,Shuang Yu,Jing Yu,Zhixin Liu,Liqiang Wen,Tonghua Su*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了一套名为MAStraceBench的新基准，用于追踪多代理系统的提案轨迹并评估协作收益。实证表明最终答案很少超越最强的初始提案；弱提案被提升，而强提案往往停滞或退化。基于这一观察，提出了CLEARS方法，通过代理间声明级评估来代替整个提案交换，从而更好地保护最强初始提案并提升协作收益，在六个任务中胜出五个。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多代理系统基准主要关注最终结果，难以诊断协作收益的产生、保持或丢失机制。因此，亟需一个能够追踪提案轨迹并评估协作增益的多层基准来系统地比较MAS方法。

Method: 创建MAStraceBench基准，涵盖六个合作和竞争任务，追踪并评估提案轨迹，提供任务得分、协作增益、提案轨迹指标和令牌成本等多层指标；提出CLEARS方法，用声明级评估代替整个提案交换以引导可靠的合成。

Result: 使用MAStraceBench进行系统比较后发现：最终答案很少超过最强的初始提案；弱提案通常被提升，而强提案往往停滞或退化。CLEARS方法在五项任务上表现最佳，更有效地保护或提升最强初始提案，并获得了最高的协作增益。

Conclusion: MAStraceBench为评估多代理系统的协作收益提供了一个系统性框架。通过引入基于声明的协作（CLEARS），可以更可靠地保持和提升最强提案，从而提高MAS的整体协作表现。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) have shown promise in complex problem solving. As MAS methods diversify, systematic evaluation becomes increasingly challenging. However, existing benchmarks largely focus on final outcomes, leaving unclear how collaboration gains arise, are preserved, or are lost. To address this limitation, we introduce MASTraceBench, a benchmark for diagnosing collaboration gains through proposal trajectories in MAS. Across six cooperative and competitive tasks, MASTraceBench tracks and grades proposal trajectories and provides a multi-layer metric suite covering Task Score, Collaboration Gain, proposal-trajectory indicators, and Token Cost. Using MASTraceBench, we systematically compare representative MAS methods not only by final performance, but also by how agent proposals evolve and are aggregated into the final answer. This analysis reveals a recurring pattern: final MAS answers rarely surpass the strongest initial proposal; interaction often lifts initially weaker proposals toward it, while strong initial proposals are seldom further improved and may regress. To reduce this risk, we propose CLEARS, which replaces whole-proposal exchange with claim-level evaluation across agents to guide reliable synthesis. CLEARS more often preserves or improves upon the strongest initial proposal and achieves the highest Collaboration Gain on five of the six tasks.

</details>


### [50] [CEO Arena: Evaluating Long-Horizon Multi-Agent Decision-Making in Competitive Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34821)
*An Yan,Yu Huo,Zhiwei Shang,Yiran Peng,Chenglin Wu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为"首席执行官竞技场"的基准测试环境，用于评估在长期不确定性条件下，各企业首席执行官（CEO）如何协调商业决策并适应竞争对手的变化。通过匹配替代评估，该基准不仅衡量运营回报，还考量各CEO对竞争对手和市场的影响。实证研究表明，大多数基于大型语言模型（LLM）的CEO在500天的模拟市场中表现出负均回报，表明私利可能伴随市场损失。尽管如此，稳健性分析揭示了某些持续性的效应，记忆、行动和会计痕迹提示需求捕捉以及对竞争对手定价和支出反应的重要作用。首席执行官竞技场提供了一个可控测试平台，用于研究长期代理竞争、战略互动和市场外部性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在创建一个基准测试平台，用于评估代理在长期不确定竞争环境中的协调能力、适应能力以及对市场的影响。由于现有的研究缺乏一个可控环境来同时考察长期动态、战略互动和市场外部性，因此引入了首席执行官竞技场。该基准通过匹配替代评估技术将运营回报与对竞争对手和市场的影响相结合，从而揭示了微观代理决策如何在大规模市场中波及宏观结果。

Method: 研究建立了一个由八家企业组成的共享市场，每个企业有专属CEO代理，模拟500天的运营时间序列。每个CEO接收私有公司信息和带噪声的市场信号，根据资源限制和延迟反馈 sequentially 进行定价、采购、营销、研发和服务决策。 此外，与每家公司的参考策略（保持其他代理身份和分配不变）进行匹配替代评估，以分离代理自身的贡献。各LLM CEO代理在27个主评估运行和26个稳健性运行中进行了测试，分析了代理的回报、记忆痕迹、行动和会计数据，以推断需求捕获和竞争对手反应的机制。

Result: 主要评估结果表明，大多数LLM CEO在500天窗口内的平均回报为负值，表明许多策略在长期竞争中不可持续。 此外，某些代理的私人收益可能导致市场整体损失。稳健性分析显示，尽管大部分模式与基于规则的基准存在显著差异，但56个定向作用中的4个仍然具有相对稳定的影响。记忆、行动和会计痕迹揭示了需求捕获和对竞争对手定价及支出反应的重要作用，从而解释了观察到的市场动态。

Conclusion: 首席执行官竞技场为研究长期代理竞争、战略互动和市场外部性提供了一个可控且可解释的基准。通过揭示LLM驱动的CEO在长期动态中的表现及其对市场的影响，本文不仅展示了当前代理在复杂商业环境中的局限性，还为提高长期竞争适应性、改善代理设计和决策机制提供了指导。

Abstract: Long-horizon competition tests agents' ability to coordinate business decisions under uncertainty and adapt to changing rival strategies. We introduce CEO Arena, a benchmark that uses matched replacement evaluation to assess operating returns alongside an agent's effects on rivals and the market. Each CEO agent is compared with a reference policy in the same company under the same economic seed, holding other agents' identities and assignments fixed while all agents adapt. In a shared eight-company market spanning 500 simulated days, CEOs make sequential decisions on pricing, procurement, marketing, research and development, and service using private company information and noisy market signals, under resource constraints and delayed feedback. We evaluate eight LLM-based CEO agents in 27 main runs and 26 robustness runs. In the main evaluation, most agents have negative mean returns, and private gains can accompany market losses. Robustness analyses suggest that aggregate patterns extend beyond the original rule-based baseline; four of the 56 directed pairs show relatively stable effects. Memory, action, and accounting traces suggest demand capture and rivals' pricing and spending responses as possible explanations. CEO Arena provides a controlled testbed for studying long-horizon agent competition, strategic interaction, and market externalities.

</details>


### [51] [Positional choice and robust collective behavior in fish schools: biohybrid experiments and modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35554)
*Vahagn Grigoryan,Donato Romano,Cesare Stefanini,Giulia De Masi*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了一种包含探索项的新模型，能稳健复现鱼群实验并解释社会凝聚力与个体探索行为的共存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典鱼群模型虽能再现群体模式，但假设的个体规则是否真实尚不清楚，且对参数高度敏感。

Method: 先分析活鱼与机器鱼互动实验，测量位置偏好；再用代理模拟实验，对比经典模型；最后提出含探索项的新模型并泛化至多鱼。

Result: 经典模型需极精确参数才能匹配实验，微小扰动即失效；新模型对参数误差鲁棒，成功复现实验，且泛化后能再现鱼群行为及探索模式。

Conclusion: 社会凝聚力与个体探索行为共存，真实的集体行为模型应同时包含社会互动与探索成分。

Abstract: Collective behavior of fish schools is usually modeled on the assumption that each individual follows specific rules of motion that depend on its position and velocity relative to its neighbors. Although these models reproduce many schooling patterns observed in nature, it remains unclear whether the assumed rules are realistic at the individual level. To address this question, we first analyzed a set of experiments in which a live fish interacted with four moving robotic fish in a tank, and measured the time it spent in each position relative to the robots. We then simulated this experiment, replacing the fish with an agent, and assessed the extent to which classical models agree with the experimental observations. An extensive exploration of the parameter space showed that these models closely matched the experimental observations, with a very specific choice of parameters. However, they were highly sensitive to the parameter values: a minor perturbation caused them to fail completely. To resolve this, we propose a new model that incorporates an additional exploratory term characterizing the agent's positional preference at every moment. This model proved robust to small errors in the parameters while successfully replicating the experiments. Furthermore, when generalised to multiple fish, the model reproduced schooling behaviour and common schooling patterns, while also exhibiting exploratory behaviour at both the individual and the school level. This shows that social cohesion coexists with individual exploratory behaviour, and that realistic models of collective behaviour should account for both social interactions and this exploratory component.

</details>

### [52] [Bandwidth, Not FLOPS: FFT Kernels, Matrix Units and SAR Imaging on Apple M6](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32237)
*Mohamed Amine Bergach*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 数据迁移（而非算术）是GPU FFT速度的主要限制因素。经典的"最大适合芯片的块"规则仍然能预测何时速度会崩溃：当变换超过GPU的32 KiB本地内存时，MPSGraph和MLX速度会损失一半以上。保持这些变换在寄存器中可以实现2.2×（全精度）到4.4×的加速。GPU的矩阵单元无帮助，而CPU的矩阵单元则表现突出：我们的内核速度可达vDSP的5.3×。整个4096x4096合成孔径雷达图像的GPU处理时间为8.1 ms（半精度中间结果为6.0 ms），比CPU参考实现快14‑19×。GPU和CPU共享内存带宽，因此并行使用它们没有任何优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究苹果M6芯片上FFT的实际性能瓶颈，验证经典的块大小规则，并比较GPU（MPSGraph、MLX）和CPU（vDSP）实现的FFT性能。

Method: 开发了自定义的GPU和CPU内核，与苹果的MPSGraph、MLX和vDSP进行了对比实验，测量了不同批量大小、精度和内存使用模式下的吞吐量，特别关注数据移动和算术密集型操作。

Result: GPU速度受内存带宽限制；大批量处理达到主存带宽极限。缓存基准的测量结果可能高出3.7×。使用寄存器的内核速度比MPSGraph快2.2×（全精度）或4.4×（半精度）。CPU矩阵单元的内核速度比vDSP快5.3×。整个4096x4096合成孔径雷达图像的GPU处理时间为8.1 ms（半精度为6.0 ms），比CPU参考实现快14‑19×。GPU和CPU矩阵单元的并行使用无法提高性能，因为它们共享相同的内存带宽。

Conclusion: FFT的性能主要取决于数据迁移，而不是算术密集型操作。经典的"最大适合芯片的块"规则仍能预测速度下降点。GPU的矩阵单元无益，而CPU的矩阵单元则能显著提升性能。通过保持变换在寄存器中和使用半精度，可以进一步提升GPU上的FFT性能。

Abstract: The fast Fourier transform (FFT) underlies radar, imaging and scientific computing. A classic rule for fast GPU FFTs is to compute the largest block that fits on chip and compose larger transforms from such blocks. We test this rule on Apple's M6 chip, whose GPU performs 29 arithmetic operations in the time it reads one byte from main memory, and which adds matrix units to both its GPU and CPU. Comparing our kernels with Apple's MPSGraph and vDSP libraries and MLX, we find that data movement, not arithmetic, sets FFT speed. Large batches run at or near the main-memory bandwidth limit in every GPU library, and benchmarks that keep data in cache overstate real throughput by up to $3.7\times$. The on-chip rule still predicts where speed collapses: MPSGraph and MLX lose half or more of their speed once a transform outgrows the GPU's 32\,KiB local memory. Keeping such transforms in registers avoids an extra trip through memory: $2.2\times$ faster than MPSGraph, and $4.4\times$ with half-precision storage. The GPU's matrix units do not help, because recasting the FFT as matrix products adds as much arithmetic as they save. The CPU's matrix unit, 47 times faster than its vector units, does: our kernel for it beats vDSP by up to $5.3\times$. End to end, a $4096\times4096$ synthetic aperture radar image takes 8.1\,ms on the GPU (6.0\,ms with half-precision intermediates), $14$--$19\times$ faster than a CPU reference. Running the CPU's matrix unit alongside the GPU gains nothing: both share one memory bandwidth.

</details>


### [53] [Tool Waiting and Re-arrival in Compile-Time-Static LLM Serving: Cost Mechanisms and Configuration Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34663)
*Dongkyeom Jang,In-Nea Wang,Junho Jeong*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 该文研究静态编译NPU LLM服务中，外部工具等待与请求再到达带来的执行成本，并提出通过模拟与KV复用损失诊断来选择编译时配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在agentic LLM服务中，会话调用外部工具后需等待再重新到达继续推理；静态编译NPU服务会在编译时固定batch bucket set、最大batch size和KV cache槽位，这些配置会影响等待与再到达时的实际执行成本。

Method: 在单一LLM实例上，基于实测工具等待时间分布构造合成负载；由于不同配置下再到达时间不同，构建按时间顺序回放请求处理的模拟器，在2077种候选配置中选择预测成本最低的配置，并在新输入上验证；进一步分析离散batch对齐、KV cache存活和prefill干扰三类机制。

Result: 并发为6且并发数不在bucket set中时，工具等待使padding比例从0.235降至0.120，但decode执行时间增加1.51倍，说明仅凭padding不能判断成本。在新输入、并发为8时，基线在24次再到达中复用KV 9次；仅扩大最大batch size可使执行成本降低8.25%，同时调整bucket set的所选配置降低9.72%。若18次再到达中已有17次复用KV，则收益仅0.59%。

Conclusion: 编译时配置不应只依据padding等单一指标选择，而应通过诊断KV复用损失及其引发的执行变化，为静态编译NPU LLM服务选择更优配置。

Abstract: In agentic LLM services, a session calls an external tool, waits for it, and re-arrives to continue inference. Statically compiled NPU serving can fix the batch bucket set, the maximum batch size, and the number of KV cache slots at compile time. We define such an environment as a compile-time-static serving substrate and analyze the execution-time cost that tool waiting and re-arrival incur in it. On a single LLM instance, we run synthetic workloads following a measured tool waiting time distribution and compare, on the same inputs, a baseline configuration with settings {1, 2, 4, 8}, 8, and 8 against configurations that change some of them. Because re-arrival times differ across configurations, we build a simulator that replays request processing in time order, select the candidate with the lowest predicted cost among 2,077 configurations, and validate it on new inputs. We identify three mechanisms: discrete batch alignment, KV cache survival, and prefill interference. At a concurrency of 6, absent from the bucket set, tool waiting lowered the padding ratio (0.235 to 0.120) yet increased decode execution time 1.51-fold, so padding alone did not indicate cost. On new inputs at a concurrency of 8, where the baseline reused KV in 9 of 24 re-arrivals, enlarging the maximum batch size alone cut execution cost by 8.25%, and the selected configuration, which also adjusted the bucket set, by 9.72%. Where 17 of 18 re-arrivals were already reused, the effect was 0.59%. Compile-time configurations should thus be selected by diagnosing KV reuse loss and the resulting change in execution.

</details>

### [54] [AirLog: Store-Level Indoor Life Logging Made Easy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31864)
*Zihui Yun,Jiaying Du,Yue Yu,Zhewei Liu,Zhen Xiang,Longfei Shangguan,Zhenlin An*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: AirLog 是一种基于智能手机的生活记录系统，通过利用商场内现有的 Wi-Fi SSID 语义信息和室内导览图像，无需专用定位基础设施，即可自动重构用户的店铺级访问轨迹，并利用大语言模型（LLM）生成人类可读的个人日记。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统室内定位系统需要繁琐的无线电地图构建和专用基础设施，难以应用于大规模个人生活记录。本研究旨在设计一种无需用户手动记录，即可自动重构购物商场内的店铺访问轨迹，并支持个人记忆回忆、活动反思和自动日记生成的轻量化系统。

Method: AirLog 首先将室内导览图像转换为空间地图，然后融合环境中的 Wi-Fi SSID 语义锚点与惯性推算（inertial dead reckoning）技术来恢复高精度的店铺级轨迹。最后，利用大型语言模型（LLM）将重构的轨迹自动总结为自然语言日记。

Result: 系统在商品智能手机上实现，并在公共数据集和自收集数据集上进行了评估。实验结果表明，AirLog 在店铺级区域恢复、语义匹配、轨迹重构和日记质量上均显著优于现有基线。人工评估显示，生成的日记连贯、准确且忠实于用户的真实访问记录。

Conclusion: AirLog 证明了利用商业空间中现有的语义线索（Wi-Fi 和导览图）进行无基础设施室内定位和自动化生活摘要的可行性。它为个人日记自动化生成和生活轨迹反思提供了一种高效、无感且准确的全新方案。

Abstract: This paper presents AirLog, a smartphone-based life journaling system that automatically reconstructs users' store visits in shopping malls and summarizes them into human-readable journals. Unlike conventional indoor localization systems, AirLog avoids labor-intensive radio-map construction and dedicated wireless localization infrastructure and algorithm calibrations. Instead, it repurposes two cues already available in commercial spaces: semantic information exposed by ambient Wi-Fi SSIDs and indoor directory images. AirLog converts directory images into spatial maps and fuses Wi-Fi semantic anchors with inertial dead reckoning to recover store-level trajectories, which are then summarized into journals by an LLM. Such store-level life logs can support applications such as personal memory recall, activity reflection, and automated diary generation without requiring users to manually record where they have been. We implement AirLog on commodity smartphones and evaluate it on both a large-scale public dataset and a self-collected dataset. The results demonstrate that AirLog substantially improves store-level region recovery, semantic matching, trajectory reconstruction, and journal quality over existing baselines. A human evaluation further shows that the generated journals are coherent and faithful to users' visits.

</details>


### [55] [Toward Agentic Optical Networks: A Vision of LLM Agent-Driven Autonomous Lifecycle Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32226)
*Yao Zhang,Shengnan Li,Yuchen Song,Yidi Wang,Yue Pang,Wenbin Chen,Xiaotian Jiang,Xiao Luo,Meixia Fu,Min Zhang,Yongli Zhao,Shanguo Huang,Alan Pak Tao Lau,Danshi Wang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于大语言模型Agent的Agentic Optical Networks（AONs）概念，并设计了分层多Agent框架以实现光网络全生命周期的自动化管理，从而推动从任务级半自动到生命周期级全自主的演进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着光网络规模、复杂度和服务多样性的增长，传统自动化手段难以保证敏捷性、效率和可靠性，亟需利用大语言模型Agent的逻辑推理、决策和多任务编排能力提升网络自动化水平。

Method: 首先回顾光网络自动化的发展历程并提炼Agent关键技术；随后提出一种层次化多Agent框架，覆盖规划、部署、运行、维护、升级、退役等全生命周期阶段；最后讨论该框架的实现路径及面临的挑战。

Result: 该分层多Agent框架在概念上能够实现高层次自治，协同各阶段任务，提升自动化覆盖面和一致性，为光网络向全生命周期自动化迈出关键一步。

Conclusion: 通过将LLM Agent与光网络的专门需求相结合，AONs有望实现从任务级半自动执行到生命周期级完全自主的转变，但仍需解决多任务协同、算力开销、数据依赖和可靠性等核心问题。

Abstract: As optical networks continue to expand in scale, complexity, and service diversity, the implementation of automation has become essential for ensuring agility, efficiency, and reliability in lifecycle management (LCM) of optical networks. Large language model (LLM) Agent, distinguished by its progressively sophisticated capabilities in logical reasoning, adaptive decision-making, complex problem solving, and multi-task orchestration, presents great opportunities to advance network automation beyond traditional AI techniques. Nevertheless, the application of LLM Agent in optical networks remains in its early exploratory stage, challenged by the lack of multi-task coordination, high computational demands, data dependence, and reliability concerns. In this paper, we envision a conceptual roadmap toward Agentic Optical Networks (AONs) by integrating LLM Agents throughout the LCM with high-level autonomy. First, we trace the evolution from manual operations to AI-empowered frameworks and distill key technologies in Agent, providing actionable insights into leveraging its strengths for addressing practical network automation challenges. A core contribution of this paper is the proposal of a hierarchical multi-Agent framework, which is specifically developed to manage every phase in LCM of AONs, including planning, deployment, operation, maintenance, upgrade, and decommission, thereby enabling more cohesive and comprehensive automation throughout the entire lifecycle. In addition, future directions and underlying challenges are also discussed at the intersection of LLM and optical networks. By aligning the LLM Agent with the specialized requirements of AONs, this work aims to explore the potential for the evolution of optical networks moving from task-level semi-automatic execution toward lifecycle-level full autonomy.

</details>


### [56] [Analyzing 10 Petabit/s Network Data with Accelerated Associative (Token) Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32978)
*Jeremy Kepner,Hayden Jananthan,LaToya Anderson,William Arcand,David Bestor,William Bergeron,Chansup Byun,Alex Bonn,Daniel Burrill,Vijay Gadepally,Michael Houle,Matthew Hubbell,Michael Jones,Piotr Luszczek,Peter Michaleas,Lauren Milechin,Julie Mullen,Andrew Prout,Albert Reuther,Antonio Rosa,Charles Yee,Alex Pentland*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种基于GPU加速的关联数组数学方法(D4M)用于隐私保护的网络分析，并通过MIT/IEEE/Amazon匿名网络感知图挑战赛进行了基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着网络规模和重要性的增长，隐私保护的网络分析变得至关重要。分析不同层次的网络需要处理多样化的数据，包括物理、逻辑或个人/代理端点。需要数学工具以支持复杂算法同时实现垂直、水平和时间维度的可扩展性能。

Method: 采用关联数组(token数组)数学方法及其对应的库，并通过GPU加速实现大规模网络分析。实现了基于Matlab的D4M GPU加速库原型，并在广泛的CPU和GPU硬件上对匿名网络感知挑战进行了基准测试。

Result: 在CPU核、CPU节点和GPU节点内部分别实现了可扩展性能。水平扩展跨越多个节点呈线性增长。在数百个GPU节点同时运行时，获得的持续处理速率足以potentially分析10 Petabit/s的网络。

Conclusion: 基于GPU加速的关联数组数学方法具有良好的可扩展性和性能，能够满足大规模网络分析的需求，特别是在隐私保护方面表现出潜力。

Abstract: As networks expand and become an ever more critical infrastructure to modern society the need to analyze these networks with the highest regard for privacy is essential to ensure their proper function. Depending on the level of the network layer to be analyzed, sources and destinations can be any combination of physical, logical, or persona/agentic endpoints, which requires the ability to handle diverse data. Invaluable to these analyses are mathematical tools that enable sophisticated mathematical algorithms to be expressed succinctly while achieving scalable vertical (within a compute node), horizontal (across compute nodes), and temporal (over different generations of hardware) performance. Associative (token) array mathematics and corresponding libraries is one approach that can meet these requirements. Accelerating these libraries with GPUs enables the analysis of the largest networks. The MIT/IEEE/Amazon Anonymized Network Sensing Graph Challenge provides a venue for highlighting the applicability of accelerated associative arrays for these types of problems. The D4M associative library has been implemented in a number of languages. This work benchmarks a prototype Matlab D4M GPU accelerated implementation of the Anonymized Network Sensing challenge across a wide range of CPU and GPU hardware. Scalable performance is demonstrated within and across CPU cores, CPU nodes, and GPU nodes. Horizontal scaling across multiple nodes was linear. Running on hundreds of GPU nodes simultaneously achieved a sustained processing rate sufficient to potentially analyze a 10 Petabit/s network.

</details>


### [57] [Type-Safe Decision Frameworks for Agentic 5G Control: A Theory-Driven Testbed Characterization of Where They Can Be Applied](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33689)
*Michail-Alexandros Kourtis,George Xilouris*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出理论驱动的类型安全决策框架用于5G网络代理控制，在Open5GS/UERANSIM测试床评估三种设计点，发现微调类型编码器泛化差，问题读取框架错误率低但存在延迟或模型规模依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决5G网络代理控制中决策必须来自声明选项集的类型安全需求，同时平衡时效性、类型一致性、认证成本和基数等约束。

Method: 提出理论框架将关键指标转化为可检查的适用性谓词，包括最优行动/升级/弃权门控、升级可行性下界、共置稳定条件、逐类型共形风险控制及类型不匹配界限；在Open5GS/UERANSIM测试床上评估三种设计：托管类型模型(Jev)、开放微调类型编码器(Laya)、零标签通用语言模型改造(AnyJev)。

Result: 类型安全消除了格式失败，但微调类型编码器对98%-99.5%的变更问题返回训练答案，校准门控在高达80%情况下错误行动；问题读取框架(Jev和AnyJev)在任何变更问题上的错误率分别≤0.143和≤0.137，但Jev托管且慢11-29倍，AnyJev依赖8B语言模型。

Conclusion: 类型安全决策框架可有效保证格式正确性，但微调编码器泛化能力弱；问题读取框架泛化更好但面临延迟或模型规模权衡，需根据具体5G决策类选择适用框架。

Abstract: This paper presents a theory-driven characterization of type-safe decision frameworks for the agentic control of 5G networks, where every decision must be an element of a declared option set rather than free text. Three design points are evaluated on an Open5GS/UERANSIM testbed with a closed core-policy loop, namely a hosted typed model (Jev), an open fine-tunable typed encoder (Laya), and a zero-label retrofit of a general language model (AnyJev). The proposed theoretical framework turns timeliness, type conformance, certification cost and cardinality into checkable applicability predicates, supported by an optimal act/escalate/abstain gate, an escalation-feasibility floor, a co-location stability condition, per-type conformal risk control with a certification label floor, and a type-mismatch bound. Measuring every predicate yields an applicability map from framework to 5G decision class. Type safety removes format failures but not the question: the fine-tuned typed encoder returned its training answer for 98-99.5% of changed questions, and its calibrated gate then acted wrongly on up to 80% of them, whereas the question-reading frameworks acted wrongly on at most 0.143 (Jev) and 0.137 (AnyJev) of any changed question, but were either hosted and 11-29 times slower (Jev) or reliant on an 8B language model (AnyJev).

</details>


### [58] [HOCCL: Offloading Collective Communication from GPU Cores to Accelerate Distributed Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34334)
*Yao Fei,Gongming Zhao,Hongli Xu,Jin Fang,Jiacheng Zhu,Shuo Xu,Kun Huang,Zhuolong Yu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: HOCCL is a zero-SM collective communication framework for LLM training that eliminates communication overhead from GPU SMs using a stream manager, P2P executor, and collective scheduler, achieving ~3% SOTA performance and reducing communication occupancy by ~10% while boosting training throughput by up to 5%.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Large language model training relies heavily on GPU Streaming Multiprocessors (SMs) for computation, but existing collective communication systems compete with computation by consuming SMs for communication data movement and synchronization operations, limiting overall training efficiency.

Method: HOCCL proposes a zero-SM collective communication framework comprising three components: a stream manager that preserves operator-level temporal ordering with other GPU kernels, a point-to-point (P2P) executor enabling zero-SM point-to-point communication, and a collective scheduler that orchestrates P2P transfers to maximize bandwidth utilization.

Result: Experiments demonstrate that HOCCL achieves within 3% of the state of the art on average, eliminates communication occupancy on nearly 10% of total GPU SMs, and improves end-to-end training throughput by up to 5%.

Conclusion: By freeing SM resources from communication tasks, HOCCL significantly enhances training efficiency through improved resource allocation between computation and communication, making it a promising direction for optimizing large language model training workloads.

Abstract: Large language model training involves massive computation on GPU streaming multiprocessors (SMs), the primary compute units of GPUs. Since SMs host specialized accelerators such as Tensor Cores, their efficient utilization is critical to training efficiency. Unfortunately, existing collective communication systems compete with computation for SMs, as they consume SMs for communication-related data movement and synchronization operations.
  We observe that communication can, in principle, be driven by DMA engines, thereby eliminating SM involvement in communication. Based on this insight, we propose HOCCL, a zero-SM collective communication framework consisting of three components: a stream manager, a point-to-point (P2P) executor, and a collective scheduler. The stream manager preserves operator-level temporal ordering with other GPU kernels. The P2P executor enables zero-SM point-to-point communication, while the collective scheduler orchestrates P2P transfers to maximize bandwidth. Experiments show that HOCCL preserves near-peak communication performance, achieving within 3% of the state of the art on average, while eliminating communication occupancy on nearly 10% of total GPU SMs. By freeing SM resources for computation, HOCCL improves end-to-end training throughput by up to 5%.

</details>


### [59] [CoRF: Cross-Scene RF Synthesis by Learning Propagation and Preserving Array Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34399)
*Kang Yang,Duaa Nakshbandi,Wan Du,Mani Srivastava*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: CoRF通过跨场景的空间谱合成实现了无需针对新场景重新训练的快速场景生成，利用共享传播模型和参考条件化技术，在35个模拟场景中取得了显著的PSNR提升（7.09 dB对已见变体，6.97 dB对全新场景）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的射频神经场需要为每个场景单独进行适配，这种方法在新场景部署时既测量密集又计算消耗高。研究旨在开发一种跨场景的空间谱合成方法，使得模型能够从稀疏目标场景测量中实例化未见过的场景，而无需针对每个场景进行特定训练。

Method: CoRF采用摊销的跨场景空间谱合成，通过共享学习传播的模型来跨场景传播，并在任意查询位置从稀疏目标场景测量中生成未见场景。该方法将学习到的场景依赖传播与已知接收阵观测模型分离，使用无序的谱仅参考条件化一个标准锚点场。分析阵物理学将这些组件映射到接收器协方差，再映射到空间谱。这种因式分解保持预训练传播模型冻结，实现在单次参考条件化后在任意查询位置进行合成。此外，还引入了必要的局部参考容量界限和误差分解来进一步刻画公式。

Result: 在35个模拟场景覆盖七个类别的测试中，CoRF相对于最强基线在已见场景变体上提高了7.09 dB PSNR，在完全未见场景类别上提高了6.97 dB PSNR。

Conclusion: CoRF通过共享传播模型和参考条件化技术实现了跨场景的快速、无需重新训练的场景合成，在多样化的场景类型中展现出优异的性能，证明了该方法在实际应用中的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Existing radio-frequency (RF) neural fields fit each scene separately, making new-scene deployment measurement- and optimization-intensive. This work studies amortized cross-scene spatial spectrum synthesis, where a shared model learns propagation across scenes and instantiates an unseen scene from sparse target-scene measurements without scene-specific training. To achieve this, CoRF separates learned scene-dependent propagation from the known receiver-array observation model. An unordered set of spectrum-only references conditions a canonical anchor field, producing arrival directions and query-dependent component powers for each query. Analytic array physics maps these components to the receiver covariance and then to the spatial spectrum. This factorization keeps the pretrained propagation model frozen, enabling synthesis at arbitrary query locations after a single reference-conditioning pass. A necessary local reference-capacity bound and an error decomposition further characterize the formulation. Across 35 simulated scenes spanning seven categories, CoRF outperforms the strongest baseline by 7.09 dB PSNR on unseen variants of represented scene categories and by 6.97 dB on entirely unseen scene categories.

</details>


### [60] [WiFi Backscatter for Green Internet of Things: Concepts, Research Trends, and Practical Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34448)
*Weiqi Wu,Shuo Wang,Zhaoyuan Xu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: WiFi backscatter shows promise for green IoT but faces practical deployment challenges related to limited operating range and packet loss in frequency-shifted backscatter, requiring further research to achieve widespread adoption.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: There is a significant gap between WiFi backscatter research prototypes and practical deployment in green IoT systems, necessitating a comprehensive understanding of current limitations and future development directions.

Method: The paper provides an overview of WiFi backscatter from practical and green IoT perspectives, covering fundamentals, key enabling techniques, IoT applications, recent research trends (higher throughput, concurrent communication, simplified deployment, commercial compatibility, joint communication and sensing), and identification of key challenges.

Result: The paper identifies two primary challenges hindering practical deployment: limited transmitter-to-tag operating range and packet loss in frequency-shifted backscatter. These issues represent critical barriers to scaling WiFi backscatter for real-world green IoT applications.

Conclusion: Addressing the limitations of operating range and packet loss in frequency-shifted backscatter is essential for enabling WiFi backscatter to become a viable and practical communication technology for future green IoT systems.

Abstract: WiFi backscatter has emerged as a promising technology for green Internet of Things (IoT) connectivity by enabling battery-free devices to communicate through widely available WiFi signals. Despite substantial progress in recent years, a considerable gap remains between research prototypes and practical deployment. This paper provides an overview of WiFi backscatter from the perspective of practical and green IoT systems. We first introduce the fundamentals and key enabling techniques, together with potential IoT applications. We then outline recent research trends toward higher throughput, concurrent communication, simplified deployment, commercial compatibility, and joint communication and sensing. Furthermore, we identify the key challenges that still hinder practical deployment, such as limited transmitter-to-tag operating range and packet loss in frequency-shifted backscatter. We believe that addressing these challenges will be critical to enabling WiFi backscatter to become a practical communication technology for future green IoT systems.

</details>


### [61] [Deconstructing BLE Multi-hop: a Model-based Approach to Quantifying the Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34483)
*Bozheng Pang,José Alamos,Thomas C. Schmidt,Matthias Wählisch*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 研究发现BLE多跳网络无法完全消除时钟漂移的影响，碰撞频率在长期运行中保持固定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BLE原本设计用于点对点通信，但多跳网络引入了节点时钟差异导致的异步问题，这对网络正常建立和运行构成挑战。需要量化分析时钟漂移对BLE多跳网络的影响，以评估其可行性并提出改进方案。

Method: 开发数学模型来量化单设备管理的BLE连接中的碰撞概率，为受限部署场景提供简化版本，并通过实际实验进行验证。

Result: 研究表明BLE多跳网络无法在大多数情况下消除时钟漂移的影响；此外，从长远来看，BLE连接之间的碰撞以固定频率发生。

Conclusion: 该研究清晰地界定了BLE在多跳网络中的局限性，并提出了可能的解决方案，如拓扑限制、BLE栈或硬件的修改，以缓解时钟漂移带来的问题。

Abstract: Bluetooth Low Energy (BLE) was originally designed for point-to-point communication, but BLE multi-hop networks have attracted academic and industrial interest. Multi-hop communication, however, introduces desynchronized nodes to the network due to the difference in individual clocks. In this paper, we study the impact of clock drift on a BLE multi-hop network. The aim is to find out if a BLE multi-hop network can be properly set up, why, and how. For this goal, we develop a mathematical model to quantify the collision probability among BLE connections managed by a single device. We provide a simplified version of the model for constrained deployment. The impact of clock drift and our mathematical model are validated through practical experiments. Afterward, we find that a BLE multi-hop network cannot eliminate the impact of clock drift in most cases. Moreover, from a long-term point of view, the collision between BLE connections occurs with a fixed frequency. We further analyze the limitation of using BLE to form multi-hop networks, and propose possible solutions, such as topology limitation, changes to BLE stack or hardware. This paper is a clear and quantitative problem statement on the limitations of using BLE in multi-hop networks.

</details>


### [62] [Task-Oriented Communications for Edge-Assisted Multi-View Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35173)
*Zhengru Fang,Huanhuan Lou,Senkang Hu,Yihang Tao,Zongdian Li,Yiqin Deng,Jingjing Wang,Yuguang Fang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种网络自适应的任务导向通信框架，用于在无GPS环境下（如城市峡谷、室内设施、干扰欺骗环境）通过视觉与地理标记数据库匹配，解决无人机（UAV）和无人地面车辆（UGV）的绝对定位问题。该框架联合优化了卸载决策、视角与语义速率选择以及客户端调度，以应对车载计算与能量受限及无线链路动态变化的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在城市峡谷、室内及受干扰环境中，无人机和无人地面车辆常失去卫星定位，需依赖基于视觉的地理标记数据库匹配进行绝对定位。然而，受限于车载计算能力和能量，定位任务往往需要卸载至边缘端，但无线链路具有时变吞吐量。如何在有限的通信预算下，自适应地决定何时卸载、传输哪些数据以及服务哪个客户端，是实现高效定位的核心难题。

Method: 该框架结合了三种关键技术：1. 可扩展的正交正则化变分信息瓶颈（O-VIB）编码，支持按重要性排序的潜在前缀和不同视角子集的压缩；2. 信息价值（VOI）引导的请求控制，仅当预测的定位风险降低值大于通信与服务成本时，才触发边缘协助请求；3. VOI加权的李雅普诺夫调度，在匹配的每路线流量预算下，动态决定服务哪个客户端及分配资源。

Result: 在CARLA多视角无人机数据集上，相比有预算的周期性卸载，该方法将平均和p95路线误差分别降低了24.8%和31.0%。在真实的室内无人机和UGV实验中，相比未压缩的全视角CLIP检索，平均位置误差分别降低了28.0%和14.4%，同时描述符流量大幅削减了98.6%和98.2%。此外，在高拥塞情况下，价值感知整形技术将前10%高价值请求的边缘侧p95延迟降低了76.2%（从137.7 ms降至32.8 ms）。

Conclusion: 所提出的网络自适应任务导向通信框架，通过智能化的O-VIB编码、VOI引导的请求控制和李雅普诺夫调度，显著提升了定位精度，极大减少了传输数据量，并在高网络拥塞下有效保障了高优先级请求的实时性，展现了优异的网络适应性与实际应用价值。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) and unmanned ground vehicles (UGVs) often lose satellite positioning in urban canyons, indoor facilities, and jammed or spoofed environments, making vision-based matching with geo-tagged databases important for absolute positioning. However, limited onboard computation and energy often require localization to be offloaded over wireless links with time-varying throughput. We present a network-adaptive task-oriented communication framework that jointly determines when to offload, which views and semantic rate to transmit, and which client to serve. The framework combines scalable orthogonality-regularized variational information bottleneck (O-VIB) encoding, value-of-information (VOI)-guided request control, and VOI-weighted Lyapunov scheduling. O-VIB supports importance-ordered latent prefixes and different view subsets, while edge assistance is requested only when its predicted reduction in localization risk exceeds the communication and service cost. Under a matched per-route traffic budget, VOI-guided control reduces mean and p95 route errors by 24.8% and 31.0% over budgeted periodic offloading on CARLA multi-view UAV data.
