In real-world indoor UAV and UGV experiments, the pipeline reduces mean position error by 28.0% and 14.4% over uncompressed all-view CLIP retrieval while cutting descriptor traffic by 98.6% and 98.2%, respectively. Under high congestion, value-aware shaping reduces edge-side p95 latency for the top-10% high-value requests by 76.2%, from 137.7 ms to 32.8 ms.

</details>


### [63] [Configuration-Induced Delivery Failures in NATS JetStream: Detection and Remediation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35310)
*Biplab Kumar Das*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: NATS JetStream's at-least-once delivery guarantee can be silently violated by five configuration errors, leading to duplicates, data loss, or redelivery storms. The standard Prometheus NATS exporter cannot detect these issues. We introduce nats-lens, a standalone monitor that uses JetStream's management API to detect all five violation classes with 100% coverage, zero false positives, and sub‑3‑second latency, supporting consumers in Rust, Go, and Python.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The need to reliably detect configuration mistakes that break JetStream's at‑least‑once delivery guarantee, as existing metrics (Prometheus NATS exporter) lack visibility into these failure modes and cause hidden data corruption or performance issues.

Method: Analyzed JetStream's management API to define precise conditions for each of the five violation classes; proved that Prometheus NATS metrics are structurally insufficient for detection; implemented five targeted detectors in nats-lens; evaluated on single‑node and 3‑node clusters (30 rounds per scenario) measuring detection coverage, false positives, and latency; validated language‑agnostic operation with Rust, Go, and Python consumers.

Result: nats-lens achieved 100% detection coverage across all five violation classes (0% for baseline), zero false positives over 30 minutes of healthy operation, detection latency of 2 003‑8 013 ms (within three poll cycles), and confirmed detection works across Rust, Go, and Python consumers.

Conclusion: nats-lens provides a comprehensive, language‑agnostic solution for monitoring JetStream configuration errors, filling the gap left by standard metrics and ensuring the at‑least‑once delivery guarantee is maintained without requiring changes to applications or client code.

Abstract: NATS JetStream's at-least-once delivery guarantee is conditional: five common configuration mistakes silently violate it, causing duplicate message processing, data loss, or redelivery storms with no error logged anywhere. The standard Prometheus NATS exporter exposes only server-level throughput metrics and cannot detect any of these failures. We present nats-lens, a standalone monitor that reads from the JetStream management API and detects all five violation classes without requiring changes to monitored applications or client code. We formally characterize each class with a precise condition, prove that standard Prometheus NATS metrics are structurally incapable of detecting any of them, and implement five targeted detectors. In a controlled evaluation of 30 rounds per scenario on both single-node and 3-node JetStream clusters, nats-lens achieves 100% detection coverage across all five classes---versus 0% for the baseline---with zero false positives over 30 minutes of healthy operation. Detection latency ranges from 2,003 ms to 8,013 ms (within three poll cycles). We confirm language-agnostic detection empirically using consumers in Rust, Go, and Python. The tool is open source and exposes findings through four output channels: web dashboard, Prometheus metrics, REST API, and NATS health events.

</details>


### [64] [Hybrid QKD-PQC Network Emulation through Automated and Scalable Cloud-Native Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35358)
*Iván Melijosa,Javier Pérez,Borja Nogales,Iván Vidal,Francisco Valera*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: Quditto平台通过云原生编排、优化配置、原生后量子节点和安全密钥管理，实现了可扩展的混合QKD-PQC网络仿真，支持低成本、可重复的实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于量子硬件成本高、可及性低以及现有仿真平台对混合QKD-PQC网络支持有限，亟需一种成本低且易于使用的仿真工具来评估量子安全网络架构。

Method: 在原有Quditto基础上添加四项核心功能：云原生编排器实现跨云多集群自动部署；优化的配置工作流支持大规模仿真；原生集成后量子节点实现QKD-PQC混合网络统一仿真；安全密钥管理模块提供持久、访问控制的密钥存储。

Result: 实验表明编排时间随网络规模的增长呈亚线性关系，并在代表性的spine-leaf拓扑上成功完成端到端的混合QKD-PQC密钥协商。

Conclusion: Quditto能够以低成本、高可重复性的方式在大规模异构网络环境中系统评估量子安全网络机制，为后续研究和标准化提供重要实验平台。

Abstract: The ongoing transition toward quantum-safe networking has motivated the development of hybrid network architectures integrating Quantum Key Distribution (QKD) and Post-Quantum Cryptography (PQC). However, the experimental evaluation of hybrid QKD-PQC network architectures remains constrained by the high cost and limited accessibility of quantum hardware, as well as by the limited support for hybrid QKD-PQC networks in existing emulation platforms. Quditto is an open-source emulation platform originally designed for QKD networks that enables cost-effective and reproducible experimentation without requiring dedicated physical quantum infrastructure. Building on this foundation, this work presents Quditto as a hybrid QKD-PQC network emulation platform featuring automated and scalable cloud-native orchestration. The proposed platform introduces four principal contributions: a cloud-native orchestrator enabling fully automated infrastructure deployment across cloud and multi-cluster environments; an optimized provisioning workflow enabling large-scale quantum-safe network emulation; native integration of post-quantum nodes enabling unified emulation of hybrid QKD-PQC networks; and a secure key management module providing persistent and access-controlled storage of cryptographic material. Experimental validation demonstrates sublinear orchestration-time scaling with network size and successful end-to-end hybrid QKD-PQC key establishment on a representative spine-leaf deployment, thereby enabling the systematic evaluation of quantum-safe networking mechanisms in large-scale heterogeneous network environments.

</details>


### [65] [Resource versus Responsiveness: Benchmarking SDN Controller Runtimes for a Moving-Target-Defense Control Plane at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35585)
*Souhail Chakkour,Umesh Biswas,Charan Gudla*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 在SDN-based Moving Target Defense中，控制器选择对性能有实质影响。研究将Continuity-Preserving Address Mutation逻辑移植至Ryu、OpenDaylight和ONOS三个控制器，在500宿校园网上实验。结果显示，All提供近零丢包、亚毫秒抖动且保持会话，但控制器行为差异大：OpenDaylight和ONOS保持低且稳定的 reactive latency，而Ryu因周期性轮换工作而延迟 reactive flow 安装，使 reactive RTT 增加约100倍并导致少量设置失败。Ryu内存占用最低，ONOS在实现OpenDaylight级别 reactive latency 的同时，CPU 利用率最低且 live heap 也小于OpenDaylight。这些结果揭示了资源与响应能力的不同运行点，表明在SDN-based MTD中，控制器应作为首要设计决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SDN-based Moving Target Defense通过持续旋转主机面地址来使攻击者的侦察无效，但每次旋转都会产生控制平面突变，且新连接可能需要即时流安装。现有文献通常将SDN控制器运行时视为实现细节，本文揭示其对性能的实质影响。

Method: 在相同的500宿校园fabric上，使用RFC 8456对齐的方法论，进行每个控制器十次运行，实验对比了三种 widely used SDN 控制器：单线程协作式Python的Ryu、以及多线程JVM的OpenDaylight和ONOS。全部移植了相同的Continuity-Preserving Address Mutation (CPAM) 逻辑。

Result: 三个控制器均提供近零损耗、亚毫秒抖动和会话保持。然而，控制平面行为差异显著：OpenDaylight和ONOS保持低且稳定的 reactive latency，而Ryu将 reactive flow 安装排在轮换工作之后，使 reactive RTT 增加约100倍并导致少量设置失败。Ryu内存消耗最少，ONOS在实现OpenDaylight级别 reactive latency 的同时，CPU 利用率最低且 live heap 也小于OpenDaylight。

Conclusion: 研究暴露了不同的资源-versus-响应能力运行点，表明在SDN-based MTD中，控制器选择应作为首要的设计决策，而非事后考量。

Abstract: Network Moving Target Defense (MTD) built on Software-Defined Networking (SDN) continuously rotates host-facing addresses to invalidate an attacker's reconnaissance. Each rotation creates a burst of control-plane mutations, while new connections may simultaneously require reactive flow installation. Yet SDN controller runtime is usually treated as an implementation detail in the MTD literature. We show that it materially affects performance. We port the same Continuity-Preserving Address Mutation (CPAM) logic to three widely used controllers: Ryu (single-threaded cooperative Python), OpenDaylight, and ONOS (both multi-threaded JVM), and evaluate them on an identical 500-host campus fabric using an RFC 8456-aligned methodology with ten runs per controller. All three provide near-zero loss, sub-millisecond jitter, and preserve established sessions, but their control-plane behavior differs sharply. OpenDaylight and ONOS keep reactive latency low and stable, whereas Ryu serializes reactive flow installation behind periodic rotation work, increasing reactive RTT by about 100x and causing a small number of setup-time failures. Ryu uses far less memory, while ONOS achieves OpenDaylight-class reactive latency with the lowest CPU utilization of the three and a smaller live heap than OpenDaylight. These results expose distinct resource-versus-responsiveness operating points and show that controller selection should be treated as a first-class design decision in SDN-based MTD.

</details>


### [66] [Energy-Driven Evaluation of Network Digital Twinning Applied to mmWave Beam Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35644)
*João Borges,Bruno Castro,Kleber Cardoso,Andrey Silva,Aldebaro Klautau*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: This paper investigates how twinning rate and fidelity affect Network Digital Twins (NDTs) for 6G, using real hardware measurements. The authors built a virtual twin with Sionna ray‑tracing and a physical twin with custom 60 GHz, 32‑antennas Wi‑Fi radios, then optimized both parameters for energy efficiency via Bayesian Optimization and what‑if analysis.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing studies treat NDT operational parameters (twinning rate, fidelity, etc.) in isolation or rely on simulations, missing practical insights from real hardware. This gap limits understanding of their joint impact on performance and energy consumption.

Method: Implemented a virtual twin (VTwin) using the Sionna ray‑tracing simulator and a physical twin (PTwin) with an in‑house 60 GHz, 32‑antenna Wi‑Fi setup. Conducted experiments to evaluate twinning rate and fidelity effects, performed what‑if analyses, and applied Bayesian Optimization to adjust these parameters while minimizing energy usage.

Result: Results show that fidelity levels change throughout the experiment and depend on what‑if difficulty. Twinning rate can be efficiently tuned using sample‑efficient Bayesian Optimization even at lower fidelity levels, revealing key insights for wireless channel NDTs.

Conclusion: Joint optimization of twinning rate and fidelity via Bayesian Optimization is feasible and enables energy‑efficient NDT operation, suggesting a path toward practical, scalable 6G network digital twins.

Abstract: Network Digital Twins (NDTs) are important enablers of 6G and future networks. However, there is a lack of studies regarding practical aspects, such as the impact of simultaneously changing twinning rate, fidelity, and other NDT operational parameters. For instance, works often consider the impact of operational parameters in isolation or with physical twin (PTwin) implementations relying on simulations. Therefore, the main contribution of this work is to provide an in-depth study on the performance impacts of both twinning rate and fidelity, using PTwins that rely on measurements obtained from hardware. We also investigate the optimization of these two operational parameters to minimize energy consumption, exploring a Bayesian Optimization (BO) method and what-if analysis. In this work, the NDT models an indoor propagation environment to optimize beam management. The virtual twin (VTwin) was implemented with the Sionna ray tracing (RT) simulator and the PTwin with our in-house setup composed of customized Wi-Fi radios with 32 antenna elements operating at 60 GHz. The study reveals important aspects of wireless channel NDTs, suggesting that fidelity levels vary throughout the experiment and with the what-if difficulty. Moreover, the twinning rate can be adjusted using sample-efficient methods, such as BO, even at lower fidelity levels.

</details>


### [67] [MINT: Modeling GenAI Impact on Network Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35742)
*Andrew Nguyen,Samson Kempiak,Agrim Gupta,Koushik Kar,Ish Kumar Jain*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: MINT is a measurement-driven framework for modeling GenAI traffic in packet-level network simulators, addressing the gap where existing simulators lack realistic GenAI traffic models and rely on approximations from traditional applications.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Generative AI is becoming a mainstream network workload, but current packet-level simulators lack measurement-driven GenAI traffic models, forcing researchers to approximate GenAI services using traditional sources like file transfer and video streaming.

Method: The authors developed MINT, a measurement and modeling framework that uses an isolated network-namespace capture pipeline to collect client-side traces from three LLM providers across four modalities, cloud and edge servers, and wired and wireless network access points. They cluster and model burst regimes and validate empirical burst timing distributions in ns-3 using normalized Wasserstein distances.

Result: MINT reveals that GenAI modalities exhibit distinct upload/download asymmetry and burst structures differing from traditional applications. Realistic packet bursts show significantly more variability than constant token generator models, with validation accuracy measured by normalized Wasserstein distances of 2-25%.

Conclusion: MINT provides the first open-source measurement-driven GenAI traffic model for packet-level network simulation, enabling more realistic evaluation of scheduling and capacity planning for GenAI workloads.

Abstract: Generative AI (GenAI) is becoming a mainstream network workload, yet packet-level simulators lack measure\-ment-driven GenAI traffic models. Currently researchers must approximate GenAI services using traditional sources such as file transfer and video streaming, limiting realistic network evaluation of scheduling and capacity planning. We present MINT, a measurement and modeling framework for GenAI network traffic. Using an isolated net\-work-namespace capture pipeline, we collect client-side traces from three LLM providers across four modalities, cloud and edge servers, and wired and wireless network access points. We find that GenAI modalities exhibit distinct upload/download asymmetry and burst structures that differ from traditional applications. MINT clusters and models these burst regimes and validate empirical burst timing distribution behavior in ns-3 with normalized Wasserstein distances of 2--25\%. Our results also reveal that realistic packet bursts have significantly more variability than constant token generator models. MINT open-sources the first measurement-driven GenAI traffic model for packet-level network simulation.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [68] [Resource-Efficient Speculative Decoding for Long-Context LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33184)
*Fei Li,Song Liu,Shiqiang Nie,Jinyu Wang,Weiguo Wu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: SpecStream 是一种投机解码系统，通过流式传输 KV 缓存块提前开始验证，利用计算气泡进行并发起草，在有限 GPU 内存下提高吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 KV 缓存卸载方案需恢复完整历史才能开始注意力计算，未充分利用验证轮内跨查询的 KV 共享；并行投机解码在显存带宽受限的目标验证等待历史 KV 时，GPU 算力闲置。

Method: SpecStream 在完整 KV 历史恢复前即启动验证，仅卸载目标模型已提交的历史，候选回滚保留在 GPU 本地；每个流式 KV 分块服务于轮内所有查询，配合在线 Softmax 保持完整注意力；目标优先调度控制草稿模型并发执行，限制对目标验证的干扰。

Result: 实验显示 SpecStream 任务质量接近 SGLang 投机解码，支持更多并发请求；在 Qwen3 和 InternLM2.5 上分别比卸载基线平均提速 1.41 倍和 1.32 倍；相比在独立 GPU 上运行目标与草稿模型的并行投机解码，单 GPU 输出吞吐平均提升 55.4%。

Conclusion: SpecStream 有效解决了 KV 缓存卸载瓶颈和 GPU 利用率不足问题，在保持生成质量的同时显著提升单 GPU 吞吐，适合长上下文、显存受限的服务场景。

Abstract: Speculative decoding reduces sequential Target model calls by verifying multiple tokens from the Draft model in parallel. Yet KV Cache growth limits long-context serving under constrained GPU memory. Offloading KV to CPU memory relieves this pressure. However, existing offloading schemes restore the full KV history before attention and fail to fully exploit the benefits of KV sharing across queries within a verification round. Existing parallel speculative decoding methods also overlook idle GPU compute capacity while memory-bandwidth-bound Target verification waits for historical KV. We present SpecStream, a speculative decoding system that begins verification without waiting for the full KV history to be restored and exploits compute bubbles during KV transfers for concurrent drafting on the same GPUs. It offloads only Target-committed history, keeping candidate rollback local to the GPUs. Each streamed KV chunk serves all queries in the round, with online softmax preserving full attention. Target-priority scheduling controls Draft concurrent execution under resource limits to constrain interference with Target verification. Experiments show that SpecStream maintains task quality close to SGLang speculative decoding while supporting more concurrent requests under limited GPU memory. Across different datasets, it achieves average throughput speedups of 1.41$\times$ and 1.32$\times$ over the offloading baseline for Qwen3 and InternLM2.5, respectively. Compared with parallel speculative decoding on separate Target and Draft GPUs, SpecStream improves output throughput per GPU by an average of 55.4%.

</details>


### [69] [MorphAtt: A Neuromorphic Accelerator for Efficient Multi-Head Attention Processing in Spiking Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33207)
*Rachmad Vidya Wicaksana Putra,Amirhesam Jafari Rad,Muhammad Shafique*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了MorphAtt数字加速器，用于加速Spiking Vision Transformers（SViTs）推理，通过SpikeQKV、SpikeAtten和RepConv模块实现高能效性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SViT虽然是计算机视觉任务的有效替代方案，但其巨大的参数规模和复杂的多头自注意力（MHSA）操作使得在边缘设备上实现高能效推理成为挑战

Method: 提出MorphAtt架构，包括Spiking Query-Key-Value生成器（SpikeQKV）、低复杂度Spiking多头自注意力引擎（SpikeAtten）和重参数化卷积（RepConv）模块，并集成专用中间模块缓冲区以缓解片上内存访问拥塞

Result: 在32nm CMOS工艺下，MorphAtt实现792-1605 GOPS的吞吐量，~39-55 mW的功耗，1.5 mm²的面积，达到20.3-29.1 TOPS/W的能效比，优于现有最先进方法

Conclusion: MorphAtt提供了更好的性能与效率权衡，使得边缘端高能效视觉AI系统成为可能

Abstract: Spiking Vision Transformers (SViTs) are developed as an energy-efficient alternative to conventional ViTs for computer vision tasks at the edge. However, huge parameter counts and complex multi-head self-attention (MHSA) operations make it challenging to achieve high energy efficiency in SViT inference, especially in tightly constrained applications. To maximize efficiency gains of SViT processing, we propose MorphAtt, a novel digital accelerator that expedites SViT inference through streamlined processing. Specifically, it processes MHSA operations using cascaded hardware modules: a Spiking Query-Key-Value generator (SpikeQKV), a low-complexity Spiking Multi-Head Self-Attention engine (SpikeAtten), and Reparameterization Convolution (RepConv) modules. To mitigate traffic congestion in on-chip memory accesses and data reuse, specialized inter-module buffers are integrated within the dataflow. Under synthesis using 32nm CMOS technology, MorphAtt achieves 792-1605 GOPS of throughput, while incurring ~39-55 mW of power consumption and 1.5 mm^2 of area, which lead to 20.3-29.1 TOPS/W of energy efficiency. These results also demonstrate that our MorphAtt offers better performance and efficiency trade-offs than state-of-the-art, thereby enabling highly energy-efficient vision-based AI systems at the edge.

</details>


### [70] [S-ALSA: Co-Design of Adiabatic Logic-based Sensing and Balanced Bit-Cells for Secure and Energy-Efficient MRAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33619)
*Wu Yang,Amit Degada,Himanshu Thapliyal*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于电路-器件协同设计的新型 MRAM 读电路（S-ALSA），它采用 4T-2MTJ 位元单元实现结构性电流平衡和 Adiabatic 电荷恢复实现动态功耗均衡，从而同时解决了传统 MRAM 读电路的高功耗和易受侧信道攻击的问题。实验结果显示，在 IoT 频率下相较于传统预充电 SA，S-ALSA 可实现高达 80% 的功耗节省，并在 PRESENT-80 加密 CPA 攻击中彻底消除了密钥泄漏风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 MRAM 读电路存在动态功耗高和因 MTJ 中电流变化引起的数据依赖侧信道漏洞，影响 IoT 设备的能效和安全。需要同时提升两种性能。

Method: 通过将 4T-2MTJ 位元单元与 Adiabatic 逻辑感应放大器相结合，实现结构性电流平衡（消除 MTJ 读电流不对称）和动态功耗均衡（利用 adiabatic 电荷恢复）。该架构适用于 STT-MRAM 和 SOT-STT-MRAM。

Result: 在 4x4 MRAM 宏的项目中，S-ALSA 相较于传统预充电 SA 在 IoT 频率下节省了约 80% 的能量；通过 CPA 攻击对 PRESENT-80 加密进行了验证，当使用平衡位元单元时，密钥完全不会泄漏。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个统一框架，在 MRAM 内存设计中同时实现了高能效和硬件安全性，为安全、低功耗的 IoT 系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: Magnetoresistive Random Access Memory (MRAM) technologies such as Spin-Transfer Torque (STT-MRAM) and Spin-Orbit Torque assisted (SOT-STT-MRAM) offer nonvolatility and low leakage, making them attractive for IoT systems. However, conventional MRAM read circuits face two fundamental challenges: high dynamic energy consumption and vulnerability to side-channel attacks caused by data-dependent current variations in Magnetic Tunnel Junctions (MTJs). This paper presents a Secured Adiabatic Logic Sense Amplifier (SALSA) that addresses both challenges simultaneously through circuit-device co-design. S-ALSA combines structural current balancing via a 4T-2MTJ bit cell, which eliminates read current asymmetry at the storage level, with dynamic power equalization via adiabatic charge recovery in the sensing circuit. The proposed architecture supports both STT-MRAM and SOT-STT-MRAM. Case studies using 4x4 MRAM macros shows up to 80% energy savings over conventional Pre-Charge Sense Amplifiers (PCSA) across IoT frequencies. Correlation Power Analysis (CPA) attacks on PRESENT-80 encryption confirm complete suppression of key leakage when S-ALSA is combined with a balanced bit cell. This work establishes a unified framework where energy efficiency and hardware security are achieved simultaneously, enabling secure and low-power IoT memory design.

</details>


### [71] [Improving Indirect Branch Prediction in Interpreters via Hardware/Software Co-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34263)
*Linfeng Zheng,Hiroshi Sasaki*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出一种硬件/软件协设方案，利用小型预取引擎和字节码元数据显著降低解释器间接分支预测误率，并在服务器工作负载上实现微小但有意义的 IPC 提速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释器存在较大的间接分支占用量，现有预测器需要较大容量才能保证准确预测，开销显著。

Method: 设计了仅需 1.3KB 片上存储、仅需修改约 50 行 CPython 代码的硬件预取引擎，该引擎在流水线前运行，利用软件提供的字节码元数据向前端供给解释器调度目标。

Result: 在 15 个 CPython 服务器工作负载上，14KB ITTAGE 配合该引擎使字节码跳跃 MPKI 相较 16KB ITTAGE 基准降低 73.7%，实现 3.2% 和谐均值 IPC 加速。

Conclusion: 该轻量级协设方案有效缓解了解释器分支预测瓶颈，展现出良好的性能提升空间与实现可行性。

Abstract: Interpreters have a large indirect-branch footprint, requiring large predictor capacity for accurate prediction. We propose a hardware/software co-design in which a hardware lookahead engine, running ahead of the pipeline with software-provided bytecode metadata, supplies interpreter dispatch targets to the frontend. The engine requires only 1.3 KB of on-chip storage and changes to about 50 lines of CPython code. On 15 CPython server workloads, a 14 KB ITTAGE augmented with the engine reduces bytecode jump MPKI by 73.7% relative to a 16 KB ITTAGE baseline, yielding a 3.2% harmonic-mean IPC speedup.

</details>


### [72] [PolyCIM: Improving Data Reuse in Digital CIM Accelerators with Polyhedral-Based Compilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34351)
*Yingjie Qi,Cenlin Duan,Yiou Wang,Yikun Wang,Xiaolin He,Weisheng Zhao,Jianlei Yang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: PolyCIM is a polyhedral compilation framework that re寫ins non微膔axis data reuse hyperplanes in CIM accelerators via affine transformations, yielding up to 4× higher macro utilization and 3.2× speedup for modern DNN operators.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CIM accelerators suffer from severe array underutilization because conventional mapping only exploits axis-aligned data reuse, missing the hyperplane structures of modern DNNs that are oriented along non微膔axial directions.

Method: PolyCIM introduces a unified polyhedral abstraction for both DNN workloads and CIM hardware, then applies systematic data‑reuse exposure, computation mapping, and data movement optimization through affine transformations to realign hyperplanes and generate efficient mappings.

Result: Experimental evaluation demonstrates up to 4× improvement in macro utilization and a 3.2× overall performance speedup, effectively bridging the gap between modern DNN operators and CIM architectures.

Conclusion: PolyCIM provides a scalable and effective solution for mapping diverse DNN operators onto CIM accelerators, significantly enhancing hardware utilization and computational performance.

Abstract: Digital Compute-in-Memory (CIM) presents a promising solution for accelerating deep neural networks (DNNs) through the integration of computational logic directly within memory arrays. However, mapping modern DNN operators to CIM accelerators often results in severe array underutilization, due to the strict data reuse constraints imposed by the rigid CIM array structure. We observe that data reuse in modern DNNs forms hyperplane structures often oriented along non-axial directions, rendering them invisible to conventional mapping methods that only exploit axis-aligned reuse. In this work, we propose PolyCIM, a polyhedral-based compilation framework for CIM architectures that systematically exposes and realigns these hyperplanes through affine transformations. PolyCIM provides a unified abstraction capable of efficiently representing both diverse DNN workloads and digital CIM architectures. Through data reuse exposure, computation mapping, and data movement optimization, PolyCIM generates mappings for CIM architectures that achieve superior array utilization and performance. Experimental results show that PolyCIM delivers up to $4\times$ improvement in macro utilization and $3.2\times$ speedup, effectively bridging the gap between modern DNN operators and CIM architectures.

</details>


### [73] [Coarse-to-Fine Macro Placement via Evolutionary Search and Critical Macro Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34452)
*Biao Liu,Zhiping Jin,Kaixuan Sun,Zengrui Lu,Qingquan Zhang,Bo Yuan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了 C2FPlace 粗细粒度宏单元放置框架，结合群体演化搜索与细粒度微调，解决了现有方法易陷入局部最优和放置精度不足的问题，显著降低了半周线长（HPWL）并优化了 PPA 指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宏单元放置是芯片物理设计的关键环节。现有基于搜索的方法中，质量偏差或空间受限的宏单元选择限制了重构多样性，容易导致局部最优；而粗网格表示则限制了放置精度，难以进一步优化线长。

Method: C2FPlace 包含两个阶段：粗粒度优化阶段，采用锦标赛选择和随机部分撕裂重放置进行全局探索，并通过两阶段调度动态调整重构比例（早期高比例探索，晚期低比例细化）；细粒度优化阶段，通过关键宏单元微调突破粗网格限制，进行精细位置调整以减少 HPWL。

Result: 在 ISPD2005 基准测试中，C2FPlace 的平均 HPWL 比 EGPlace 降低了 17.82%，比 RollPlace 降低了 17.86%；在 ICCAD2025 基准测试中，在功耗、性能和面积（PPA）指标上均取得了最佳平均排名。

Conclusion: C2FPlace 通过粗细粒度的协同优化，有效提升了宏单元放置的质量与精度，是一种具有竞争力的先进宏单元放置方法。

Abstract: Macro placement is a critical stage in chip physical design that substantially affects downstream implementation quality. Recent search-based methods improve existing layouts through partial reconstruction, but quality-biased or spatially restricted macro selection can limit the diversity of reconstruction proposals, potentially hindering escape from local optima. Moreover, coarse-grid representations restrict placement precision. To address these challenges, we propose C2FPlace, a \textbf{C}oarse-to-\textbf{F}ine macro \textbf{Place}ment framework that integrates population-based evolutionary search with fine-grained refinement. During coarse-grained optimization, tournament selection chooses promising parents from randomly sampled groups of layouts, and stochastic partial rip-up and re-place generates offspring by sampling macro subsets across the entire layout. A two-phase schedule samples reconstruction ratios from a higher range early in the search and a lower range later, supporting broad exploration followed by more conservative refinement. During fine-grained optimization, critical macro tuning enables positional adjustments beyond the coarse grid to obtain additional half-perimeter wirelength (HPWL) reduction. Experiments on the ISPD2005 benchmark show that C2FPlace reduces HPWL by 17.82\% over EGPlace and 17.86\% over RollPlace on average. On the ICCAD2025 benchmark, C2FPlace achieves the best average ranking among the compared methods under the evaluated power, performance, and area (PPA) metrics. Our codes are available in \href{https://github.com/lxxxxb/C2FPlace}{https://github.com/lxxxxb/C2FPlace}.

</details>


### [74] [SPIMOE: Exploiting Hybrid Sparsity for Reasoning MoE Inference on Heterogeneous PIM Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34612)
*Rubing Yang,Cenlin Duan,Yingjie Qi,Xiaolin He,Xiao Ma,Jianlei Yang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: SPIMOE is a co-design framework that combines adaptive routing, block-sparse attention, and physical KV-cache eviction to efficiently execute long‑reasoning MoE models on heterogeneous PIM architectures, achieving up to 8.35× end‑to‑end speedup over an A100 GPU and 3.33× speedup in MoE FFN execution while preserving accuracy.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Long‑reasoning MoE models suffer from two coupled bottlenecks—growing KV caches that shift the critical path to attention and sparse expert activation causing load imbalance and low hardware utilization—making it difficult to accelerate with existing PIM solutions that optimize attention or FFNs in isolation.

Method: SPIMOE introduces hybrid sparsity, adaptive expert routing, block‑sparse attention, physical KV‑cache eviction, and disaggregates attention and FFNs across SRAM‑PIM and HBM‑PIM, using static expert mapping and dynamic sub‑batch scheduling to balance channel loads and overlap the two inference paths.

Result: Evaluations show up to 8.35× end‑to‑end speedup over an NVIDIA A100 GPU and 3.33× speedup in MoE FFN execution over PIMoE, with reasoning accuracy comparable to full‑attention baselines.

Conclusion: By co‑designing the MoE inference pipeline with hybrid sparsity and PIM‑aware scheduling, SPIMOE effectively removes the key bottlenecks, delivering substantial performance gains while maintaining accuracy.

Abstract: Long-reasoning Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models expose two coupled inference bottlenecks: growing KV caches shift the critical path toward attention, while sparse expert activation causes load imbalance and low hardware utilization. Although Processing-in-Memory (PIM) offers a promising way to mitigate data movement overhead, existing PIM-based accelerators typically optimize attention or FFNs in isolation. We propose SPIMOE, the first co-design framework that exploits hybrid sparsity for efficient MoE inference on heterogeneous PIM architectures. SPIMOE combines adaptive expert routing with block-sparse attention and physical KV-cache eviction, and disaggregates attention and FFNs across SRAM-PIM and HBM-PIM. Static expert mapping and dynamic sub-batch scheduling further balance channel loads and overlap the two paths. Evaluations show that SPIMOE achieves up to $8.35\times$ end-to-end speedup over an NVIDIA A100 GPU and $3.33\times$ speedup in MoE FFN execution over PIMoE, while preserving reasoning accuracy comparable to full-attention baselines.

</details>


### [75] [Torch-PIM: Automated Profile-Guided PIM Offloading for PyTorch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34657)
*Heeeon Lee,Hyunwoo Nam,Junyong Heo,Hyunmo Sung,Jay Hwan Lee,Yeonsoo Kim,Seongho Jeong,Shinhyung Yang,Bernd Burgstaller*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: Torch-PIM 是一个编译器框架，利用基于剖析的优化（PGO）在进进性降低生成的循环嵌套上决定主机与处理内存（PIM）的放置，实现深度学习工作负载的自动卸载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习工作负载受限于数据移动，PIM 通过将计算单元置于内存附近来解决该瓶颈，但 PyTorch 等深度学习框架缺乏编译器支持以在降低代码时做出卸载决策；现有卸载框架针对手写 C/C++ 程序，而面向深度学习的框架在降低前就将候选集固定为算子类型列表。

Method: Torch-PIM 将流水线生成的每个并行循环嵌套纳入候选空间，通过两阶段评估：工作量和内存受限程度。所有评估所需量要么在主机上剖析获取，要么从多层中间表示（MLIR）中获得。

Result: 在 32 至 128 核的 PIM 配置下，Torch-PIM 的卸载决策相比仅 CPU 执行实现了最高 8.6 倍张量算子加速、2.9 倍 MLP 加速、4.4 倍 Attention 加速、5.1 倍 GPT-J-6B 加速和 3.6 倍 LLaMA-7B 加速。

Conclusion: Torch-PIM 通过编译器层面的剖析引导优化，细粒度地在循环嵌套级别决定 PIM 卸载，有效解决了深度学习框架缺乏自动 PIM 卸载支持的问题，并在多种模型和算子上显著提升了性能。

Abstract: Modern deep learning (DL) workloads are limited by data movement, and processing-in-memory (PIM) targets this bottleneck by placing compute units near the memory. However, PyTorch and other DL frameworks lack compiler support for making this decision on the code they lower: existing offloading frameworks target hand-written C/C++ programs, while those that address DL fix the candidate set to a list of operator types before lowering. We present Torch-PIM, a compiler framework that uses profile-guided optimization (PGO) to decide host-versus-PIM placement over the loop nests that progressive lowering materializes. Every parallel loop nest the pipeline emits enters the candidate space, and each is assessed in two stages: the amount of work it carries, and its memory boundedness. Every quantity the assessment consumes is profiled on the host or obtained from the multi-level intermediate representation (MLIR) of the code. Across PIM configurations of 32 to 128 cores, Torch-PIM's offloading decisions yield speedups of up to 8.6x on tensor operators, 2.9x on MLP, 4.4x on Attention, 5.1x on GPT-J-6B, and 3.6x on LLaMA-7B over CPU-only execution.

</details>


### [76] [MEGATRON: a 28nm Analog PCM CiM/Digital System-on-Chip for Edge GenAI at 57.5 TOPS/W and 1.52 Mparam/mm${}^2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35254)
*Alessandro Nadalini,Angelo Garofalo,Lorenzo Greco,Andrea Belano,Alessio Antolini,Francesco Zavalloni,Andrea Lico,Riccardo Zurla,Emanuela Calvetti,Luigi Croce,Marco Pasotti,Alessandro Cabrini,Eleonora Franchi Scarselli,Davide Rossi,Francesco Conti*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: MEGATRON是一款基于28nm FD-SOI CMOS工艺的异构Edge GenAI SoC，结合了基于相变存储器(PCM)的类比存储在内计算引擎和基于RISC-V的灵活数字神经处理单元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发低功耗、高性能且面积紧凑的Edge GenAI芯片，以满足边缘设备对能效和存储密度的严苛要求。

Method: 采用28nm FD-SOI CMOS工艺集成非易失性PCM阵列（4Mi cells）实现模拟存储在内计算，并结合数字RISC-V神经处理单元进行灵活运算。

Result: 实现了高达3.5 TOPS/W（RISC-V处理器）和57.5 TOPS/W（PCiM）的能效，以及1.52 Mparam/mm²的存储密度，权重精度为4位。

Conclusion: MEGATRON通过异构架构和存储在内计算技术显著提升了Edge GenAI系统的能效与存储密度，适用于资源受限的边缘场景。

Abstract: We present MEGATRON, a heterogeneous Edge GenAI System-on-Chip in 28nm FD-SOI CMOS technology combining a non-volatile analog in-memory-computing engine based on a 4Mi-cell phase-change memory (PCM) array with a digital RISC-V-based flexible neural processing unit. MEGATRON demonstrates up to 3.5 TOPS/W using the RISC-V processors and 57.5 TOPS/W with PCiM, at a storage density of 1.52 Mparam/mm${}^2$ with 4-bit effective weight precision.

</details>


### [77] [Analog Computing revisited: A fully analog and minimalistic Damage Detector for Ultrasonic Testing enabling Material-Integrated Structural Health Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35478)
*Stefan Bosse*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于有机印刷电路的全模拟超声波损伤检测系统，利用模拟希尔伯特变换实现包络检测、模拟算术运算提取特征，并通过模拟神经网络进行分类和回归预测。该系统旨在将晶体管数量控制在100以下以降低功耗和提高鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字信号处理系统虽然功能强大，但存在芯片面积和能耗高、易受电源干扰等问题。研究旨在探索一种完全模拟的处理方法，利用低功耗的印刷有机电子技术实现传感器级损伤检测，避免数字处理带来的诸多劣势。

Method: 通过制作印刷有机电路实现模拟希尔伯特变换（采用全通滤波网络逼近）以获取超声波信号包络；使用模拟加法器和乘法器等组件进行特征提取；构建一个完全模拟的神经网络，用于缺陷状态的分类和缺陷尺寸的回归。系统以钢板上的PZT换能器信号作为测试数据。

Result: 成功实现了基于模拟计算的完整损伤检测流程，包括模拟包络检测、特征提取和神经网络预测。实验验证了系统能够可靠地检测圆形缺陷并估计其尺寸，为低功耗、高集成度的传感器级损伤监测提供了一种新的解决方案。

Conclusion: 本工作展示了印刷有机电子技术在传感器级模拟信号处理方面的潜力。该系统在低晶体管数量下实现了从信号预处理到机器学习的全模拟处理，为开发自带计算能力的智能传感器开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Ultrasonic Testing (UT) is commonly used to detect damage in structures, e.g., metal plates. A sensor acquires Ultrasonic waves, e.g., by using PZT transducers. The time-resolved sensor signal must be processed with analog electronics, e.g., amplified and filtered. Commonly a digitalization follows using an Analog-to-Digital converter, finally processing the digital sensor signal, applying digital signal processing, feature extraction, and Machine Learning by using powerful microprocessor systems. The disadvantages of digital processing systems are their high number of transistors (microchip area), energy consumption, state-dependent processing and therefore sensitivity to energy supply interruption. Beyond silicon electronics, printed organic electronics gains interest. But printed electronics is still limited to low transistor and electronic component counts (typically 100). We will investigate and demonstrate a fully analog signal processing and feature extraction system consisting of an analog Hilbert transform deriving the signal envelope, simple analog arithmetic calculations for feature extraction, and finally damage classification and regression using an analog Artificial Neural Network. We expect a full damage detection system with less than 100 transistors. We will test our damage detection system with PZT transducer signals from Steel plates with circular defects. The focus of this work is the analog computation of the signal envelope (using all-pass filter networks for approximation of the Hilbert transform) and the analog feature extraction as well as the prediction of damage, forming an analog computer which can perform in-sensor computation, computing without a digital computer.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [78] [IceCube Takes Flight with Pelican - A First Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31851)
*David Schultz,Maclean Mansfield-Parisi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: IceCube migrated from GridFTP and x.509 certificate authentication to a token‑based system using Pelican, OSDF, and WLGC tokens, implementing custom token issuer and HTCondor modifications to maintain POSIX permissions and simplify job submission; after early issues, the new system now outperforms GridFTP.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To modernize data transfers, improve security and usability, and align with current computing infrastructure while preserving IceCube’s existing POSIX filesystem permissions and easing scientist interaction with data.

Method: Developed a custom WLCG token issuer for POSIX filesystem handling with bespoke user/group permissions; modified HTCondor with a custom credmon to support token‑based job submission; transitioned to Pelican Platform, OSDF, and WLGC tokens, addressing initial Pelican issues through troubleshooting.

Result: The token‑based Pelican system now provides superior performance over GridFTP, offers streamlined token authentication, and maintains compatibility with existing storage and job submission workflows.

Conclusion: The migration successfully resolved initial difficulties, delivering a more robust, secure, and user‑friendly data transfer and job submission environment for IceCube scientists.

Abstract: The IceCube Neutrino Observatory has removed GridFTP and x.509 certificate authentication for data transfers, migrating to the Pelican Platform, the Open Science Data Federation, and WLGC tokens. While this is a common solution on the computing infrastructure we use, we required several customizations to work with our existing data storage structure and make it easier for scientists to use. We wrote a custom WLCG token issuer to support our POSIX filesystem with custom user and group permissions across the entire filesystem. We also made several modifications to HTCondor to ease job submission using tokens, including a custom credmon. After an initially bumpy transition due to several now-resolved Pelican issues, the Pelican-based system has already proven superior to GridFTP in several ways.

</details>


### [79] [Towards Simple Models of Complex SmartNICs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32055)
*Robert Chang,Teng Jiang,Wonsup Yoon,Daehyeok Kim,Sam Kumar,George Varghese*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为ZRAM的模型，用于在SmartNIC平台上选择和评估应用程序任务的部署方式，它结合了平台图形和程序图形，计算可行性、性能限制和容量，并为DDoS检测等用例提取了设计模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着云供应商在NIC上进行昂贵的计算（如加密、遥测）以跟上千兆以太网速度，SmartNIC的编程变得复杂且难以预测。设计人员需要一种廉价且快速的方法来评估部署选项并识别可能的瓶颈，而无需编写任何代码。

Method: ZRAM将处理区域和通道建模为平台图形，并将任务和流量比例建模为程序图形。通过映射程序图形到平台图形，计算三个直接指标：能力、屋顶线和容量。用于评估放置的可行性和表示瓶颈资源。该方法用于DDoS检测、决策树推断和RDMA遍历案例研究，并提取了七种编程SmartNIC的设计模式。

Result: ZRAM为DDoS检测、决策树推断和RDMA遍历工作负载提供了可行的部署模式，并识别了关键瓶颈。提出了七种通用设计模式（包括'筛选'模式）。该方法已扩展到Intel IPU E2200和AMD Pensando Salina 400 SmartNIC。

Conclusion: ZRAM为SmartNIC编程提供了一个统一的框架，有助于编译器和硬件设计者更快地选择优化的部署方案，为SmartNIC生态系统开辟了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Cloud vendors push ambitious in-network processing (e.g., crypto, telemetry) onto the NIC to offload servers even as link rates climb to terabit speeds. Vendors have responded with heterogeneous SmartNICs. For example, NVIDIA BlueField-3 interposes---between the wire and the host CPUs---a line-rate eSwitch, a multithreaded Data-Path Accelerator, general-purpose ARM cores, and a sea of fixed-function accelerators. These devices are notoriously hard to program, and harder still to predict. Applications can be implemented in many ways, with each choice potentially hitting a different bottleneck. A designer ideally needs to know---cheaply, and before a line of code is written---feasible choices and their bottlenecks, and design patterns to improve performance.
  Our paper offers a starting point to answer these questions using what we call the ZRAM model. It pairs a platform graph of processing zones and their channels with a program graph of tasks and their traffic fractions. The application designer or a compiler chooses a placement that maps the program graph onto the platform graph. Three metrics computed directly from this mapping---capability, roofline, and capacity---score the placement, deciding its feasibility and naming the bottleneck resource. We use a DDoS detector as a primary case study, and briefly explore two other applications, decision-tree inference and RDMA traversal. We distill seven design patterns for programming SmartNICs including a key one we call sifting. ZRAM generalizes to other SmartNICs such as Intel IPU E2200 and AMD Pensando Salina 400, and opens a new research agenda that includes compilers and hardware design.

</details>


### [80] [Distributed Service Orchestration in Edge-Cloud Continuum for Digital Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28855)
*Johirul Islam,Hafiz Faheem Shahid,Ijaz Ahmad,Tanesh Kumar,Ayan Mondal,Erkki Harjula*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 研究提出了一个三层注册架构，结合Docker Hub的远程公共注册表、MEC‑based的远程私有注册表和LAN‑based的本地私有注册表来部署边缘节点上的微服务。通过实验证明，该架构在网络中断时具有更好的容错性，并在部署时间、网络计算负载和能耗方面优于单一的远程公共注册表，从而验证了亲和性‑aware服务分发的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当边缘设备上的服务依赖于集中化的注册服务器进行部署时，如果网络连接或注册服务器本身不可用，就会导致服务部署失败，从而影响服务可用性。研究旨在通过引入分层注册机制来提高边缘服务部署的可靠性和容错能力。

Method: 提出了三层注册架构：1) 远程公共注册表（如Docker Hub）；2) 基于MEC的远程私有注册表；3) 基于LAN的本地私有注册表。通过测量从不同注册表级联部署所需的微服务，评估部署时间、网络和计算负载及能耗等指标，验证了该架构的性能。

Result: 实验结果表明，多层注册策略提高了网络中断情况下的容错能力。本地私有注册表和远程私有注册表在部署时间和资源消耗方面均优于远程公共注册表，从而提升了整体部署效率和性能。

Conclusion: 分层亲和性aware注册机制增强了边缘服务部署的弹性，提高了性能和效率。因此，建议在边缘计算环境中采用本地及远程私有注册表与公共注册表的组合部署方式。

Abstract: Today's digital healthcare services rely on various applications and functions that must be continuously accessible. Cloud computing enables global access to these services through public networks, which often also introduce increased latency, higher bandwidth consumption, and additional security risks compared to local operation. Edge computing mitigates these challenges by deploying cloud services closer to the end users and data sources, thereby improving resilience to network disruptions and reducing latency, bandwidth usage, and exposure to security threats. However, service deployment typically relies on the availability of centralized registry servers. Consequently, if the network connection or the registry server itself becomes unavailable, service deployment at the target edge node may fail. To address this, we propose a three-tier registry architecture to enhance deployment reliability and service availability, considering DockerHub as a remote public registry, an MEC-based off-premises registry as a remote private registry, and a LAN-based on-premises registry as a local private registry. The performance and efficiency are analyzed through measurements related to the estimated deployment time, inflicted network and computational load, and energy consumption, while the required nanoservices are deployed from different tiers. The experimental results indicate that alongside the improved tolerance to network disruptions, the local private and remote private registries also outperform the remote public registry in the deployment performance. The findings highlight the effectiveness of proximity-aware service distribution in improving the resilience, performance and efficiency of service deployment.

</details>


### [81] [Empowering Hybrid Attention Models on NPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32114)
*Yinyuan Zhang,Daliang Xu,Xiaolong Huang,Wangsong Yin,Yun Ma,Mengwei Xu,Gang Huang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: HA-NPU is the first system to enable efficient hybrid attention LLM inference on edge NPUs without modifying underlying algorithms. It achieves up to 35.95× LA kernel speedup and 2.03× faster end-to-end latency.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hybrid attention models (e.g., Qwen3.5, Kimi series) are crucial for LLMs and attractive for on-device inference due to memory/compute efficiency. However, naive execution on edge NPUs fails to deliver benefits, especially bottlenecking the prefill stage due to memory-system inefficiencies and architectural mismatches in linear attention layers.

Method: HA-NPU reorganizes the dataflow of linear attention components across three levels: (1) Core level: partitions workloads by head dimension and fuses dependent operators to eliminate cross-core global memory accesses; (2) Operator level: reorders execution to consume intermediate tensors immediately, minimizing local-buffer pressure; (3) Tensor level: employs dataflow-aware layout planning to minimize transformation overhead between matrix and vector processing units.

Result: Compared to competitive baselines, HA-NPU achieves up to 35.95× LA kernel speedup, 36.14× energy reduction, and 2.03× faster end-to-end request latency.

Conclusion: HA-NPU effectively addresses the inefficiencies of running hybrid attention models on edge NPUs through multi-level dataflow optimization, enabling practical and efficient on-device LLM inference.

Abstract: Hybrid attention models have emerged as a crucial architecture for Large Language Models (LLMs) (e.g., the Qwen3.5 and Kimi series). Their memory and computational efficiency make them highly attractive for on-device inference, forming a promising synergy with edge Neural Processing Units (NPUs). However, naive execution of these hybrid models on edge NPUs fails to deliver these benefits, often bottlenecking the prefill stage due to severe memory-system inefficiencies and architectural mismatches within the linear attention (LA) layers. We present HA-NPU, the first system to enable efficient hybrid attention LLM inference on edge NPUs without modifying the underlying algorithms. HA-NPU enhances execution efficiency by reorganizing the dataflow of the LA components across three levels: (1) At the core level, it partitions workloads by the head dimension and fuses dependent operators, eliminating cross-core global memory accesses; (2) At the operator level, it reorders execution to consume intermediate tensors immediately, drastically minimizing local-buffer pressure; (3) At the tensor level, it employs dataflow-aware layout planning to minimize transformation overhead between matrix and vector processing units. Compared to competitive baselines, HA-NPU achieves up to 35.95$\times$ LA kernel speedup and 36.14$\times$ energy reduction, delivering up to 2.03$\times$ faster end-to-end request latency. The source code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/yinyuanzhang/HA-NPU

</details>


### [82] [SparSP: Exploiting Communication Sparsity for Sequence-Parallel Video DiTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32197)
*Desen Sun,Xinrui Zhong,Yuke Wang,Sihang Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 论文提出SparSP，这是一个专门为稀疏视频扩散模型设计的稀疏序列并行通信系统，通过协调块的分布、基于需求的KV路由和异步通信运行时，显著降低了计算和通信开销，带来了显著的加速和带宽提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成模型的计算开销巨大，需要跨多GPU进行扩展，但PCIe链路的带宽有限。现有的稀疏注意力机制虽然降低了计算量，但其通信开销仍不为人知，传统的分布式训练框架无法有效利用稀疏注意力模式。

Method: SparSP 利用三个互补的技术：1) 依赖感知布放：根据稀疏注意力模式对序列块进行智能分布；2) 需求驱动的KV路由：直接将KV块发送到请求GPU，避免中间中转；3) 解耦传输运行时：将通信操作与GPU计算分离，以减少资源竞争，提高带宽利用率。通过这种系统级的协同设计，SparSP 能够更好地利用稀疏注意力模式下的数据局部性。

Result: 评估结果显示，SparSP 在注意力计算方面实现了 1.38‑1.5× 的加速；在三种服务器和三种视频扩散模型上，端到端速度平均提升了 1.17×1.69×；通信量减少了 12.54‑23.055%；相对 NCCL，平均带宽提升了 1.53‑1.76×。

Conclusion: 通过将注意力稀疏性视为通信原语，SparSP 有效地降低了跨PCIe GPU服务器上的通信开销，为大规模视频扩散模型训练提供了高性价比的扩展方案。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers have become the dominant architecture for video generation. Their substantial computational cost motivates scaling inference across multi-GPU servers, yet efficient scaling remains challenging on commodity GPUs connected via PCIe, whose bandwidth is limited. Although sparse attention substantially reduces computation, its implications for communication remain underexplored.
  This paper argues that attention sparsity should be treated as a communication primitive. We present SparSP, an efficient sparse sequence parallel communication system that co-designs token distribution, communication routing, and asynchronous execution for sparse video diffusion models. First, Dependency-Aware Placement distributes sequence blocks according to diffusion models' sparse attention patterns. Second, Demand-Directed KV Routing transfers KV blocks directly to requesting GPUs without intermediate relays. Third, a Decoupled Transfer Runtime separates communication from GPU computation to reduce resource contention and maximize effective bandwidth. Our evaluation shows that SparSP improves attention performance by 1.38- 1.5$\times$, achieves an average 1.17$\times$ (up to 1.69$\times$) end-to-end speedup across three representative servers and three video diffusion models, and reduces communication volume by 12.54-23.05%. Moreover, we achieve an average 1.53-1.76$\times$ bandwidth improvement over NCCL.

</details>


### [83] [REBASE: Device-Cloud Experience Coherence for GUI Agents Across App Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32130)
*Beining Wu,Jun Huang,Yanxiao Zhao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出 REBASE 协议，通过在版本更新时在云端重放并本地逐步验证经验动作，实现设备与云端 GUI 经验的一致性，使旧经验的成功率恢复至新经验水平，且通信开销和能耗显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当手机应用更新时，设备上缓存的旧经验动作会变得失效，导致代理成功率下降且难以及时发现错误，因而需要一种轻量的机制来保持经验在版本变化中的一致性。

Method: REBASE 包含云端在新版本上重放经验、设备在执行前逐步验证每一步、失败时按递增粒度（从可访问性子树 diff 开始）向云端发送证据，云端基于版本重新导出可执行补丁并分发给全 fleet。

Result: 在九对真实应用版本对中，携带旧经验的代理比无经验代理成功率低 10.1 分数，且错误通常在三步后才被发现；使用 REBASE 后， stale experience 的成功率恢复到 fresh experience 水平，相比上传截图方案减少 708 倍字节数和 47.3 倍云端调用，且比无经验运行节省 2.5 倍设备能耗。

Conclusion: 通过轻量级的经验一致性协议，REBASE 能够在应用更新后快速恢复代理性能，显著降低通信和能源开销，为移动端 GUI 代理的持续学习提供可行方案。

Abstract: A graphical user interface (GUI) agent that ships on a phone runs a small model and reuses experience: action paths recorded on earlier runs and cached from the cloud. When an app updates, part of this experience becomes silently wrong. On nine real app version pairs, an agent carrying experience recorded on the old version succeeds 10.1 points less often than one carrying none, and it does not notice the mismatch until 3 steps after acting on it. We propose REBASE, a protocol that keeps the device copy and the cloud copy of the experience coherent across updates. When a version changes, the cloud replays its copy on the new version and the device verifies each recorded step before executing it; when a step still fails, the device sends the cloud evidence in increasing size, starting from an accessibility-subtree diff, until the cloud re-derives an executable patch, keyed by version so that one repair serves the whole fleet. On two Jetson devices, REBASE restores the success rate of stale experience to that of fresh experience recorded on the new version within one episode, at 708x fewer bytes and 47.3x fewer cloud calls than uploading screenshots to the cloud, and 2.5x less device energy than running without experience.

</details>


### [84] [ThreadShift: Transparent Thread-Level Offloading on Transient Cloud Resources Using MPKs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32153)
*Antoine Murat,Clément Burgelin,Rachid Guerraoui*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了 ThreadShift 系统，透明地将未修改的 Linux 应用中的计算密集型线程卸载到廉价的瞬时云资源，通过线程粒度的细粒度检查点和内存依赖跟踪，在保证正确性的同时在云成本上最多节省 56%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 瞬时云资源虽具成本优势，但不可预测性使得无法安全重启的应用难以使用，现有方法需应用修改或依赖粗粒度检查点，成本限制了效益。

Method: ThreadShift 在线程粒度操作，通过高效跟踪内存依赖、识别每线程内存写入并在多机间维持一致性，利用三种创新的内存保护键（MPK）用法实现无暂停的增量检查点，卸载线程耗时仅 164 μs，检查点速度比 CRIU 快三个数量级。

Result: 在机器学习推理、加密任务和内存处理工作负载上的评估显示，ThreadShift 可在瞬时资源上安全卸载线程，实现细粒度检查点，并将部署成本降低至最多 56%。

Conclusion: ThreadShift 展示了无需应用修改即可利用瞬时云资源的可行性，通过线程粒度的 MPK 机制平衡了性能、正确性与成本，为异构云环境中的透明卸载提供了新思路。

Abstract: Transient cloud resources offer significant cost savings, but their unpredictability makes them hard to use for applications that cannot be safely restarted after reclamation. Existing approaches require application changes or rely on coarse-grained checkpointing, whose cost limits its benefits.
  We present ThreadShift, a system that transparently offloads compute-intensive threads of unmodified Linux applications to cheap transient resources while preserving correctness under reclamation. By operating at thread granularity, ThreadShift enables fast, fine-grained checkpointing and offloads only the threads that benefit from transient execution.
  The main challenge is to checkpoint individual threads despite cross-thread dependencies. ThreadShift does so by efficiently tracking memory dependencies, identifying per-thread memory writes, and maintaining coherence across machines. Three novel uses of Memory Protection Keys (MPKs) make these mechanisms efficient, enabling fast incremental checkpoints without pausing the entire application.
  We implement ThreadShift on x86-64 Linux and evaluate it on workloads including machine-learning inference, cryptographic tasks, and in-memory data processing. ThreadShift offloads threads in as little as 164 us, performs checkpointing up to three orders of magnitude faster than CRIU, and reduces deployment cost by up to 56% on commodity clouds.

</details>


### [85] [DPS: Dual-Mode Precision LLM Serving with Semi-Unified Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34380)
*Xuan Truong Nguyen,Tien Son Pham,Tuan Duc Chu,Wookeun Jung,Thanh Tuan Dao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出DPS系统，利用双精度模型表示，在KV缓存压力时动态切换精度并重用权重内存作为KV缓存，以提升吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务系统将权重视为固定内存，而多精度模型允许单一存储模型支持全精度和低精度执行，使得权重内存占用运行时可变。在突发负载下，KV缓存需求波动影响吞吐和SLO，因此需要将权重内存变为弹性资源。

Method: DPS基于半统一内存(SUM)，将权重区域划分为持久低精度子区域和共享区域，共享区域在残差权重张量和KV缓存块之间交替。在正常负载下使用全精度模型，在KV压力时切换到嵌套低精度变体，并将空闲权重内存用作KV缓存。系统在vLLM上实现。

Result: 在密集和MoE模型及生产负载跟踪上的实验表明，相比静态FP16，持续吞吐量提升2.1-3.3倍，有效pass@1提升最多41个百分点，同时保持FP16级精度。

Conclusion: DPS通过弹性利用权重内存，显著提升LLM服务性能，同时保持精度，为应对突发负载提供有效方案。

Abstract: Existing LLM serving systems virtualize and optimize KV-cache memory, but treat model-weight memory as fixed throughout execution. Recent work on multi-precision model representations challenges this design by allowing a single stored model to support both full-accuracy and lower-precision execution, making the effective weight footprint runtime-dependent. This creates an opportunity under bursty workloads, where temporary spikes in KV-cache demand often determine throughput and SLO compliance. We present DPS, a dual-precision LLM serving system that turns weight memory into an elastic resource: under normal load, DPS serves the full-accuracy model; under KV pressure, it switches to a nested, lower-precision variant and repurposes unused weight memory for KV cache blocks. DPS is built on Semi-Unified Memory (SUM), which partitions the weight region into a persistent lower-precision sub-region and a shared region that alternates between residual weight tensors and KV-cache blocks, preserving compatibility with paged KV-cache management. We implement DPS on top of vLLM and evaluate it across both dense and MoE models and various production workload traces. Our results show that \sysname improves sustained throughput by $2.1$--$3.3\times$ and effective pass@1 by up to $+41$\,pp over Static FP16, while preserving FP16-class accuracy.

</details>


### [86] [Accelerator Choice Is Not Enough: AlphaFold2 Inference on Cloud TPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34818)
*Lorenzo Pazienza,Ihab El Bani*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: AlphaFold2 在 JAX 中可跨 CPU、GPU、TPU 无缝运行，但在 Cloud TPU v5e 上实际性能受软件层决策显著影响：默认仅使用单芯片、vmap 批处理无增益、pmap 跨芯片映射才能获得 6.5-7.9 倍吞吐，且首次调用 75% 时间耗于编译。硬件优势（TPU 单芯 0.47s vs T4 13.1s）仅针对特定测试活动，五周后复现失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究 AlphaFold2 在 JAX 可移植性下，不同硬件加速器的实际推理性能差异，以及软件执行路径、批处理、分片策略对硬件利用率和成本效益的影响。

Method: 在 Colab CPU、NVIDIA T4 GPU、8 芯片 Cloud TPU v5e 切片上运行同一 AlphaFold2 推理负载；对比默认单芯片执行、jax.vmap 批处理、jax.pmap 跨芯片映射、自动分片的吞吐与占用；分析首次调用的追踪编译开销；五周后重跑基线验证可复现性。

Result: TPU 单芯稳态 0.47s/次，T4 13.1s/次（单次测试活动）。默认仅用 1/8 芯片，闲置导致切片成本每预测约等于 GPU。vmap 从未超越单查询吞吐；pmap 使 8 芯片达单芯 6.5-7.9 倍，自动分片未减少单芯占用（推测为复制因无分片注解）。首次调用 75% 时间在 JAX 追踪编译。五周后复现失败，GPU 基线偏差约 2 倍，硬件比率仅限该活动。

Conclusion: 硬件原始性能优势被软件层默认配置、批处理机制、分片缺失大幅抵消；用户需显式使用 pmap 等并行原语才能发挥 TPU 多芯片优势。首次编译开销大，且云环境性能随时间漂移，基准测试需谨慎解读。

Abstract: AlphaFold2 is written in JAX, so the same inference code compiles and runs unchanged on CPUs, GPUs and Google Cloud TPUs. That portability makes the accelerator look like the main decision a user has to make. We show that it is not. Running one AlphaFold2 inference workload across a Colab CPU runtime, an NVIDIA T4 GPU and a dedicated eight-chip Cloud TPU v5e slice, we find a large hardware advantage for the TPU, 0.47 s per call in steady state on a single chip against 13.1 s on the T4 in the same measurement campaign, and three ways in which the software layer decides how much of it a user actually gets. The default execution path uses one chip of the eight, and at list prices the idle capacity makes the slice cost about as much per prediction as the GPU. Batching with jax.vmap never exceeds single-query throughput, while mapping queries across chips with jax.pmap gives eight chips 6.5-7.9x the throughput of one on a matched grid; automatic sharding leaves the per-chip footprint unchanged, consistent with replication, most plausibly because AlphaFold2 carries no sharding annotations. Our retained trace analysis of a first call at a new input shape reports about three quarters of the traced span in JAX tracing and compilation rather than execution. Reruns five weeks later reproduced neither cloud baseline, the GPU one off by roughly a factor of two, so the hardware ratio above is specific to one campaign.

</details>


### [87] [No-Restart Elasticity in an Adaptive Runtime System for Cloud-Native HPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32217)
*Aditya Bhosale,Laxmikant Kale*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一种 Charm++ 运行时的无重启伸缩机制，该机制使进程能够在中断时保持存活，从而将伸缩开销从多秒降至毫秒级，并在 GPU 上显著降低了峰值中断的开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的弹性机制会完全销毁进程并冷重启，导致重启阶段成为总开销的最大部分（>95%），对于 GPU 来说由于 CUDA 上下文初始化而更加严重。此外，GPU 弹性还需要检查点守护程序，而受监督的进程管理器则与突发实例中断不兼容。

Method: 我们引入了一种无重启的伸缩方法：一个轻量级的外部协调器进行成员资格协商，协调 UCX 通信端点以适应新的集群视图，并返回运行时初始化路径的顶部以保留活动状态。进程的生存保证了 GPU 设备状态的持久性，启动机制独立于启动器，从而消除了对检查点守护程序和受监督进程管理器的依赖。

Result: 在 CPU 和 GPU 上，单个伸缩操作的开销从数秒降至 8‑15 ms 和 7‑11 ms（4‑ 32 个实例）；与现有的突发实例管理框架集成后，对于八次同时中断的端到端开销，CPU 和 GPU 分别降至 0.2% 和 0.6%，在 1% 开销以下的情况下，伸缩频率提高了 7 倍。

Conclusion: 无重启伸缩机制显著降低了基于突发实例的高性能计算的开销，为大规模使用 GPU 的工作负载提供了高效且可扩展的弹性处理能力。

Abstract: Exploiting discounted spot instances for HPC requires an application to change its resource allocation at runtime, shrinking ahead of an interruption and expanding onto replacement capacity. Existing elasticity mechanisms implement rescaling as a full process teardown followed by a cold restart at the new processor count, and the restart stage accounts for up to 95% of the total overhead, growing with node count and increasing substantially on GPUs due to CUDA context initialization. In this paper, we present a no-restart rescaling mechanism for the Charm++ runtime system in which surviving processes never exit: a rescaling operation consists of a membership negotiation with a lightweight external coordinator, reconciliation of the UCX communication endpoints with the new cluster view, and a return to the top of the runtime initialization path that preserves live application state. This reduces the cost of a rescaling operation, excluding the load balancing step that any rescaling model requires, from multiple seconds to 8--15ms on CPUs and 7--11ms on GPUs, at 4 to 32 instances. Because processes survive, GPU device state persists in place, eliminating the checkpointing daemons previously required for GPU elasticity, and a launcher-independent bootstrap mechanism removes the dependence on supervised process managers, which are incompatible with spot instance interruptions. Integrated with an existing spot instance management framework, the mechanism cuts the end-to-end overhead of eight simultaneous interruptions to 0.2% of runtime on CPUs and 0.6% on GPUs, and raises the rate at which a job can be rescaled below 1% overhead by a factor of seven.

</details>


### [88] [RR-Evict: Fine-Grained Prefix Cache Eviction beyond LRU for Agentic LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32278)
*Zaifeng Pan,Chris Wu,Zhengding Hu,Xinwei Qiang,Zhongkai Yu,Yufei Ding*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 文章介绍了一种名为 RR-EVICT 的细粒度前缀缓存驱逐策略，解决 LLM 代理在长期任务中因 LRU 驱逐导致某些代理的上下文完全丢失，造成严重的 TTFT 异常问题。通过轮流驱逐空闲代理的尾部缓存，RR-EVICT 能够更均衡地分配缓存回收，降低代理返回时的重计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 代理在执行长任务时，通过前缀缓存来避免重复预填充整个上下文。随着上下文长度和并发量的增加，缓存总量不断增长，需要进行驱逐以释放空间。传统的 LRU 策略存在“近期同步”问题，导致 eviction 集中在少数代理的私有历史，造成一些代理完全丢失缓存，重计算开销大，TTFT 严重变差。

Method: 提出 RR-EVICT 策略：按照轮转顺序遍历空闲代理，从每个代理驱逐一个尾部块，保留可重用的前缀。当容量仍有剩余时，优先保留空闲状态的代理的前缀。这样，即使完全 evicted 的代理返回，也只需要重计算较小的缺失后缀部分。RR-EVICT 无需预测未来到达或工具延迟。

Result: 在 SGLang 中实现并评估了对话和代码代理工作负载，涵盖了协同放置和预填充-解码分离的服务方式。与 LRU 对比，RR-EVICT 最多可将 P99 TTFT 降低 75.4%，最多可将 P99 未缓存提示令牌数降低 65.7%。

Conclusion: RR-EVICT 提供了一种有效且实用的缓存驱逐策略，在 LLM 代理场景中显著改善了延迟和缓存利用率，为服务系统提供了更平滑的性能表现。

Abstract: LLM-based agents execute long-horizon tasks through repeated model calls interleaved with tool execution and user interaction. As each call extends the history accumulated in previous turns, prefix caching avoids repeated prefill of the agent's entire context. However, the aggregate cache footprint grows with context length and concurrency, forcing serving systems to reclaim cached KV tensors. We identify recency synchronization: accesses to an agent's cached history refresh its cache nodes together, causing least recently used (LRU) eviction to concentrate on a few agents' private histories. When a fully evicted agent returns, it must recompute nearly its entire accumulated context, producing a large time-to-first-token (TTFT) outlier even if most other requests retain substantial cache reuse. We present RR-EVICT, a fine-grained prefix-cache eviction strategy that distributes reclamation across idle agent trajectories. RR-EVICT visits agents in round-robin order and evicts a tail chunk from each, preserving reusable prefixes for more agents when capacity remains for idle state. Returning agents can reuse these partial histories and recompute smaller missing suffixes. The policy requires no prediction of future arrivals or tool latency. We implement RR-EVICT in SGLang and evaluate conversational and coding-agent workloads under colocated and prefill-decode-disaggregated serving. Compared with LRU, RR-EVICT reduces P99 TTFT by up to 75.4% and P99 uncached prompt tokens by up to 65.7%.

</details>


### [89] [AgentReplay: Token-Wise Trace Replay Is Essential for Fair Serving System Performance Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32283)
*Zaifeng Pan,Michael Wang,Chris Wu,Zhengding Hu,Xinwei Qiang,Zhongkai Yu,Yufei Ding*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了 AgentReplay，一个可配置的代理服务痕迹记录与重放框架，通过记录输入/输出令牌、专家选择、请求依赖和工具时长，在重放时强制输出已记录令牌以实现公平对比，解决了因生成 token 工作负载变异导致的性能评估困难问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估 LLM 代理服务优化很困难，因为相同任务可能产生不同的执行轨迹，生成的 token 会影响后续提示、工具调用和推理轮次，使得难以区分系统改进与工作负载变异。贪婪解码不能保证输出一致，而仅重放序列长度会丢失影响前缀缓存和 MoE 路由的 token 信息。

Method: AgentReplay 记录输入/输出 token、专家选择、请求依赖和工具时长。在重放时，执行正常的自回归计算，同时强制输出已记录的 token，并可选控制 MoE 专家选择和工具延迟。这使得不同系统在执行相同记录工作负载的同时，保留各自的批处理、调度和并行化决策。将轨迹生成与性能评估分离，使得较小的兼容模型可以重放由更强模型收集的长 horizon 轨迹。

Result: 实验表明，AgentReplay 的令牌级重放有效消除了贪婪解码和序列长度重放无法避免的工作负载变异，从而在不同的服务配置之间实现了更公平的性能比较。

Conclusion: AgentReplay 通过痕迹记录与可控重放，实现了跨模型和跨配置的公平性能评估，为 LLM 代理服务优化提供了基准测试基础。

Abstract: LLM-based agents execute multi-turn workflows with interleaved model inference and tool calls, making efficient serving increasingly important. However, evaluating serving optimizations is challenging because identical tasks can produce different execution trajectories. Changes in generated tokens can alter subsequent prompts, tool calls, and reasoning turns, making it difficult to distinguish system improvements from workload variation. Greedy decoding does not guarantee identical outputs, while replaying only sequence lengths loses token information that affects prefix caching and mixture-of-experts (MoE) routing. To address these problems, we propose AgentReplay, a configurable trace record-and-replay framework for agent serving. AgentReplay records input/output tokens, expert selections, request dependencies, and tool durations. During replay, it forces the recorded output tokens while performing normal autoregressive computation, with optional controls for MoE expert selection and tool delays. This allows different systems to execute the same recorded workload while retaining their own batching, scheduling, and parallelization decisions. We further separate trajectory generation from performance evaluation, enabling compatible smaller models to replay long-horizon traces collected with more capable models. Our experiments show that token-wise replay in AgentReplay effectively eliminates workload variation that greedy decoding and length-wise replay cannot avoid, enabling fairer performance comparisons across serving configurations.

</details>


### [90] [ASCEND: Personal AI Agents for Autonomous Scientific Computing Across HPC Clusters and GPU Workstations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32868)
*J. Paul Liu,Uthpala Herath,Andrew Petersen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ASCEND，一种在研究人员本地笔记本电脑上运行的AI代理接口，用于自主进行科学计算。它通过本地安全连接和远程托管的语言模型，自动管理作业提交、故障诊断与修复、代码并行化及结果复现，无需复杂的设施级服务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统科学计算需要研究人员手动配置环境、提交作业，并从复杂的调度器状态和日志中诊断故障，效率低下。ASCEND旨在利用AI代理将研究人员从这些繁琐的日常计算管理任务中解放出来。

Method: ASCEND在用户本地笔记本电脑上运行，通过多路复用认证连接访问Slurm集群和GPU工作站。语言模型远程托管且不持有凭据，特定站点的执行策略由本地工具检查。用户只需提供各资源账户即可部署使用。

Result: 展示了四个实际案例：(1) 自动闭环修复了张量设备故障；(2) 复现了天气预报模型评估并发现了原论文的数据不一致；(3) 对12,693行求解器进行位对位一致性并行化，运行时间从12小时缩短至2小时；(4) 发现并修复了求解器中的两处未定义行为。策略层测试表明其能有效拦截违规请求，但存在少量对合法请求的误拒。

Conclusion: ASCEND在人工监督下成功展示了自主处理科学计算任务（如故障恢复、复现、并行化和代码修复）的能力。尽管表现出了高自主性，端到端的恢复基准测试仍有待进一步评估。

Abstract: Traditional scientific computing requires researchers to translate computational intent into environment configuration, resource requests, and executable jobs, then diagnose failures from scheduler state and application logs. We present ASCEND (Autonomous Scientific Computing Engine and Novel Discovery), an AI-powered agent interface that runs the agent on the researcher's own laptop, reaching Slurm-managed clusters and a GPU workstation over a multiplexed authenticated connection, with site-specific execution policies checked by locally executed tools; the language model is hosted remotely and holds no credentials. No facility-scale service is required: an account on each resource is sufficient, and the public installer lets users link additional Slurm clusters or workstations of their own. We report four recorded cases: (1) the agent closed a failure-recovery loop on a planted tensor-device fault, submitting, diagnosing, repairing and resubmitting with job-level artifacts preserved; (2) it reproduced the published evaluation of a weather-forecasting model from the author's released forecasts, agreeing with the published curves to 2.1% (z500) and 2.4% (t850) while identifying a unit discrepancy in the paper's prose and an initialization-field discrepancy in its released data; (3) it parallelized a released 12,693-line geophysical solver under a bit-for-bit identity requirement, reducing wall-clock runtime from about twelve hours to about two; (4) that requirement exposed two instances of undefined behaviour in the published solver, both repaired and reported upstream. Separately, a pre-specified evaluation of the policy layer found the deployed validator rejected 29 of 30 constructed violations and held the remaining one for approval, while denying 3 of 14 legitimate requests. Autonomy was exercised under author supervision; an end-to-end recovery benchmark remains outstanding.

</details>


### [91] [Time Semantics and Liveness Artifacts in Adversarial Consensus Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32972)
*Nasit S Sony,Xianzhong Ding*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了对抗性共识模拟器中时间模型与调度器活动的耦合对协议行为的影响，通过对比EventCoupled与RoundTick两种定时机制，发现耦合时间模型会因调度活动推进故障检测器状态，导致领导不稳定率随消息延迟增加而急剧上升，而解耦机制则能保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示在超时敏感的共识协议中，时间模型与调度策略的相互作用如何引发非预期行为，以及解耦时间推进对系统稳定性的影响。

Method: 在VeriProtocol中设计受控的Multi-Paxos和Raft实验，比较EventCoupled（每次调度机会推进协议时间）与RoundTick（将调度事件与超时推进解耦）两种定时模型；通过改变消息延迟预算K，观察领导不稳定率；结合匹配轨迹分析和心跳延迟控制实验。

Result: 当N=5，K从6增至14时，EventCoupled的领导不稳定率从5%升至100%，而RoundTick在140次执行中未产生额外有效领导选举；K=0时两者均无额外选举。匹配轨迹显示EventCoupled中调度活动直接推进故障检测器状态，RoundTick中则不直接对应协议时间推进。心跳延迟控制证实RoundTick并未抑制超时驱动的选举。

Conclusion: 时间模型与调度器的耦合会显著影响共识协议的稳定性，解耦调度事件与协议时间推进（如RoundTick）可避免因调度活动导致的非预期故障检测器过期，从而提高模拟保真度与协议可靠性。

Abstract: Adversarial consensus simulators require not only a model of message delivery but also a model of time. For timeout-sensitive protocols, coupling protocol-time progression to scheduler activity can allow communication scheduling to influence when failure detectors expire.
  We study this interaction in VeriProtocol using controlled Multi-Paxos and Raft experiments. In the primary Multi-Paxos study, we compare EventCoupled timing, where every scheduler opportunity advances protocol time, with RoundTick timing, which decouples individual scheduler events from timeout progression. At N=5, increasing the bounded message-delay budget from K=6 to K=14 increased the observed EventCoupled leadership-instability rate from 5\% to 100\% across the tested grid, while no additional valid leader elections occurred in any of the corresponding 140 RoundTick executions. At K=0, neither model produced additional elections.
  Matched-trace analysis identifies the mechanism: under EventCoupled timing, scheduler activity used to perturb communication also directly advances failure-detector state. Under RoundTick, comparable scheduler activity does not imply equivalent protocol-time progression. A targeted heartbeat-delay control confirms that RoundTick does not suppress timeout-driven elections; an additional election remains reachable when leader contact is unavailable across sufficient logical time.

</details>


### [92] [Simple and Fast Signature-Free Blockchain Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32985)
*Giuliano Losa,Xuechao Wang,Zhuolun Xiang,Qianyu Yu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出两种无签名区块链共识协议，针对 eventual synchrony 实现最优良-case 提交延迟（f<n/3 为 3 消息延迟，f<n/5 为 2），无推测情况下区块时间仅 2 消息延迟，每轮 O(n²) 通信，并引入 view agreement 抽象参数化 Generic Simplex 构造，该构造同时捕获 Simplex、Minimmit 及新的同步无签名协议（f<n/4，2 消息延迟）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 避免后量子签名的计算与存储开销，设计高效的无签名区块链共识协议。

Method: 提出两种针对 eventual synchrony 的简单无签名共识协议，引入 view agreement 抽象并参数化 Generic Simplex 构造，分析其延迟、响应性和通信复杂度。

Result: 两种协议分别实现 f<n/3 时 3 轮和 f<n/5 时 2 轮的良-case 提交延迟，无推测即区块时间 2 消息延迟，每轮 O(n²) 通信。同一构造也捕获了 Simplex、Minimmit 以及新的同步无签名协议（f<n/4，2 轮延迟）。

Conclusion: 提供了一种灵活高效的无签名共识框架，兼顾量子安全性和高效率，扩展了现有共识协议的边界。

Abstract: Signature-free protocols avoid the cost of post-quantum signatures. We present two simple signature-free blockchain consensus protocols for eventual synchrony with optimal good-case commit latency (three message delays for \(f<n/3\) and two for \(f<n/5\)), optimistic responsiveness, a block time of only two message delays without speculation, and \(O(n^2)\) communication per view. They instantiate Generic Simplex, a blockchain consensus construction parameterized by a new abstraction called view agreement. The same construction also captures Simplex, Minimmit, and a new synchronous signature-free protocol with optimal good-case commit latency of two message delays for \(f<n/4\).

</details>


### [93] [Tessera: Demand-Driven KV Cache Management for Retrieval-Augmented LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32999)
*Fei Fang,Chung-Hsiang Lo,Yi Liu,Yifan Hua,Chen Qian*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出一种名为Tessera的分离式服务系统，将检索作为KV状态重用的控制平面，解决因重复记录跨越前缀而导致的缓存不可用问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在RAG和基于检索的Agent记忆中，检索内容会重复出现在不同请求的不同位置，导致超过70%的注入内存token无法通过传统前缀缓存重用，因而需要更灵活的KV重用管理。

Method: Tessera通过在模型执行前暴露所需的上下文单元，让检索系统根据当前需求、历史检索、KV驻留情况和生成负载进行统一的缓存管理和请求路由；生成节点并行准备本地缓存、远程缓存和缺失的KV状态，并在请求的非关键路径上保留新计算的状态。

Result: 在RAG和Agent记忆工作负载上，Tessera相较于SGLang和带有EPIC的LMCache基线，使平均TTFT（首 token 延迟）降低最高达3.6倍，且在基线饱和的请求速率下仍能保持低TTFT，同时保持与底层组合策略相同的答案质量。

Conclusion: 通过将检索提升为控制平面并采用分离式缓存协调，Tessera有效解决了跨前缀重复记录的KV重用难题，显著提升了检索增强生成系统的响应速度和可扩展性。

Abstract: RAG and retrieval-based agent memory both inject retrieved content into LLM prompts, as document chunks and recalled memory records, respectively. The same content can recur across requests at different prompt positions or after different preceding contexts, preventing reuse through conventional prefix caching. Our characterization finds that records recurring outside the matching prefix account for over 70% of injected memory tokens in agent-memory workloads. Composable KV-reuse methods enable reuse in such cases, but online serving introduces a management problem: a recurring unit's KV states may not yet exist, may have been evicted, or may reside on another node. We present Tessera, a disaggregated serving system that makes retrieval the control plane for KV reuse. By exposing the context units needed before model execution, retrieval allows Tessera to combine current demand with retrieval history, KV residency, and generation load to coordinate cache management and request routing. Generation nodes concurrently prepare locally cached, remotely cached, and missing states, while retaining newly computed states off the request's critical path. Across RAG and agent-memory workloads, Tessera lowers mean TTFT by up to 3.6x over SGLang and LMCache with EPIC at matched request rates, and sustains low TTFT at rates where the baselines saturate, while matching the answer quality of the underlying composition policy.

</details>


### [94] [Hierarchical Secure Distributed Linearly Separable Computation with Arbitrary Heterogeneous Data Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33092)
*Ziting Zhang,Chenyi Sun,Kai Wan,Xiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种在三层分层网络中进行安全分布式线性可分计算的方法，支持用户降级和节点协同攻击，同时实现了最优通信率或近最优通信率的分布式梯度编码方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究需要解决在分布式计算场景中如何确保服务器和中间节点（如路由器）不泄露用户隐私，同时用户数据分配可能存在异构性，且系统需容忍用户故障和恶意协同攻击。传统方法难以同时满足这些安全约束和通信效率要求。

Method: 采用三层架构（用户→中继→中心服务器），通过加密消息传递和安全聚合技术实现隐私保护。对于K_c=1的情况退化为分布式梯度编码；针对多维线性可分任务扩展到无用户丢失设置。引入安全约束：服务器仅学习目标任务函数，无额外输入信息；每个中继不获取用户输入信息。支持用户降级和节点协同攻击。

Result: 证明了在特定条件下可达到两层通信率的最优性，在另一条件下获得接近最优（因子2内）的通信率。对于K_c>1情况，成功将构造扩展至多维线性可分任务，在无用户丢失设置下实现了有效的安全分布式计算。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种在复杂网络环境中兼顾安全性、隐私保护与通信效率的理论框架，为分布式机器学习中的隐私保护计算提供了新的方法论基础。

Abstract: This paper studies secure distributed linearly separable computation over a three-layer hierarchical network, where clustered users communicate with a central server through relays. The server aims to recover Kc linear combinations of K intermediate outcomes, where each intermediate outcome is a separable function of one dataset. We consider a more general setting with arbitrary heterogeneous data assignment across users, where ''arbitrary'' means that the data assignment is given in advance (which can be in any form) and ''heterogeneous'' means that the users may hold different numbers of datasets. Under this assignment, each user computes the intermediate outcomes of its assigned datasets and sends masked messages to its associated relay. The relays subsequently process and forward the received messages to the server. We impose two security constraints: (i) security against server, requiring the server to learn only the desired task function without gaining any additional information about users' inputs; and (ii) security against relays, ensuring each relay learns nothing about users' inputs. Moreover, the server or any relay may collude with a subset of users. For Kc=1, the underlying computation reduces to distributed gradient coding. We propose a secure scheme tolerating user dropouts and user collusion, achieving the optimal two-layer communication rates in one regime and order-optimal communication rates within a factor of 2 in the other regime. For Kc>1, we extend the proposed construction to multi-dimensional linearly separable tasks under the no-dropout setting.

</details>


### [95] [VarioPath: Workload-Aware All-to-All Communication for PCIe GPU Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34340)
*Yao Fei,Jin Fang,Size Zheng,Gongming Zhao,Hongli Xu,Jiacheng Zhu,Zhijing Xin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: VarioPath is a framework for efficient AlltoAllv communication on PCIe GPU systems, combining offline topology-aware analysis with online demand-aware scheduling to reduce contention and improve performance.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AlltoAllv is critical for distributed MoE model inference, but PCIe GPU systems lack dedicated high-speed interconnects, leading to contention between intra-node and inter-node traffic over shared PCIe links, especially under skewed and dynamic traffic demands.

Method: VarioPath uses an offline topology-aware analyzer to record contention-free transfer patterns as channels, leveraging topology symmetry to build a compact catalog. An online demand-aware scheduler then decomposes AlltoAllv demands across these channels, adapting to dynamic and skewed traffic with low planning overhead.

Result: Evaluated on four platforms up to 256 GPUs, VarioPath achieves average AlltoAllv speedups of 5.88x over FAST and 1.72x over DeepEP. It reduces Qwen3 inference latency by up to 27.2% and Wan2.1 generation latency by 6.1%.

Conclusion: VarioPath effectively addresses PCIe link contention in AlltoAllv operations for large-scale MoE model inference, offering significant performance improvements over existing approaches while maintaining practicality through low scheduling overhead.

Abstract: AlltoAllv communication is a critical primitive in distributed large-model inference, particularly for mixture-of-experts (MoE) models. The growing adoption of PCIe GPU systems for cost-efficient inference makes AlltoAllv performance on these systems increasingly important. Without a dedicated scale-up interconnect (e.g., NVLink or Infinity Fabric), PCIe GPU systems carry both intra-node and inter-node traffic through the PCIe hierarchy, where concurrent transfers can contend for PCIe link bandwidth. This link contention, compounded by skewed traffic distributions and dynamic traffic demand, makes efficient AlltoAllv scheduling challenging. Existing approaches are either poorly suited to PCIe GPU systems or incur substantial schedule synthesis overhead that reduces their practicality in real-world deployments.
  We present VarioPath, an efficient AlltoAllv scheduling framework for PCIe GPU systems. It combines an offline topology-aware analyzer with an online demand-aware scheduler.
