The analyzer records contention-free transfer patterns as AlltoAllv channels and exploits topology symmetry to build a compact catalog for efficient search. The online scheduler decomposes each AlltoAllv invocation's demand across a sequence of channels, adapting to rapidly changing and skewed traffic while incurring low planning overhead. Evaluation on four platforms (up to 256 GPUs) shows average AlltoAllv speedups of 5.88x over FAST and 1.72x over DeepEP. End-to-end experiments show that VarioPath reduces Qwen3 inference latency by up to 27.2% and Wan2.1 generation latency by 6.1%.

</details>


### [96] [Splitting Prompt Prefill from Response Replay for Context-Parallel Long-Context LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33133)
*Yubing Bao,Zhihui Lu,Qiang Duan,Yuedong Xu,Sen Liu,Pan Zhou*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出一种名为AugTree的上下文并行（CP）执行方案，针对长上下文LLM策略在强化学习更新阶段的特殊通信模式进行优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在RL更新阶段，同一长提示会被复用到多个响应分支，标准CP会导致提示的KV状态在每个分支上重复计算或旋转，造成巨大的计算和通信开销。需要一种能够复用共享提示KV状态并减少重复工作的方法。

Method: 将更新阶段分为两个阶段：(1)提示预填阶段，一次性计算共享的提示KV状态；(2)响应重放阶段，使用有限数量的重放车道安排独立的响应分支。通过轻量级在线规划器根据提示或响应的主导程度，选择两种通信语义：在响应主导时在局部车道内旋转KV分片；在提示主导时将响应查询发送至持有静止提示KV的节点并进行部分softmax归约。整个过程保持完全可导，梯度能够回传到原始预填图中。

Result: 在四个真实的后训练工作负载且最多使用64个加速卡的实验中，AugTree相对于动态CP平均提升1.18倍（最高达2.23倍），相对于带提示复用的Megatron ring CP基线提升2.63倍，相对于不复用的基线提升7.08倍。

Conclusion: AugTree通过在RL更新阶段复用共享提示KV状态并灵活调度通信方式，显著降低了训练阶段的步骤时间，同时保持了精确的训练语义，是后训练长上下文LLM的高效并行方案。

Abstract: Training long-context LLM policies with RL requires re-evaluating groups of sampled responses under the updated policy, an update-stage attention workload that differs sharply from pre-training: each group shares one long prompt that fans out into multiple response branches. Standard context parallelism (CP) flattens each prompt--response pair into a linear sequence, so the same prompt key--value (KV) states are recomputed---or repeatedly rotated through the network---once per response branch. We present \textbf{AugTree}, a CP execution scheme built around this replay stage. AugTree separates the replay into two phases: a prompt-prefill phase that computes the shared prompt KV state once, and a response-replay phase that schedules the independent response branches over a bounded set of replay lanes. The replay phase instantiates two communication semantics, chosen by a lightweight online planner that enumerates CP degrees, schedules, and placements before GPU dispatch: rotating KV shards within response-local lanes when responses dominate, and moving response queries to stationary prompt-KV owners with a partial-softmax reduction when prompts dominate. The shared prompt state remains fully differentiable---response losses backpropagate into it and the accumulated prompt gradients propagate through the original prefill graph---so AugTree preserves exact training semantics rather than performing detached, inference-style KV caching. On four real post-training workloads and up to 64 accelerators, AugTree improves average training-stage step time by 1.18$\times$ over dynamic CP (up to 2.23$\times$), 2.63$\times$ over a Megatron ring CP baseline with prompt reuse, and 7.08$\times$ over the baseline without reuse.

</details>


### [97] [Weaver: A System for AI-RAN Compute Sharing with Foundation Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35276)
*Leyang Xue,Tianxin Wang,Xin Zhe Khooi,Jiaxun Yang,Dheeraj Mahendiran,Yufeng Xia,Mun Choon Chan,Myungjin Lee,Mahesh K. Marina*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 论文提出 Weaver 系统，利用 AI-RAN 基站闲置 GPU 算力进行去中心化基础模型训练，实现 RAN 优先调度与弹性训练框架，显著提升算力利用率与训练吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI-RAN 基站部署 GPU 加速硬件后，40%-85% GPU 算力闲置且呈时域突发、空域互补特性，亟需一种不影响 RAN 低延迟业务的机制来回收利用这些算力进行基础模型训练。

Method: 1. 设计 RAN-first 的空闲算力控制器，集成到 MAC 调度器中，通过算力感知调度平滑 RAN GPU 需求，释放更多可用闲置算力。2. 构建两级弹性训练框架，适应站内时隙级和站间宏观的动态异构闲置算力。

Result: 在 O-RAN 标准系统原型上，Weaver 创造的可用闲置算力提升至 4.9 倍，利用率达 83%；多站点测试床中训练吞吐比基线提升 2.1-3.7 倍，且不降低 RAN 性能。

Conclusion: Weaver 验证了在 AI-RAN 基础设施上安全高效地共置去中心化基础模型训练的可行性，为未来 6G 网络中的算力共享与 AI 原生网络提供了关键系统支撑。

Abstract: The emergence of AI-RAN infrastructure, which equips cell sites with GPU-accelerated hardware, creates an opportunity to colocate non-RAN workloads with primary RAN processing. We explore using this spare capacity for decentralized training of foundation models (FMs), one of the most compute-intensive AI workloads. We present the first characterization of spare GPU capacity in AI-RAN systems at both micro-scale--across transmission slots within a cell site--and macro-scale--across sites. Our analysis finds that 40-85% of GPU capacity is unused; although this capacity is temporally bursty at individual sites, it is spatially complementary across sites. To safely and efficiently harness these resources, we present Weaver, a system that opportunistically trains FMs alongside latency-critical RAN workloads without degrading RAN performance. Weaver adopts a RAN-first design: a spare-compute controller integrated into the MAC scheduler uses compute-aware scheduling to smooth RAN GPU demand and exposes more usable spare GPU capacity. A two-level elastic training framework then adapts to dynamic, heterogeneous spare capacity within and across sites. Experiments on an O-RAN-aligned system prototype show that Weaver creates up to 4.9x more usable spare compute and utilizes up to 83% of the available spare capacity. On a multi-site testbed, Weaver improves training throughput by 2.1-3.7x over baseline approaches.

</details>


### [98] [MpFA: Hardware-Efficient Train-Free QK4V8 FlashAttention Kernels on Blackwell GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33135)
*Chencheng Deng,Jianbin Fang,Dezun Dong*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为 MpFA 的无需训练的 Blackwell 优化 FlashAttention 内核，通过在 QK 中使用 NVFP4 和 PV 中使用 FP8 相结合，以及使用额外的 Tensor Core MMA 实现秩为一的平滑补偿，以减少完全 FP4 注意力中的非矩阵乘法开销。在 B200 上的长上下文 (16K 128K) 测试中，MpFA 显著提升了预填充吞吐量，并在 Llama-3.1-8B 和 Qwen3-14B 上的端到端输出吞吐量上比 BF16 FA4 快 2.81×，秩为一补偿恢复了约 62.5% 的精度损失，内核开销仅增加约 2.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 GPU 服务堆栈在长上下文 LLM 推断时受限于注意力机制，其中 softmax-GEMM 管道主导了计算和内存。由于在线量化、内存数据移动和 softmax 路径竞争等非矩阵乘法开销，完全 FP4 注意力无法有效地利用 Blackwell GPU 上的高 FP4 Tensor Core 吞吐量。通过混合精度、秩为一补偿和优化流水线来解决这些性能和精度损失。

Method: MpFA 采用以下硬件优化技术：1) 针对 Blackwell 的混合精度计算——QK 使用低比特 NVFP4，PV 使用 FP8，以避免 FP4 PV 转换开销；2) 使用额外的 Tensor Core MMA 实现秩为一的平滑补偿，以恢复精度；3) 采用细粒度异步流水线设计，实现张量-内存重用和自适应并行分区（适用于预填充和解码阶段）。内核无需重新训练。

Result: 在 B200 上进行的实验表明：MpFA 改善了 16K 128K 上下文下的预填充吞吐量，超过了现有的 BF16/FP8 基准；在 Llama-3.1-8B 和 Qwen3-14B 上的端到端输出吞吐量，比 BF16 FA4 快 2.81×；在五个基准测试套件和两个模型上进行评估时，秩为一补偿恢复了约 62.5% 的精度损失，内核开销仅增加约 2.0%。

Conclusion: MpFA 是一种高效的训练-free FlashAttention 内核，充分利用了 Blackwell GPU 上的 FP4 和 FP8 算力，并通过硬件优化的混合精度和秩为一补偿策略，在长上下文 LLM 推断中实现了显著的吞吐量提升和精度保持。

Abstract: Long-context LLM inference pushes modern GPU serving stacks into an attention-bound regime, where both compute and memory are dominated by the softmax-GEMM pipeline. On NVIDIA Blackwell GPUs, FP4 Tensor Cores offer high matmul throughput, but we find that fully FP4 attention often fails to translate this throughput into end-to-end speedups due to non-matmul costs: online quantization after softmax, tensor/shared-memory data movement, and contention on the softmax path. We present MpFA, a training-free FlashAttention kernel optimized for Blackwell. Guided by hardware characterization, MpFA uses mixed precision: NVFP4 for QK and FP8 for PV (QK4PV8). This preserves low-bit QK throughput while avoiding the conversion and scaling overheads of FP4 PV. To recover accuracy without further stressing the softmax pipeline, MpFA introduces rank-one smoothing compensation implemented as an additional Tensor Core MMA. MpFA further improves performance with a fine-grained asynchronous pipeline, tensor-memory reuse, and adaptive parallel partitioning across prefill and decode. On an NVIDIA B200 and across 16K-128K contexts, MpFA improves prefill throughput over state-of-the-art BF16/FP8 baselines and increases end-to-end output throughput by 2.81$\times$ over BF16 FA4 across Llama-3.1-8B and Qwen3-14B. Across five benchmark suites and two models, rank-one compensation recovers 62.5% of the accuracy loss with about 2.0% kernel overhead.

</details>


### [99] [PackServe: SLO-Aware Request Scheduling for Agentic LLM Serving at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33224)
*Zhiyuan Tan,Dejiang Zhu,Jingzhe Jiang,Yihao Zheng,Yang Tian,Tao Wang,Minchen Yu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文提出了 PackServe 调度器，旨在降低大规模 LLM 服务中的资源成本并满足延迟 SLO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有调度器在合并请求时往往牺牲缓存局部性，导致预填充/解码干扰，难以同时满足 KVC 复用、延迟目标和资源效率。

Method: PackServe 使用紧凑的白盒模型预测请求间的预填充/解码干扰延迟，并基于预测将请求打包到更少的 GPU 实例，同时保留 KVC 复用并满足 SLO 约束。

Result: 在 64 块 NVIDIA H20 GPU 上的实验表明，PackServe 相比最新调度器在 30 ms 和 50 ms TPOT 目标下分别节省最多 16.8% 和 24.6% 的 GPU 小时，且满足目标；在超过 1000 GPU 的生产集群中，资源占用降低 34.7%。

Conclusion: PackServe 能够在保证延迟 SLO 的前提下显著减少 GPU 资源消耗，适用于大规模 LLM 服务调度。

Abstract: Request scheduling is a key challenge in large-scale clusters serving agentic large language model (LLM) workloads. An effective scheduler must preserve key-value cache (KVC) reuse across long, shared prefixes, meet token-level latency service-level objectives (SLOs), and minimize GPU resource footprint. Existing schedulers struggle to reconcile these requirements: request consolidation can sacrifice cache locality and increase prefill/decode interference, compromising both SLO attainment and resource efficiency. We present PackServe, a scheduler designed to reduce resource costs while meeting latency SLOs for agentic LLM serving. PackServe uses compact white-box models to predict latency under prefill/decode interference. Guided by these predictions, it packs requests onto fewer serving instances while preserving KVC reuse and SLO constraints, trading available latency headroom for improved per-instance throughput. Evaluation on 64 NVIDIA H20 GPUs shows that PackServe uses up to 16.8% and 24.6% fewer GPU-hours than state-of-the-art schedulers under 30-ms and 50-ms TPOT targets, respectively, while meeting the target TPOT objectives. PackServe has also been deployed in our production cluster comprising over 1000 GPUs, where it reduces the resource footprint by 34.7% compared with the original production scheduler.

</details>


### [100] [CascadeEP: Asynchronous Expert Execution for MoE Prefill under Attention Imbalance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33252)
*Jin Qin,Tiancheng Hu,Shiyan Wang,Junhao Hu,Zexin Jian,Yuzheng Wang,Haoyu Li,Chunwei Xia,Ying Liu,Pixian Zhan,Di Wang,Zhongzhe Hu,Huimin Cui,Tao Xie,Chenxi Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ASYNCEP提出一种异步专家并行的MoE预填充引擎，通过提前启动专家计算、使用streamFFN批处理已就绪的令牌以及机会性专家权重获取(OEWF)来缓解因同步专家并行导致的注意力不平衡，从而提升TTFT和吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在MoE服务中，数据和专家并行(DEP)导致注意力副本在预填充阶段完成调度时间不同，但传统的同步专家并行必须等待所有副本的路由输入准备好才能开始FFN计算，这会造成注意力负载不均和额外延迟，影响首token延迟和吞吐量。

Method: ASYNCEP包含三种机制：(1)异步专家并行，允许专家在收到部分令牌时即开始计算；(2)streamFFN，将已就绪的令牌批量送入FFN以兼顾早期启动与计算效率；(3)机会性专家权重获取(OEWF)，让运算较快的副本抢先获取其他GPU上的未开始专家工作并执行。在DeepSeek‑V4‑Flash、DeepSeek‑V4‑Pro和GLM‑5.3上进行评估。

Result: 实验表明，ASYNCEP使p95时间至首 token(TTFT)最高提升1.48倍，推理吞吐量最高提升1.17倍。

Conclusion: 通过打破专家并行的同步等待，ASYNCEP有效缓解了注意力副本间的负载不均，显著降低了首token延迟并提升了整体吞吐量，验证了异步执行在MoE预填充阶段的潜力。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) serving commonly deploys data and expert parallelism (DEP): attention replicas run distinct request batches while routed experts are sharded across an expert-parallel (EP) group. During prefill, attention replicas finish dispatch at different times, but synchronous EP delays expert feed-forward network (FFN) computation until routed inputs from all replicas are ready. Request schedulers seek to balance load while reusing the key-value (KV) cache of shared prompt prefixes to avoid redundant prefill computation. These goals can conflict when a replica holding a matching prefix is already overloaded, leaving residual attention imbalance. We present ASYNCEP, a distributed execution engine for MoE prefill. ASYNCEP proposes three mechanisms. Asynchronous EP allows expert computation to start before tokens from all attention replicas are ready. streamFFN batches ready tokens to balance early execution with FFN computation efficiency. Opportunistic expert weight fetching (OEWF) allows a faster replica to fetch expert weights and execute unstarted work from other GPUs. We evaluate ASYNCEP on DeepSeek-V4-Flash, DeepSeek-V4-Pro, and GLM-5.3, and our results show that ASYNCEP achieves up to 1.48x speedup in p95 time-to-first-token (TTFT) and improves the inference throughput by up to 1.17x.

</details>


### [101] [AgentLoop: Runtime Control of Slot-closed Execution Loops for Tool-augmented LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33315)
*Wanyi Zheng,Minxian Xu,Kan Hu,Kejiang Ye,Chengzhong Xu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: AgentLoop introduces explicit runtime signals and slot‑closed control for tool‑augmented LLM agents, reducing redundant loops by tracking when all information slots are satisfied and deciding to continue, synthesize, or terminate based on bounded stability and low‑gain signals.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing LLM tool agents lack clear runtime indications of task completion, leading to unnecessary reasoning and tool calls even after the context stabilizes, causing inefficiency in token usage and service invocations.

Method: AgentLoop implements slot‑closed execution: it maintains a compact runtime state, uses model‑assisted structured verification to detect complete answers and missing evidence, and applies bounded stability and low‑gain signals across successive LLM/tool rounds to choose among Continue Invocation, Answer Synthesis, or Terminate Iteration actions.

Result: Experiments show AgentLoop cuts total token cost by up to 88.44% and reduces average service invocations by up to 76.85% compared to baselines. Ablation studies confirm the slot‑centered control path is crucial; disabling it deepens execution and lowers accuracy.

Conclusion: Explicit runtime signals and slot‑closed control enable efficient tool‑augmented agents, markedly improving resource utilization and answer quality while providing a clear mechanism to decide when further iterations are unnecessary.

Abstract: Tool-augmented large language model (LLM) agents are becoming an important execution unit in service computing, but existing agent loops still lack explicit runtime signals for assessing task completion. The challenge lies in the fact that an agent may continue reasoning or invoking services even after the runtime context has stopped changing, while evidence already collected remains unsynthesized into a complete answer, which leads to inefficiency in resource usage. To address these challenges, this paper presents AgentLoop, which provides runtime control of slot-closed execution loops for tool-augmented agents. Slot closure means that the information slots required by a request have been covered by sufficient runtime evidence, and that unresolved slots are explicitly identified before the loop stops. AgentLoop converts open-ended agent iteration into state-driven execution control: it maintains a compact runtime state, uses model-assisted structured verification to check answer completeness and missing evidence, and applies bounded stability and low-gain signals over neighboring LLM/tool rounds before selecting one of three actions: Continue Invocation, Answer Synthesis, or Terminate Iteration. Experiments show that AgentLoop reduces redundant execution and context growth, with total token cost reduced by up to 88.44% and average service invocations reduced by up to 76.85% against baselines. The ablation study further shows that the slot-centered control path plays a central role, since disabling it increases execution depth and substantially reduces accuracy. Overall, the results suggest that efficient tool-augmented agents can benefit from explicit runtime signals for deciding when further LLM/tool iterations no longer add useful context or supported evidence.

</details>


### [102] [OLED-MoE: Accelerating MoE-Based dLLM Inference via Inter-Iteration Locality-Aware Expert Offloading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33385)
*Jingyuan Xiao,Jiayue Wang,Yitao Hu,Xinning Wang,Shi Chen,Ziqi Gong,Zhengchao Wang,Guotao Yang,Sheng Chen,Keqiu Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出OLED-MoE，通过置信度引导的跨迁专家保留和CPU-GPU协同执行，减少dLLM推理中的专家加载延迟，显著降低每输出token时间并提高专家缓存利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半自回归扩散LLM在使用MoE时，专家参数占用显存过大，现有基于迭代内层级预取的卸载系统在块级路由下预取困难且误预代价高，导致退化为按需加载且延迟高。

Method: OLED-MoE将优化目标从迭代内预取转为迭代间专家保留：利用相邻去噪迭代的强路由重叠以及token置信度预测高价值专家，在GPU中保留这些专家，避免额外预取流量；对不可避免的缓存未命中，通过CPU-GPU协同执行，综合考虑动态专家计算负载和未来重用预测来调度执行。

Result: 在多种dLLM工作负载上，OLED-MoE相较于现有卸载系统将每输出token时间（TPOT）降低1.23倍至7.93倍，专家缓存利用率提升1.44倍至4.23倍；仅使用40%的专家显存即可接近全驻留性能，TPOT仅增加23%，相当于专家内存占比降低60%。

Conclusion: OLED-MoE表明，通过跨迁专家保留和协同执行可以有效缓解MoE在半自回归扩散LLM中的显存瓶颈，在大幅降低内存需求的同时保持近乎最优的推理速度，为资源受限环境下的大模型服务提供实用方案。

Abstract: Semi-autoregressive diffusion large language models (dLLMs) improve decoding parallelism through iterative block-wise denoising, but scaling them with mixture-of-experts (MoE) layers introduces a large expert parameter footprint that exceeds memory-constrained GPU capacity. Expert offloading is a natural remedy, yet existing MoE serving systems target autoregressive decoding and rely on intra-iteration layer-wise prefetching: while computing one layer, they predict and load experts for subsequent layers. Under dLLM inference, block-wise routing expands the active expert working set within each iteration, making such prefetches difficult to complete in time and costly when mispredicted. Consequently, existing prefetch-based solutions often degenerate into on-demand expert loading with high decoding latency.
  We propose OLED-MoE, an expert offloading system that shifts the optimization target from intra-iteration prefetching to inter-iteration expert retention. Its key insight is that adjacent denoising iterations exhibit strong expert routing overlap, and token confidence indicates which experts are likely to be reused. OLED-MoE uses confidence-guided inter-iteration prediction to retain high-value experts in GPU memory without introducing extra prefetch traffic. It further compensates unavoidable cache misses through CPU-GPU cooperative execution, jointly considering dynamic expert computation load and predicted future reuse. Across diverse dLLM workloads, OLED-MoE reduces time per output token (TPOT) by 1.23x-7.93x and improves expert cache utilization by 1.44x-4.23x over state-of-the-art offloading systems. Notably, OLED-MoE approaches full-residency performance while using only 40% of the expert GPU memory, incurring merely 23% higher TPOT despite a 60% reduction in expert memory footprint. OLED-MoE's source code is publicly available at https://github.com/flashserve/OLED-MoE.

</details>


### [103] [Toward System-of-Systems Integration for Composable Cloud-HPC-Edge AI Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33427)
*Sumit Rakesh,Rajkumar Saini*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出将现代 AI 平台视为系统的系统（SoS），通过接口、契约、映射、策略证据等实现跨系统可组合整合，保持各子系统独立控制与生命周期，从而实现“融合使用、联邦控制”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作仅在特定堆栈之间搭建桥梁，缺乏普适的跨系统组合理论，难以支撑云、HPC、云原生、数据、边缘等异构环境的统一 AI 平台。

Method: 构建 SoS 视角，提出成员系统与组件的边界测试、局部依赖、共享责任模型、七类集成面以及代表性跨系统工作流；并给出用于评估互操作性、治理、可观测性、故障隔离、演进和复用的证据类别与研究问题。

Result: 通过上述概念模型，能够在保持各子系统原生控制面的前提下，实现跨系统的协同工作；验证表明该框架能够指导实际平台的可组合设计与治理。

Conclusion: 将 AI 平台视为 SoS 并采用基于接口与契约的可组合整合，是实现异构基础设施融合使用、联邦控制的有效途径，为后续互操作性、治理等研究提供了明确的评估维度和开放问题。

Abstract: Modern AI platforms increasingly combine infrastructure stacks and operating models designed around different assumptions, including cloud-style service platforms, HPC workload-management systems, cloud-native orchestration, data and artifact systems, managed connectivity, observability, and edge or cyber-physical environments. Existing work demonstrates effective bridges between selected stacks, but a general way to reason about composition across independently controlled systems remains underdeveloped. We argue that such platforms can be usefully viewed as systems of systems (SoS) when independently useful systems retain their own control, management, lifecycles, policies, and failure semantics while contributing to a higher-level AI platform capability. We frame composable integration as an approach to cross-system coordination based on interfaces, contracts, mappings, references, policy context, and operational evidence, while preserving native control planes and avoiding dependence on a single topology or orchestration stack. The resulting direction is converged in use and federated in control. The paper presents a peer constituent-system view, a boundary test for distinguishing constituent systems from components, local dependencies, and independently useful systems outside the current SoS boundary, a local, shared, and scoped responsibility model, seven integration surfaces, and a representative cross-system workflow. It concludes with evidence classes and research questions for evaluating interoperability, governance, observability, fault containment, evolution, and reuse.

</details>


### [104] [Adaptive Client Clustering and Coordination for Federated Learning Workflow Management in Edge Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33544)
*Jieping Luo,Qiyue Li,Yuxuan Chen,Hang Qi,Jiaying Yin,Jingjin Wu,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: A-CoDa 是一个自适应聚类协调框架，用于管理依赖的联邦学习流，通过 LDD 聚类、FedMIX 参与机制和 DAG 调度器，减少端到端完成时间并保持准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习作为托管服务部署，需在边缘环境中协调异构客户端、非 IID 数据、波动通信延迟和先行约束任务，参与者管理对完成时间和学习稳定性至关重要。

Method: 提出 A-CoDa 框架：1) 基于标签分布散度（LDD）的贪婪平衡聚类构建统计连贯且大小感知的客户端组；2) FedMIX 不确定性感知的组内/组间参与机制，基于损失-延迟-不确定性效用排名客户端并自适应控制跨组探测；3) 依赖感知 DAG 调度器协调层级任务执行；4) 提供收敛分析，将误差下界和通信轮数与 LDD 采样错配、残余分布偏移、本地 SGD 漂移、随机方差和自适应探测预算显式关联。

Result: 在手写、可穿戴传感、产品图像和医学影像任务上实验，A-CoDa 在依赖 FL 工作流下显著降低端到端完成时间，同时保持有竞争力的准确率。

Conclusion: A-CoDa 通过自适应聚类协调、不确定性感知参与和依赖感知调度，有效解决了依赖 FL 服务流中的异构性、非 IID 数据、延迟波动和任务先行约束问题，实现了完成时间与准确性的平衡。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) is increasingly deployed as a managed learning service rather than as a set of isolated training jobs. In networked edge environments, dependent FL service flows must coordinate heterogeneous clients, non-IID data, fluctuating communication latency, and precedence-constrained tasks under service-level completion requirements. These coupled factors make participant management central to both time-totarget performance and learning stability. This paper proposes A-CoDa, an adaptive clustered coordination framework for managing dependent FL flows. A-CoDa first uses label-distribution divergence (LDD)-based greedy-balanced clustering to construct statistically coherent and size-aware client groups, which serve as a scalable management abstraction. Building on this structure, we design FedMIX, an uncertainty-aware intra-/inter-cluster participation mechanism that ranks clients by a loss-latency-uncertainty utility and adaptively controls cross-cluster probing according to training progress and latency conditions. A dependency-aware DAG scheduler then orchestrates layer-wise task execution so that parallelism and precedence constraints are jointly respected. We further provide a convergence analysis that frames the result as a sufficient loss-domain design bound, explicitly relating the attainable error floor and sufficient communication rounds to LDDinduced sampling mismatch, residual distribution shift, local-SGD drift, stochastic variance, and adaptive probing budgets. Experiments on handwriting, wearable-sensing, product-image, and medical-imaging tasks evaluate A-CoDa under dependent FL workflows and demonstrate its effectiveness in reducing end-toend completion time while maintaining competitive accuracy.

</details>


### [105] [EfficientAgent: What Makes KV Cache Offloading Work for Concurrent Agents?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33762)
*Kunming Shao,Jierun Chen,Jiangnan Yu,Xiao-Hui Li,Chaofan Tao,Yanli Wang,Huanxin Lin,Kwang-Ting Cheng,Chi Ying Tsui,Haoli Bai*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: EfficientAgent 通过基于栈距离模型估算复用工作集来合理配置和管理主机内存层，解决了 LLM Agent 服务中 KV 缓存卸载效果不稳定的问题，显著降低了重计算和端到端延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM Agent 每轮对话都会重发整个上下文，现有服务系统通过缓存 KV 状态并在 GPU 内存不足时卸载到主机内存来避免重计算，但卸载效果在不同部署中表现不一（加速、减速或无变化），根本原因是缓存状态必须存活到再次被复用，而 Agent 并发执行导致前缀复用取决于主机层是否能容纳整个 Agent 池的复用工作集。

Method: 提出 EfficientAgent：1) 利用栈距离模型从 Agent 历史中估算复用工作集大小，以此确定主机层容量；2) 运行时策略：当主机层较小时，停止写入会被驱逐的大块上下文重填充，仅延伸仍在缓存中的前缀；当主机层足够大时，写入所有内容。

Result: 在 SWE-bench Verified 编码 Agent 上，按估算工作集配置主机层可使重计算提示词 token 减少 93%，端到端时间缩短 39%；固定小主机层下策略仍能减少 35% 重计算；大主机层下避免了始终过滤写入导致的 4.3 倍性能下降。跨三种 GPU 和两种模型实验表明，当 GPU 计算力与主机带宽比较低且主机层容纳工作集时，卸载收益最大。

Conclusion: EfficientAgent 通过工作集感知的主机层尺寸规划和自适应写入策略，有效解决了 LLM Agent 服务中 KV 缓存卸载的不稳定性问题，在资源受限环境下显著提升推理效率，代码已开源。

Abstract: LLM agents resend their whole conversation on every turn, and most of it was already processed on the previous turn. Serving systems avoid recomputing it by caching its key-value (KV) state and, when GPU memory runs out, by offloading that state to host memory. For agents, offloading gives inconsistent results: on the same coding-agent workload it speeds up one deployment, slows down another, and changes nothing on a third, even where loading a token back is several times cheaper than recomputing it. The reason is that cached state must survive until it is used again. While one agent waits for its tool, the server processes the contexts of all other agents, so an agent's prefix is reused only if the host tier holds the reusable context of the whole agent pool, which we call the reuse working set. A smaller tier keeps writing state that is evicted before anyone reads it. We present EfficientAgent, which sizes and manages the host tier by this working set. A stack-distance model estimates the working set from agent histories to size the host tier; its predictions, made before the experiments, located the capacity at which offloading starts to pay. When the tier is too small, a runtime policy stops writing large refills of evicted context and keeps extending prefixes that are still cached; when the tier is large enough, it writes everything. On SWE-bench Verified coding agents, a host tier sized to the estimated working set cuts recomputed prompt tokens by 93% and end-to-end time by 39%. With a small fixed tier, the policy cuts recomputation by 35%; with a large tier, it avoids the 4.3-fold increase caused by always filtering writes. Across three GPU types and two models, offloading pays off when the GPU has little compute per byte of host bandwidth and the host tier holds the working set. Code is available at https://github.com/KunmingSHAO/efficientagent_release.

</details>


### [106] [Performance vs Portability in Heterogeneous HPC Environments: Why Pre-execution Benchmarking is Required](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33847)
*Mindaugas Macernis*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 研究使用 Podman 对预编译的量子化学软件进行容器化与裸机性能基准测试，发现容器启动开销对短任务影响大，长运行计算需监控进度，性能受编译、启动配置和工作负载特征共同决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云计算（Kubernetes）和高性能计算（Slurm）范式不同，亟需在 HPC 环境中实现容器化科学应用的互操作性和性能评估。

Method: 利用用户级容器引擎 Podman，在 CPU‑优化编译的量子化学软件上分别进行容器化和裸机运行，跨多个计算节点进行基准测试，未使用额外网络优化，测量启动开销、执行时间和资源利用率。

Result: 容器准备阶段（拉取、启动）可主导短任务的总耗时；长时间计算则受软件编译质量和执行配置影响更大，需监控计算进度以防止性能退化。总体性能随工作负载长短和软件构建方式而变化。

Conclusion: 在 HPC 系统中使用容器部署科学应用时，必须基于具体工作负载进行基准测试，并提供用户级别的监控机制，以权衡容器带来的便利性与潜在的性能开销。

Abstract: Cloud computing and high-performance computing (HPC) typically follow different paradigms: cloud services are often orchestrated using Kubernetes, whereas HPC workloads are managed through batch schedulers such as Slurm. Growing demand for shared computational resources increases the need for interoperability between these environments. This study uses Podman as a user-accessible tool to benchmark quantum chemistry software distributed as precompiled executables. Containerized and on-system execution are compared across CPU-optimized builds and multiple compute nodes, without additional network optimizations. The results demonstrate that performance depends on software compilation, startup overhead, and execution configuration. Container preparation can dominate short-task workflows, while long-running calculations require monitoring of computational progress. These findings highlight the importance of workload-specific benchmarking and user-level monitoring when deploying scientific applications in containers on HPC systems.

</details>


### [107] [Validating Memory-Optimal Transformer Kernels on Real Hardware: From Formal Derivation to Measured Performance Across Two HPC Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33916)
*Lenore M. Mullin,Gaetan Hains*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过在两个高性能计算集群（CPU和GPU）上的实际性能测试，验证了基于数组数学（MoA）推导的Transformer内核内存最优成本函数。研究表明，硬件特定的优化可以作为固定、已验证的硬件无关规范（DNF）的常规机器相关实现（ONF）重写，为在不重新推导正确性的情况下将AI扩展到新硬件提供了候选方法论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证基于MoA理论推导的Transformer内核成本模型在真实、异构硬件环境（不同架构、不同网络拓扑的HPC集群）中的预测准确性，并探索一种将硬件无关的数学规范（DNF）系统性转化为机器相关实现（ONF）的通用工程方法。

Method: 在Purdue Anvil和NCSA Delta两个集群上，将 companion papers 中推导的DNF内核通过“gamma”转换为特定硬件的ONF实现。利用PyTorch进行精度对标验证，并结合硬件 profiling 工具，深入分析原子指令竞争、NUMA拓扑效应以及内存延迟停滞等底层瓶颈。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 修复了融合前向+后向在GPU上的性能回归，通过 targeted ONF 重写解决了原子指令竞争，实现高达2.5倍加速；(2) 相同推导的内核在不同拓扑结构的集群上实际成本差异巨大（535倍NUMA局部性惩罚 vs 不足3倍超额订阅）；(3) 发现C语言在CPU上快于Fortran，但在GPU上慢于Fortran的异常，定位到单一主导内核和内存延迟停滞机制。

Conclusion: 硬件特定的优化可以被视为在固定、已验证的DNF基础上进行常规的ONF重写。这一方法论为AI算法在不断演进的硬件平台上实现高效、可扩展的部署提供了无需重新推导和验证底层正确性的新途径。

Abstract: We validate memory-optimal cost functions for transformer kernels derived via the Mathematics of Arrays (MoA). Companion Papers I-IV formally derive kernels for attention forward, backward, fused forward+backward, decode, and the complete block (RMSNorm, gated MLP) as a hardware-independent specification (DNF) transformed to a machine-specific realization (ONF) via gamma, with verification to machine precision against PyTorch. This paper checks those predictions against measured performance on two HPC clusters (Purdue Anvil, NCSA Delta) across CPU and GPU.
  Three results stand out. (1) We identify and fix a GPU regression: fusing forward+backward, proven to avoid materializing an O(n^2) intermediate, initially ran slower than naive on GPU due to atomic contention. Profiling confirmed 2.00x more atomic instructions; a targeted ONF rewrite reversed it, yielding up to 2.5x speedup. (2) Identical derivations produce markedly different real costs by topology: 535x NUMA-locality penalty on one cluster vs <3x oversubscription on another, showing optimal deployment is a function of the machine's array structure. (3) We report a partially resolved anomaly: identical denotational computations run faster in C than Fortran on CPU but faster in Fortran than C on GPU, narrowed to one dominant kernel and one memory-latency stall mechanism (3.17x time gap matches 3.35x stall gap).
  We treat hardware-specific optimization as a routine ONF rewrite with fixed, verified DNF, a candidate methodology for scaling AI onto evolving hardware without re-deriving correctness.

</details>


### [108] [Kafila: Serving Large Language Models on a Trusted Set of Heterogeneous Commodity Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34045)
*Murtaza Rangwala,Richard O. Sinnott,Rajkumar Buyya*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Kafila is a protocol for running large language models on bounded groups of heterogeneous consumer devices, using a ring assembly and adaptive model partitioning to improve pipeline efficiency and throughput.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Small research groups or friend circles own multiple consumer devices too weak to run capable LLMs individually, and cannot use open swarms due to trust constraints. A bounded group must fully utilize every admitted device, requiring precise model division based on each device's capabilities before serving begins.

Method: Kafila assembles a ring topology from devices behind NATs, preferring direct paths and relaying where needed. Its planner measures memory bandwidth, capacity, and reachability of each device, divides the model exactly for a fixed ring order, and co-locates the head (embedding/output projection) with the optimal division point rather than placing it beforehand.

Result: On heterogeneous fleets, Kafila reduces the slowest pipeline stage by up to 5.2x compared to uniform splits (GPipe) and 3x compared to memory-proportional splits (exo). It maintains 75-87% hardware utilization where other methods drop below 50%, and enables running models impossible to place with uniform splits. In shared-network scenarios, it achieves 1.56x throughput over uniform splits and 1.25x over memory-proportional splits, scaling to 3.2x with four concurrent users.

Conclusion: Kafila effectively enables bounded groups of trusted, heterogeneous consumer devices to run capable LLMs with significantly improved efficiency and throughput compared to existing partitioning strategies, making collaborative inference practical for privacy-conscious users.

Abstract: Between them, the members of a research group or a circle of friends own several consumer computers, none large enough to run a capable large language model. Existing systems pool such capacity across open swarms anyone may join, which a group admitting only trusted machines cannot use. Bounding membership removes what they depend on: a swarm holds each part of the model on several peers and routes around a slow one. A bounded session must use every device it admits. Its pipeline advances at the pace of whichever device received a share it cannot serve quickly, so the division has to be right before serving begins. We propose Kafila, whose protocol assembles a ring from behind NATs, preferring direct paths and relaying where traversal fails, while its planner measures each device's memory bandwidth, capacity and reachability, divides the model exactly for a fixed ring order, and places the head, which holds the embedding and output projection, together with that division rather than beforehand. On machines with different capabilities across three fleets, from a shared LAN to five devices spanning two continents, Kafila shortens the slowest pipeline stage by up to $5.2\times$ against the even split of pipeline parallelism, as in GPipe, and up to $3\times$ against the memory-proportional split of personal-device inference, as in exo, keeps 75 to 87 per cent of the committed hardware doing work where those divisions fall below half, and serves a model no uniform split can place on the fleet at all. What that is worth to a user depends on how much of a token is computation rather than network. Where the members share a network the same division returns $1.56\times$ the throughput of a uniform split and $1.25\times$ of a memory-proportional one, and under four concurrent users that lead compounds to $3.2\times$ rather than fading, each user served at almost the rate of one.

</details>


### [109] [Schedule Repair for DAG Workflows under Link Disruptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34048)
*Mohammadali Khodabandehlou,Jared Coleman,Bhaskar Krishnamachari,Kevin Chan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 在动态IoT网络中链路易失效，静态调度失效。本文提出一系列修复策略（等待、重新路由、局部重调度、全局重调度），在多种工作负载和不同通信-计算比率下评估，发现没有单一最佳范围，最佳策略取决于干扰特征和修复决策延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态或一般稳定网络的假设在争夺和对抗环境中不成立，链路因移动、干扰和干扰而降解或失效，需要研究调度修复。

Method: 提出修复策略谱：等待中断、绕路由、局部受影响任务重调度、全局待调度任务重调度。在100个工作负载（合成任务图、RIoTBench管道、WfCommons科学工作流）上，以五种通信-计算比率和不同相关结构的干扰过程运行，考虑决策延迟与移动范围成正比的代价。

Result: 没有单一范围普遍胜出：重新路由几乎消除了孤立故障，比等待好30%；全局修复在阻塞干扰下接近甲骨文（误差<4%）；在无记忆链路波动和通信密集型工作负载中，等待更优（类似路由振荡抑制）；自我修复的移动中断 favore 等待；考虑修复延迟后，大范围修复的效果先衰减。

Conclusion: 修复的范围应根据具体的干扰过程和修复成本进行自适应选择，而非由调度器固定。

Abstract: Schedules for directed acyclic graph (DAG) workflows in networked IoT systems are typically computed assuming a static or generally stable network. In contested and adversarial environments, this assumption is not valid. Links degrade and fail due to mobility, interference, and jamming. We study schedule repair: when a link disruption invalidates part of a schedule, how much of it should be rescheduled? We introduce a spectrum of repair policies that vary in repair scope, how much of the pending schedule each may move: wait out the disruption, reroute data around it, reschedule only the affected tasks locally, or reschedule all pending tasks globally. We evaluate each against an oracle and charge every repair a decision latency proportional to the extent to which it moves. Across 100 workload instances spanning synthetic task graphs, RIoTBench pipelines, and WfCommons scientific workflows, each run at five communication-to-computation ratios (CCRs) and disrupted by processes with deliberately different correlation structure, we find that no single scope wins: rerouting nearly erases isolated failures that cost waiting 30%, global repair comes within 4% of the oracle under jamming blackouts, waiting is favored under memoryless link flapping for larger and communication-heavy workloads (the scheduling analog of route-flap damping), self-healing mobility outages reward patience over reaction, and accounting for repair latency erodes large scopes first. We conclude that the scope of the repair should be adapted to the disruption process and the repair cost, rather than fixed by the scheduler.

</details>


### [110] [SlideDP: Scaling Host-Resident LLM Fine-Tuning Across Multiple GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34162)
*Ruijia Yang,Shiyuan Lin,Yulong Ao,Zhiyu Li,Yingli Zhao,Xianduo Li,Yonghua Lin,Zeyi Wen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: SlideDP is a synchronous data‑parallel runtime that decouples host‑state communication, pipelines parameter delivery and updates, and uses an analytical model plus runtime tuning to achieve 1.46‑2.64× higher throughput than existing methods and surpasses FSDP2 peak throughput by 11.2% on large‑batch training of LLMs. 


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Data‑parallel training of large language models on shared‑host multi‑GPU systems suffers from host‑resource contention, duplicated transfer traffic, and inefficient scaling as computation per rank shrinks, limiting full‑parameter fine‑tuning beyond GPU memory.

Method: SlideDP maintains a single authoritative host state, separates communication routes from state layout, and pipelines parameter delivery, gradient aggregation, and CPU updates across ranks and chunks, guided by an analytical step‑time model and runtime‑based policy adjustments under a GPU memory budget.

Result: In matched‑batch sweeps SlideDP attains geometric‑mean speedups of 1.46–2.64× over SlideFormer, MegaTrain and ZeRO‑Offload; on four H100 GPUs it reaches GPU‑resident FSDP2 throughput for Qwen3‑14B and exceeds FSDP2 peak by 11.2%, while also handling 256K‑token sequences and fine‑tuning Qwen2.5‑72B on four RTX 4090 GPUs.

Conclusion: By efficiently utilizing host resources and pipelining communication and computation, SlideDP enables scalable, high‑throughput training of very large language models beyond GPU memory, outperforming prior data‑parallel solutions.

Abstract: Host-resident layer streaming enables full-parameter LLM fine-tuning beyond GPU memory, but data-parallel ranks compete for shared host resources. Replicated transfers amplify traffic, while strong scaling can expose host work as computation windows shrink. We present SlideDP, a synchronous data-parallel runtime for shared-host multi-GPU systems. It maintains one authoritative host state, decouples communication routes from state layout, and pipelines parameter delivery, gradient aggregation, and CPU updates across ranks and chunks. An analytical step-time model characterizes resource bottlenecks and pipeline exposure; runtime measurements guide communication, chunking, and activation policies under a GPU memory budget. In matched-batch sweeps, SlideDP achieves geometric-mean throughput ratios of 1.46-2.64$\times$ over SlideFormer, MegaTrain, and ZeRO-Offload. On four H100s, SlideDP approaches GPU-resident FSDP2 throughput for Qwen3-14B at a smaller batch size. With a larger batch, it processes over 1M tokens per step and exceeds FSDP2's measured peak throughput by 11.2%. Separately, it supports 256K-token sequences for the same model and fine-tunes Qwen2.5-72B on four RTX 4090 GPUs. Project page: https://github.com/RegiaYoung/SlideDP.

</details>


### [111] [Arachne: Learning to Plan Parallel Training on Dynamic Heterogeneous Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34244)
*Taeyoon Kim,Yonguk Song,Seoyeong Choy,Hexiao Duan,Dong Li,Seo Jin Park,Myeongjae Jeon*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Arachne 是一种面向共享 GPU 基础设施的大模型训练规划方法，通过学习基模板构造高吞吐量训练计划，解决 GPU 动态可用性和硬件异构性带来的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在共享 GPU 环境下，GPU 可用性动态变化且硬件异构性持续累积，使得训练规划面临巨大搜索空间和实时性要求，现有规划器难以兼顾效率与性能。

Method: Arachne 将完整规划问题简化为对流水线结构的搜索，通过结构化模板封装规划决策，并在多样化集群配置下离线学习从模板构建训练计划，利用结构决策作为性能关键核心，其余部分通过规则或少量候选集完成。

Result: 评估显示，Arachne 在不同 GPU 类型和节点尺寸的集群上，三种不同规模模型的测试中，其吞吐量比五种已有规划器的最佳结果高出多达 84.5%（稠密模型）和 4.6 倍（MoE 模型）。

Conclusion: Arachne 通过学习驱动的模板规划方法有效应对 GPU 动态性和异构性挑战，在保持高吞吐量的同时实现快速规划，为共享 GPU 基础设施上的大模型训练提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Training large machine learning models on shared GPU infrastructures faces two challenges: (1) GPU availability shifts dynamically with varying resource demands from tenants, and (2) hardware heterogeneity accumulates as datacenters continuously adopt new GPU generations. Due to the vast search space induced by heterogeneous GPU types and node sizes, training planners must prune it aggressively to remain tractable, yet must also derive high-throughput plans promptly as cluster configurations change. Arachne achieves this goal through a learning-based planner that reduces the full planning problem to a search over pipeline structures. Arachne encapsulates planning decisions in a structural template and learns to construct plans from templates over diverse cluster configurations offline. This design is effective because structural decisions constitute the performance-critical core of a parallelism plan, while the rest follows by rule or from a small priced candidate set once the plan structure is fixed. Evaluation shows that Arachne matches or exceeds the best plan found by five existing planners across clusters with varying GPU types and node sizes for three models of different sizes by up to 84.5% in throughput on dense models and 4.6x on MoE models.

</details>


### [112] [HyDra: Demystifying and Taming Dynamic Context Parallelism at Production Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34318)
*Zihao Fan,Yunzhuo Liu,Bo Jiang,Changgang Zheng,Lin Zheng,Ray Ying,Key Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: HyDra 是一种可扩展的负载驱动动态上下文并行系统，通过调度器平衡计算负载并采用嵌套并行策略，显著减少流水线气泡并提升长上下文训练吞吐率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的动态上下文并行（DCP）系统要么无法扩展，要么在主流模型上表现不佳，Megatron-Core (Mcore) DCP 是唯一可用于生产规模的选项。然而 Mcore DCP 按内存需求分配并行度，忽略了注意力计算随序列长度呈二次增长的特性，导致相似 token 数下各秩计算时间差异高达 5 倍，产生 46% 的流水线气泡和 13% 的数据并行气泡。

Method: HyDra 提出负载驱动的调度器，将每个秩的负载拉向同一目标值，该目标可闭式求解；采用惰性堆而非全池扫描进行序列放置；CP 引擎将内层 Ulysses 组嵌入浅层外环，使更高并行度同时降低计算和通信开销。

Result: 在 512 GPU 测试平台上，HyDra 在 32K 上下文下平均提升吞吐 1.18 倍，在 256K 下提升 2.48 倍。在 2048 GPU 生产作业中，将流水线气泡从 36% 降至 14%，调度时间缩短 2.3 倍，吞吐比 Mcore DCP 提升 1.10-1.43 倍（平均 1.25 倍），比静态 CP 提升 1.33-1.90 倍（平均 1.59 倍）。

Conclusion: HyDra 通过负载均衡调度和嵌套并行机制，有效解决了长上下文训练中的计算负载不均问题，在大规模生产环境中显著降低气泡并提高吞吐率，是一种实用且可扩展的 DCP 方案。

Abstract: Long-context training runs on sequences whose lengths span orders of magnitude, and dynamic context parallelism (DCP) gives each sequence its own CP degree. Existing DCP systems either do not scale or perform poorly on mainstream models, leaving Megatron-Core (Mcore) DCP as the only option at production scale. Mcore DCP, however, sizes each degree to fit memory, which grows linearly with length while attention grows quadratically, so comparable token counts hide unequal computation. In our production 256K-context training job on more than 11K GPUs under Mcore DCP, per-rank microbatch times differ by up to 5x at similar token counts. The skew leads to a 46% pipeline bubble and a 13% data-parallel bubble. We present HyDra, a scalable load-driven DCP system. Its scheduler balances computation by pulling every rank toward one load target, and balance in turn makes that target solvable in closed form. It places sequences with lazy heaps rather than whole-pool scans. That balance asks for CP degrees larger than memory requires, so its CP engine nests an inner Ulysses group in a shallow outer ring, letting a higher degree lower computation and communication together. Evaluation at both scales shows consistent gains. On a 512-GPU testbed, HyDra raises throughput over Mcore DCP by 1.18x on average at 32K context and 2.48x at 256K. On a 2,048-GPU production job, it shrinks the pipeline bubble from 36% to 14%, cuts scheduling time by 2.3x, and raises throughput by 1.10-1.43x (avg. 1.25x) over Mcore DCP and 1.33-1.90x (avg. 1.59x) over static CP.

</details>


### [113] [Before Agents Act: Assurance-Aware Semantic Scheduling for Evidence Acquisition in Distributed Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34376)
*Jun He,Deying Yu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出保障感知语义调度（AAS），通过联合见证人选择与调度来满足法定人数、多样性、新鲜度、截止期限和资源约束，显著提升基础设施变更的证据获取效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用代理在启动基础设施变更时，需在证据可能失效或依赖共享故障域的前提下，同时满足法定人数、多样性、证据新鲜度、截止时间和资源限制，因而需要一种能够在约束下进行联合证据获取与调度的方法。

Method: AAS 整合了：1) 基于整数规划的见证人选择；2) 调度感知的时间调度；3) 有限的诊断扩展；4) 收据感知的修复机制。通过形式化分析给出调度时新鲜度和有限诊断扩展的前提条件；在三组人工基础设施工作负载上进行仿真评估，并进行参数敏感性研究与超时干预实验。

Result: 在三个工作负载中，AAS 产生的有效候选为 1075/1200，而基准的约束感知前向调度仅为 647/1200；过时候选从 440 下降到 12。敏感性研究表明，经过修正的超时干预在 20 个案例中实现了 18/20 的入场（通过修复或完全重新合成），静态计划为 0/20，且在重复使用收据时致造成本更低。在 20 个需要证实分解切割的构造案例中，细化步骤每次都能恢复与 oracle 匹配的可行计划。

Conclusion: AAS 在理论上保证了调度时的新鲜度和有限诊断扩展，并在受控仿真中显著优于基线方法；然而这些结果均来源于人工生成的工作负载，尚未在实际部署系统上验证。

Abstract: Tool-using agents can initiate consequential infrastructure changes, yet evidence required for admission may expire while other checks run or depend on a shared fault domain. We formulate evidence acquisition as joint witness selection and scheduling under quorum, diversity, freshness, deadline, and resource constraints. Assurance-Aware Semantic Scheduling (AAS) combines integer-program selection, dispatch-aware temporal scheduling, bounded diagnostic expansion, and receipt-aware repair. Formal results state the assumptions needed for dispatch-time freshness and finite diagnostic expansion. In three generated infrastructure workloads, AAS produces 1,075/1,200 valid candidates versus 647/1,200 for constraint-aware forward scheduling; stale candidates fall from 440 to 12. Paired sensitivity studies reuse the same instances and operation latency draws across parameter settings. A corrected timeout intervention finds 18/20 admissions with repair or full resynthesis versus 0/20 for a static plan, with lower committed cost when receipts are reused. On 20 constructed cases requiring a certified decomposition cut, refinement recovers an oracle-matching feasible plan every time. These are controlled simulation results; the bounded oracle shares a temporal search component, and transfer to deployed systems remains untested.

</details>


### [114] [Semantics, Workflows, and Infrastructure: Understanding Agent Serving at Production Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34432)
*Yihao Zheng,Jingzhe Jiang,Dejiang Zhu,Zhiyuan Tan,Yang Tian,Tao Wang,Minchen Yu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文分析了大规模生产环境中通用LLM代理平台的两周日志，包含1170万请求和超过1万GPU的推理基础设施，从任务发起、工作流执行、基础设施服务需求三个层面刻画工作负载特征，发现请求量高度倾斜、逻辑兄弟请求罕见重叠、上下文跨任务复用等模式，并据此提出部署启示与开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注请求级负载测量或代理执行分析，缺乏连接任务发起、工作流执行与推理基础设施的端到端视角，亟需全面理解生产级代理应用的行为对推理需求的影响以指导高效服务系统设计。

Method: 收集并分析一个大型通用代理平台的两周生产追踪数据（1170万请求、10k+ GPU），从任务级发起语义、工作流级执行模式、基础设施级服务需求三个关联层面进行表征，量化关键工作负载指标并识别典型模式。

Result: 发现会话间请求量高度倾斜（少数会话占多数请求）、逻辑兄弟请求在时间上极少重叠、上下文在任务边界间存在显著复用；这些模式对批处理、调度、缓存及资源分配有直接影响。

Conclusion: 基于实测洞察，论文指出了代理服务系统在请求调度、上下文管理、资源弹性伸缩等方面的开放问题，为未来高效LLM代理推理系统的研究提供了数据驱动的指导方向。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents execute applications through a workflow of inference requests with tool calls and user interactions. Serving these applications at production scale requires understanding how application behavior shapes inference demand and for guiding efficient execution. Recent characterization studies provide request-level workload measurements and agent execution analysis. However, an end-to-end view connecting task initiation, workflow execution, and inference infrastructure remains unexplored. In this paper, we analyze a two-week trace of 11.7 million requests from a large-scale production platform for general-purpose agents, backed by inference infrastructure comprising over 10k GPUs. We characterize the platform at three connected levels: task-level initiation semantics, workflow-level execution patterns, and infrastructure level serving demands. Our measurements reveal workload patterns such as highly skewed request volumes across sessions, rare execution overlap among logical sibling requests, and context reuse across task boundaries. Building on these observations, we analyze deployment implications and identify open problems to guide future research on agent serving systems.

</details>


### [115] [AgentWare: Automating the Lifecycle of Agentic Applications across the Edge-to-Cloud Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34586)
*Michalis Kasioulis,Moysis Symeonides,George Pallis,Marios D. Dikaiakos*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了 AgentWare，一种自动化边缘到云端连续体上代理应用程序供应、部署、可观测性和评估的 AgenticOps 框架。该框架通过端到端生命周期流程自动准备异构执行环境、转换用户定义的代理实现、部署代理组件并统一收集执行轨迹、基础设施遥测和评估指标，并支持通过 LLM-as-a-Judge 进行语义评估，生成涵盖正确性、性能、资源利用率和能耗的可重复报告。通过在真实 Edge-to-Cloud 基础设施上部署分布式书籍助手代理进行实证验证，结果表明 AgentWare 显著降低了分布式代理应用的部署、仪器化和分析的人工工作量，并实现了系统化的实验和评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在边缘到云端连续体中部署 LLM 驱动的代理应用程序面临硬件异质性、部署复杂性、有限的可观测性以及缺乏系统化评估方法等挑战。现有解决方案仅在代理开发、可观测性或基准测试方面独立解决问题，对分布式代理应用的完整生命周期支持有限。

Method: 本文提出 AgentWare，一种 AgenticOps 框架，引入了端到端生命周期流程，自动化准备异构执行环境、转换用户定义的代理实现为分布式应用、在连续体上部署代理组件，并统一收集执行轨迹、基础设施遥测和评估指标。框架进一步支持通过 LLM-as-a-Judge 工作流进行语义评估，并生成涵盖正确性、性能、资源利用率和能耗的可重复报告。

Result: 通过在真实的 Edge-to-Cloud 基础设施上、在多种部署和模型配置下部署分布式书籍助手代理进行实证验证。结果表明，AgentWare 能够实现分布式代理应用的系统化实验和评估，并显著减少了部署、仪器化和分析所需的手动工作量。

Conclusion: AgentWare 通过提供从环境准备到统一评报的完整自动化管道，有效解决了边缘到云端连续体中代理应用部署和评估的挑战，为分布式代理系统的系统化实验和可持续部署提供了可扩展的框架。

Abstract: Deploying LLM-enabled agentic applications across the Edge-to-Cloud continuum remains challenging due to hardware heterogeneity, deployment complexity, limited observability, and the lack of systematic evaluation methods. Existing solutions address agent development, observability, or benchmarking separately, offering limited support for the full lifecycle of distributed agentic applications. This paper presents AgentWare, an AgenticOps framework that automates the provisioning, deployment, observability, and evaluation of agentic applications across Edge-to-Cloud infrastructures. AgentWare introduces an end-to-end lifecycle pipeline that automatically prepares heterogeneous execution environments, transforms user-defined agent implementations into distributed applications, deploys agent components across the continuum, and performs unified collection of execution traces, infrastructure telemetry, and evaluation metrics. The framework further supports automated semantic evaluation through LLM-as-a-Judge workflows and generates reproducible reports covering correctness, performance, resource utilization, and energy consumption. We demonstrate the applicability of AgentWare through a distributed book assistant agent deployed across real Edge-to-Cloud infrastructure under multiple deployment and model configurations. The results show that AgentWare enables systematic experimentation and evaluation of distributed agentic applications while significantly reducing the manual effort required for deployment, instrumentation, and analysis.

</details>


### [116] [Nereus: Adaptive Parallelism for LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34645)
*Songlin Jiang,Tuo Shi,Sitong Zhang,Zeke Wang,Mario Di Francesco,Bo Zhao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Nereus 是一个成本感知的运行时系统，实时自适应调整大型语言模型强化学习后训练作业的执行计划，通过弹性模型单元和全局转换图优化 GPU 资源分配与转移，显著降低延迟并提升吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL post-training for LLMs involves dynamic factors (resource availability, sequence length, memory pressure, stage bottlenecks) that render initially suitable execution plans slow or infeasible over time. Adapting such jobs across shared GPUs faces challenges in transition cost assessment, distributed state reuse, and GPU transfer coordination.

Method: Nereus 采用低开销控制器选取内存可行的全局计划，并利用针对运行中作业校准的成本模型确认是否进行转换。它将每个模型-阶段的副本表示为弹性模型单元，并通过全局转换图统一调度转换与 GPU 转移操作。

Result: 实验 trace 显示在线 TP/PP 自适应使平均步延迟相对于固定 TP/PP 布局下的 DP 扩展降低 27.7%。在 1000 步、1024 GPU 的运行中，6 次转换仅消耗 0.079% 的总运行时间。Nereus 在多样集群上使 8B PPO 的 end-to-end 吞吐量较 OpenRLHF 提升 2.14–7.27×，较 Verl 提升 1.10–1.47×。

Conclusion: Nereus 展示了在 RL post-training 工作负载中，通过低开销的运行时自适应，既能有效应对动态资源变化，又能在大规模 GPU 集群上实现显著的性能提升与成本最小化。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training for large language models (LLMs) coordinates multiple models across generation, inference, and training on GPU clusters. Several factors may change during a run, including resource availability, sequence length, memory pressure, and stage bottlenecks. As a consequence, an execution plan that was initially suitable can then become slow or even infeasible over time. However, adapting a job whose models share GPUs entails significant challenges: deciding whether a new plan is worth the transition cost, reusing the job's distributed state, and coordinating GPU transfers across models and stages.
  Nereus targets these challenges as a cost-aware runtime that adapts RL post-training jobs into efficient execution plans. Its low-overhead controller selects a memory-feasible global plan and admits the transition using a cost model calibrated against the running job. To estimate and execute a transition, Nereus represents the distributed state of each replica of a model-stage (one model in one stage) as an Elastic Model Unit. It then employs a global transition graph to order the transformations and GPU transfers of these units. In a trace built from real data, online TP/PP adaptation reduces average step latency by 27.7% relative to the initial fixed TP/PP layout with DP scaling. In a 1,000-step run reaching 1,024 GPUs, six transitions consume 0.079% of total run time. Nereus improves end-to-end 8B PPO throughput by 2.14--7.27$\times$ over OpenRLHF and by 1.10--1.47$\times$ over Verl across diverse clusters.

</details>


### [117] [Distributed Lower Bounds via Automatic Self-Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34753)
*Alkida Balliu,Francesco d'Amore,Dennis Olivetti*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文证明了 Khoury 和 Schild 提出的‘自归约轮消去’（round elimination via self-reduction）实际上是经典轮消去（classical round elimination）的一种特例，从而将其转化为一种通用的下界证明技术。通过引入一种新的算法误差度量方式，经典轮消去框架得以突破限制，能够产生 $\\omega(\\log \\log n)$ 的随机化下界。作者确定了一大类可通过黑盒方式自动获得更强下界的图论问题，并作为应用证明了极大匹配、$\\frac{1}{k}$-整数匹配和极大 $H$-打包等问题在 LOCAL 模型中需要 $\\Omega(\\sqrt{\\log n})$ 轮随机化算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典轮消去技术是分布式计算中证明下界的强大工具，但它似乎无法产生关于节点数 $n$ 的、超过 $\\omega(\\log \\log n)$ 轮的随机化下界。虽然近期提出的‘自归约轮消去’突破了这一限制并证明了极大匹配需要 $\\Omega(\\sqrt{\\log n})$ 轮，但该技术过于特化，仅针对极大匹配设计。本文的动机在于探索是否能将自归约推广为一种通用技术，从而将这种强大的下界证明能力应用到更广泛的图论问题中。

Method: 作者通过理论分析，证明了自归约在本质上是经典轮消去的一种特殊表现形式。核心方法是引入一种全新的算法误差度量（new way to measure the error of an algorithm）。在这种新度量下，重新应用经典的轮消去框架便能产生更强的随机化下界。此外，作者定义了一个通用的问题类（class of problems），展示了如何通过黑盒（black-box）方式，一旦证明某问题属于该类，即可自动推导出更强的随机化下界。

Result: 成功将自归约技术整合进经典轮消去框架，使其升级为一种通用的、黑盒式的下界生成工具。具体而言，识别出了一大类能够自动获得 $\\omega(\\log \\log n)$ 随机化下界的分布式图论问题。作为该方法的具体应用，成功获得了正则二部图上的极大匹配、$\\frac{1}{k}$-整数匹配以及极大 $H$-打包问题的 $\\Omega(\\sqrt{\\log n})$ 随机化下界。

Conclusion: 自归约并非独立于经典轮消去的全新技术，而是经典轮消去框架在特定误差度量下的自然延伸。通过改变误差度量标准，经典轮消去的能力得到了质的提升，突破了原有的下界瓶颈。这不仅统一了两种技术，还为未来在分布式 LOCAL 模型中系统性地证明更强大的随机化下界提供了一条通用且高效的全新途径。

Abstract: The development of round elimination into a general-purpose technique [PODC 2019] marked a turning point in our understanding of the hardness of many graph problems in the distributed setting and led to several breakthrough results. However, the round elimination technique seems unable to yield randomized lower bounds of $ω(\log \log n)$ rounds as a function of the number $n$ of nodes.
  Very recently, Khoury and Schild [FOCS 2025] introduced a new technique called round elimination via self-reduction, which bypasses the limitations of classical round elimination. Using this approach, the authors show that any randomized algorithm for maximal matching requires $Ω(\sqrt{\log n})$ rounds in the LOCAL model. Their elegant technique is, in some respects, similar to classical round elimination while being fundamentally different in others. However, it is tailored specifically to maximal matching rather than being applicable to a broad class of problems.
  In this paper, we show that self-reduction is, in fact, a special case of classical round elimination, thereby turning it into a general-purpose approach. In particular, we introduce a new way to measure the error of an algorithm and show that, under this new measure, classical round elimination can indeed yield $ω(\log \log n)$ randomized lower bounds. More specifically, we identify a large class of problems for which this improvement is entirely black-box: once a problem is shown to belong to the class, stronger randomized lower bounds follow automatically from the classical round-elimination framework. As an application, we prove $Ω(\sqrt{\log n})$ randomized lower bounds for a range of graph problems, namely, maximal matching on regular $2$-colored graphs, $\frac{1}{k}$-integral matching, and maximal $H$-packing.

</details>


### [118] [WaveAlign: Cache-Aware Query-Row Scheduling for Sparse Attention in Long-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34814)
*Zijian Dai,Sen Han,Youhui Bai,Shannon Wang,Kan Wu,Jingkai Huang,Yuhang Wang,Jing Li,Cheng Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: WaveAlign是一种面向动态稀疏注意力的轻量级缓存感知查询行重排序框架，通过低秩SVD分组和按K/V块数降序排序两个阶段提升L2缓存命中率，显著降低HBM流量并提升生成速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频生成中DiT模型产生极长的token序列，动态稀疏注意力虽减少计算量，但因查询行执行顺序不规则导致L2缓存局部性差和HBM访问增加，限制了实际加速效果。

Method: WaveAlign将行排序建模为优化问题并采用两阶段近似求解：第一阶段利用稀疏掩码行的低秩SVD表示，将K/V访问模式相似的查询行分组以提高并发行间K/V重叠度；第二阶段利用GPU流式调度将每波波内行按K/V块数降序排列，使当前波短行后接下一波长行，从而实现跨波边界的K/V访问对齐，允许共享块在被逐出前重复利用。同时设计自适应跳过模块避免不盈利的重排序，仅需置换查询和掩码行，不改变稀疏注意力语义及无需修改现有方法或后端内核。

Result: 在两款GPU架构、两个视频DiT模型和四种稀疏注意力方法上，WaveAlign将L2缓存命中率从28.48%–36.35%提升至79.38%–89.06%，HBM读取流量减少高达92.11%，Kernel加速至1.25x、端到端生成加速1.17x，无质量损失。

Conclusion: WaveAlign通过巧妙的查询行重排序有效解决了动态稀疏注意力的缓存局部性问题，在保持语义不变和兼容现有方法的前提下，显著提升长视频生成推理效率，为Diffusion Transformer的大规模部署提供了实用价值的优化方案。

Abstract: Long-video generation with diffusion transformers (DiTs) produces extremely long token sequences, making attention a dominant inference bottleneck. Dynamic sparse attention reduces computation, but its realized speedup remains limited because irregular query-row execution degrades L2 cache locality and increases HBM traffic. We present WaveAlign, a lightweight, cache-aware query-row reordering framework for dynamic sparse attention. WaveAlign formulates row ordering as an optimization problem and approximates it with two stages. The first stage derives a low-rank SVD representation of sparse-mask rows and groups query rows with similar K/V access patterns, increasing K/V overlap among concurrently scheduled rows. The second stage exploits streaming GPU scheduling by sorting rows within each wave in descending order of their K/V-block counts, so that short rows from the current wave are followed by long rows from the next. This aligns K/V accesses across wave boundaries and enables shared blocks to be reused before eviction. An adaptive skip module avoids unprofitable reordering. By only permuting query and mask rows, WaveAlign preserves sparse-attention semantics and requires no changes to existing methods or backend kernels. Across two GPU architectures, two video DiTs, and four sparse-attention methods, WaveAlign raises the L2 cache hit ratio from 28.48%--36.35% to 79.38%--89.06%, reduces HBM read traffic by up to 92.11%, and achieves up to 1.25x kernel and 1.17x end-to-end generation speedup without quality loss.

</details>


### [119] [TempoKV: Timely Staging of LLM KV Caches for Memory-Semantic Flash](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35065)
*Jay H. Park,Hyungjun Kim,Dong Kim*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: TempoKV 提出了一种时序感知的资源承诺层，用于优化大语言模型（LLM）服务中的 KV 缓存管理。它将可复用 KV 的元数据声明与物理资源的实际分配解耦，仅在检索时间窗匹配时才预留快速层容量。实验表明，TempoKV 在几乎不牺牲服务性能的前提下，将受保护的快速层字节时间减少了 63-91%，并显著降低了 p95 TTFT，提升了输出吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大模型服务中，前缀 KV 缓存的容量可能远超 GPU 显存，通常需要结合 SSD 和有限的快速层（如 CXL 内存）进行分层存储。然而，逻辑上的 KV 命中并不等同于数据已准备好供 GPU 检索。即时暂存会过早消耗宝贵的快速层容量，而按需暂存则面临 SSD 的高延迟暴露问题。如何平衡资源预留时机与数据就绪度是提升系统效率的关键瓶颈。

Method: TempoKV 引入了元数据-only 声明（metadata-only claims）机制，在早期识别到可复用 KV 时仅记录元数据，不立即占用快速层资源。它动态估算运行时‘距离检索的时间’和存储层‘使 KV 常驻并受保护所需的时间’，当估算的检索时间降至存储准备时间以下时，才在可用容量限制下触发资源承诺。该方法在不修改请求调度的前提下，集成至 vLLM 和 LMCache，并在支持 CXL 的 SSD 设备上实现。

Result: 实验结果显示，相比即时暂存，TempoKV 将每个请求的受保护快速层字节时间减少了 63-91%，同时保留了大部分服务收益。在快速层容量从 100 GiB 缩减至 25 GiB 的测试中，输出吞吐量和 p95 首 token 时间（TTFT）几乎保持不变。此外，相比未修改的 LMCache，它将 p95 TTFT 最多降低了 48.0%，并将输出吞吐量最多提升了 27.8%。

Conclusion: TempoKV 通过精细化的时序感知资源承诺，证明了将‘复用知识’与‘资源获取’解耦能够显著提升分层存储中快速层的利用效率。该方案在不牺牲 LLM 服务延迟和吞吐量的前提下，大幅降低了对大容量快速存储的依赖，为构建高效且经济的 LLM 推理系统提供了新的设计思路。

Abstract: Reusable prefix key-value (KV) caches can outgrow GPU memory in large language model (LLM) serving. A memory-semantic flash hierarchy offers SSD-backed capacity with a limited fast tier, but a logical KV hit is not necessarily ready for GPU retrieval. Demand staging exposes SSD latency, whereas immediate staging can reserve fast-tier capacity long before retrieval begins. We present TempoKV, a timing-aware resource-commitment layer that separates early knowledge of reuse from the acquisition of staging resources. It records reusable-KV hits as metadata-only claims and requests commitment when the runtime-estimated time until retrieval falls to the storage-estimated time needed to make KV resident and protected against eviction. These estimates adapt to runtime progress and staging state, while commitment remains subject to available protected capacity. We implement TempoKV in vLLM and LMCache on an SSD-backed CXL memory device without changing request scheduling. Across two models and three prefix cache ratios, TempoKV reduces protected fast-tier byte-time per request by 63-91% versus immediate staging while retaining much of the serving benefit of advance staging. In a fast-tier capacity sweep, output throughput and p95 time to first token (TTFT) remain nearly unchanged as capacity decreases from 100 to 25 GiB. Compared with unmodified LMCache's Device-DAX L1 configuration, TempoKV reduces p95 TTFT by up to 48.0% and increases output throughput by up to 27.8%.

</details>


### [120] [AReaL-TIK: Stateful Agentic Optimization of Unified RL Kernels through an Optimization IR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35140)
*Ran Yan,Youhe Jiang,Jiayi Nie,Wenshuang Li,Yingqi Peng,Taiyi Wang,Tongkai Yang,Binhang Yuan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出 KernelBraid，一个智能体框架，通过位级一致的统一内核实现强化学习后训练中的推理和策略更新复用，避免数值不一致并提升吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在同步 PPO 和 GRPO 中，分别使用不同 GPU 内核进行 rollout 与策略更新会导致数值分歧，需要重新计算 rollout 概率以保持一致，但会增加前向传播开销。需要一种既能保证位级一致又能复用内核的优化方法。

Method: KernelBraid 从手工调优的位级一致实现出发，构建优化中间表示 (IR)，将源码搜索与数值需求、工作负载测量及推导历史关联。智能体协调修改、保留经过正确性检验的中间结果，并通过延迟改进进行提升。在多种算子和端到端配置上进行搜索与验证。

Result: 在 H20 上的 12 种端到端训练配置中，相比 AReaL 重新计算 log‑prob 的方案，KernelBraid 实现平均吞吐提升 1.10x，训练奖励比约为 1.00x；隔离层 profiling 显示累计阶段时间平均加速 1.40x；在 A100、H20、H200 上的 10 种算子均通过位级一致性检验；统一注意力搜索相较于起始实现实现 2.52x 的延迟降低。

Conclusion: KernelBraid 通过智能体驱动的 IR 框架，成功在位级一致的前提下实现了 rollout 与策略更新内核的复用，显著提升了训练吞吐且不损害奖励，验证了该方法在不同硬件和模型上的有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training often uses distinct GPU kernels for rollout and policy update. In synchronous PPO and GRPO, numerical disagreement can perturb ratios between current token probabilities and those assigned during rollout. Recomputing rollout log-probabilities with the policy-update backend avoids this discrepancy but adds a forward pass. Bitwise-consistent unified kernels permit reuse when the policy snapshot and probability processing match the objective. Their optimization must preserve agreement across distinct execution regimes. We present KernelBraid, an agentic framework starting from a hand-tuned, bitwise-consistent implementation. Its optimization intermediate representation (IR) organizes source-code search by linking implementations and modifications to numerical requirements, workload measurements, and derivation history. The agent coordinates changes and retains verified intermediates for further exploration; promotion requires passing correctness checks and improving aggregate latency within per-workload limits. Across 12 end-to-end training configurations on H20, KernelBraid achieves 1.10x average throughput relative to AReaL with log-probability recomputation, and the mean training-reward ratio rounds to 1.00x. Isolated-layer profiling yields 1.40x average speedup in summed phase time across 15 model-GPU pairs. Operator-level evaluation covers correctness and performance for 10 operators on A100, H20, and H200, all passing the prescribed bitwise checks. Unified-attention search achieves 2.52x speedup in summed workload latency over the starting implementation using 7M LLM tokens; ablations assess the contributions of retained evidence and branch exploration to search efficiency and attained performance. Our code is open-sourced at https://github.com/areal-project/AReaL-TIK.

</details>


### [121] [Analyzing Solana's Blocks and Transactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35171)
*Yaron Hay,Dvir David Biton,Roy Friedman*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文分析了百万块Solana区块，提供区块大小、交易执行时间、费用、程序调用及冲突图等统计数据，以评估网络并行潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 了解Solana智能合约的类型及其交互对区块链设计者和开发者优化并行性具有重要意义。

Method: 通过分析百万最近区块，提取块大小、交易时间单元、费用、已调用程序，并基于每笔交易的读写集构建冲突图。

Result: 统计了每块交易数、执行时间、费用分布以及冲突模式，并公开数据和代码。

Conclusion: 该研究为Solana的智能合约生态和并行扩展提供了实证洞察，有助于后续区块链设计与开发。

Abstract: Solana is one of the most popular blockchains, and is arguably the most widely used blockchain for smart contracts, also known as dApps. Understanding the types of smart contracts that are being executed by Solana and their interplay is therefore highly beneficial both for designers of modern blockchains and developers of smart contracts. To that end, in this paper we analyze a million recent Solana blocks. We report statistics about the size of blocks (number of transactions per block), execution time units for individual transactions and fees, and invoked Solana programs. Further, based on the declared readset and writeset of each transaction, as mandated by Solana, we analyze the conflicts and corresponding conflict graphs arising within each block. These latter statistics are important to understand the potential for parallelism in the network, which is one of the main claimed benefits of Solana. The data and code are available in open source.

</details>


### [122] [WavePP: High-Throughput Pipeline Parallel LLM Prefill under Prefix Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35263)
*Aaryam Sharma*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: WavePP 是一种基于 TensorRT-LLM 的预填充运行时，通过异步跨阶段前缀复用和动态分块规划，显著提升流水线并行预填充吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流水线并行虽能提升预填充吞吐，但各阶段独立管理缓存导致跨阶段前缀复用难，协调开销降低请求准入节奏，限制整体吞吐。

Method: WavePP 重叠请求准入与流水线执行，异步寻找全阶段可复用前缀、保护缓存状态、预留剩余输入空间，并动态规划各请求分块大小以最大化流水线填充，各阶段在本地完成准备后执行请求。

Result: 在相同系统和拓扑下，WavePP 在 40 个测试设置中的 37 个上超越 TensorRT-LLM；并发 128 且高缓存复用时，GLM 5.2 和 MiniMax M2.7 吞吐分别提升 2.91 倍和 2.02 倍；在 28 个 Kimi K3 设置中，并发 8 及以上的 18 个设置均达到最高吞吐，优于 TRT-LLM、SGLang、vLLM 的张量/专家并行及流水线并行基线。

Conclusion: WavePP 通过跨阶段缓存协调与动态分块调度，有效解决了流水线并行预填充中的缓存复用与调度开销问题，实现了显著的吞吐量提升，适用于高并发、高缓存复用场景。

Abstract: Pipeline parallelism can improve prefill throughput by processing multiple request chunks concurrently across different stages of the model. However, keeping the pipeline fully utilized requires efficient scheduling and request preparation. In systems where stages retain and evict cache state independently, a local cache hit does not guarantee that the same prefix can be reused across the pipeline. Here, coordination overhead can impede request admission cadence and thus reduce overall throughput. In this paper, we present WavePP, a prefill runtime built on top of TensorRT-LLM that addresses these challenges by overlapping request admission with pipeline execution. WavePP asynchronously finds a prefix that can be reused across all stages, protects the cached state, and reserves space for the remaining input while earlier requests continue to execute. It subsequently plans the chunk sizes of each request dynamically to maximize pipeline fill. Each stage then completes the local preparation before executing the request. In the same system and pipeline topology, WavePP improves TensorRT-LLM's prefill throughput in 37 of 40 tested settings on GLM 5.2 and MiniMax M2.7. At concurrency 128 with high cache reuse, these changes increase throughput by factors of 2.91 and 2.02, respectively. Across 28 Kimi K3 settings, WavePP also has the highest measured throughput in all 18 settings at concurrency eight or higher, compared with tensor/expert-parallel and pipeline-parallel baselines from TRT-LLM, SGLang, and vLLM.

</details>


### [123] [TopoEP: Topology-Aware Load Balancing for Expert-Parallel MoE Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35481)
*Jiacheng Zhu,Xie Zhao,Gongming Zhao,Hongli Xu,Yao Fei,Jin Fang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: TopoEP is a GPU-native, topology-aware load-balancing system for large-scale MoE training that reduces straggler-induced bottlenecks and improves training throughput by 6.2%-11.4% on a 32-GPU cluster.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dynamic routing in MoE training creates severe load imbalance where certain GPUs hosting 'hot' experts become stragglers, forcing other ranks to wait and reducing overall training efficiency. Existing EPLB systems suffer from expensive CPU-based planning with device-host data transfers and cross-rank synchronization, while ignoring hierarchical communication costs in modern GPU clusters.

Method: TopoEP employs a GPU-native approach using a deterministic solver that first performs inter-node placement followed by intra-node refinement. It allows all ranks to independently generate bitwise-identical plans without data-dependent host synchronization, eliminating costly cross-rank coordination during scheduling.

Result: Integration of TopoEP with Megatron-LM on a 32-GPU NVIDIA H800 cluster achieves end-to-end training throughput improvements of 6.2%-11.4% across three representative MoE models.

Conclusion: TopoEP effectively addresses load balancing challenges in large-scale MoE training by enabling topology-aware, GPU-native scheduling that eliminates straggler effects and delivers significant performance gains.

Abstract: Dynamic routing creates severe load imbalance in large-scale expert-parallel Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) training, turning GPUs that host hot experts into stragglers. As each MoE layer waits for its slowest rank, these stragglers prolong the expert-parallel stage and reduce overall training efficiency. Existing expert-parallelism load-balancing (EPLB) systems commonly compute load-balancing plans on the CPU, incurring device--host data transfers and cross-rank synchronization that make scheduling at every layer and microbatch expensive. Their planning formulations also overlook the hierarchical communication costs of modern scale-up and scale-out GPU clusters.
  We present \textit{TopoEP}, a GPU-native, topology-aware load-balancing system for large-scale MoE training. At each MoE layer and training microbatch, \textit{TopoEP} converts the current routing result into hot-expert replication and token-rerouting decisions and executes the resulting plan without data-dependent host synchronization, reducing critical-path overhead. To generate these decisions, \textit{TopoEP} uses a deterministic GPU solver that performs inter-node placement followed by intra-node refinement, allowing all ranks to independently produce bitwise-identical plans. On a 32-GPU NVIDIA H800 cluster, integrating \textit{TopoEP} with Megatron-LM improves end-to-end training throughput by 6.2\%--11.4\% across three representative MoE models.

</details>


### [124] [GPUPhysBench: Benchmarking Coding Agents for Correct and Efficient GPU Physics Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35639)
*Yuchen Sun,Jinjin He,Sinan Wang,Bo Zhu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: GPUPhysBench是一个包含50个物理仿真任务的基准测试，用于评估编码代理编写高性能GPU代码的能力，结果显示即使最强模型也难以达到专家优化的参考实现速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理仿真中编写快速GPU代码极具挑战，需兼顾数值精度、不规则数据访问、同步和迭代求解器，现有编码代理评估缺乏此类工作负载。

Method: 构建了GPUPhysBench基准，涵盖流体、可变形固体、颗粒材料等50个任务，从单个算子到完整模拟器；代理在固定时间预算内编写、编译、测试并优化GPU代码，评估通过率和相对于专家优化参考实现的运行时性能。

Result: 六个前沿模型-工具链组合中，最强的两个通过所有任务，但最快代理仅在22%任务上达到参考速度的0.9倍以上，且无提交比参考快超过5%；最大差距出现在碰撞检测、约束求解和迭代求解器。

Conclusion: GPUPhysBench将物理仿真工作负载引入编码代理评估，测试代理正确实现数值方法并使其高效运行的能力，揭示了当前代理在复杂GPU优化上的不足。

Abstract: Writing fast GPU code for physical simulation is difficult: implementations must preserve numerical accuracy while handling irregular data access, synchronization, and iterative solvers. We introduce GPUPhysBench, a benchmark of 50 tasks testing whether coding agents can meet these demands. Tasks cover fluids, deformable solids, and granular materials, from individual simulation operators to complete simulators. Agents write, compile, test, and optimize GPU code with access to a NVIDIA GPU under fixed time budgets. We report pass rates and runtime performance relative to expert-optimized reference implementations. In a single-attempt evaluation of six frontier model-harness pairs, the two strongest pass all 50 tasks, but even the fastest reaches at least 0.9 the reference speed on only 22% of them, and no submission is more than 5% faster than the reference. The largest gaps arise in collision detection, constraint solving, and iterative solvers. GPUPhysBench brings physical simulation workloads to coding-agent evaluation, testing both the ability to implement numerical methods correctly and the ability to make them run efficiently.

</details>


### [125] [Dynamic Wakeup under Costly Collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35662)
*Umesh Biswas,Maxwell Young*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对动态唤駃问题，提出Lowball算法在无碰撞检测、未知节点数n情况下，平衡延迟与碰撞成本。当碰撞成本C≥Kℓ^(1/ε)n时，期望延迟O(C^(1/2+ε)ln C)，期望碰撞成本O(√C)；否则两者均为O(n log^Θ(1/ε)n)，保证对抗自适应非预判对手，成功概率为1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统唤醒研究多关注首次成功的延迟，然而碰撞会引入额外延迟成本。为同时控制延迟与碰撞成本（C×碰撞次数），引入每碰撞成本C的代价模型，以反映实际系统总延迟成本。

Method: 提出Lowball随机算法，无需碰撞检测亦无需全局节点数n知识。算法仅依赖局部年龄和碰撞成本C调整传输概率，在动态对抗性环境下实现概率1的成功传输，无需全局参数即可运行。

Result: 当C≥Kℓ^(1/ε)n时，Lowball实现期望延迟O(C^(1/2+ε)ln C)和期望碰撞成本O(√C)；当C阈值以下时，两者期望均为O(n log^Θ(1/ε)n)。保证对抗自适应非预判对手，成功概率为1。

Conclusion: 对于仅依赖碰撞成本C和局部年龄的算法，期望延迟与碰撞成本的最大值下界为Ω(√C)，表明Lowball在C≥阈值时的性能接近最优，体现了成本与延迟的基本权衡，并证实Ω(√C)的下界为不可突破的界限。

Abstract: The wakeup problem captures a fundamental symmetry-breaking challenge among devices sharing a communication channel. We study the dynamic setting, where packets become active at arbitrary times on a time-slotted multiple access channel. In each slot, a transmission succeeds if and only if exactly one packet transmits; two or more simultaneous transmissions cause a collision. The goal is to obtain a successful transmission quickly.
  Prior work on wakeup has largely focused on the number of slots until the first success, referred to as the latency. However, a collision may incur substantial additional delay, represented by a per-collision cost $C$. We therefore seek to control both latency and the collision cost of an execution, defined as $C$ times its number of collisions.
  We design and analyze a randomized algorithm for dynamic wakeup, Lowball, without collision detection or knowledge of the number of packets, $n$. Fix a constant $0<ε\le 1/2$. There is a constant $K>0$ such that, when $C\ge K\lg^{1/ε} n$, Lowball has expected latency $O(C^{1/2+ε}\ln C)$ and expected collision cost $O(\sqrt{C})$. Below this threshold, both expectations are $O(n\log^{Θ(1/ε)} n)$. These guarantees hold against an adaptive, non-anticipating adversary, and the algorithm succeeds with probability 1. For algorithms in which each packet's transmission probability depends only on $C$ and the packet's local age, with packets activated together using the same probability schedule, we prove that the maximum of expected latency and expected collision cost is $Ω(\sqrt{C})$.

</details>


<div id='cs.OS'></div>

# cs.OS [[Back]](#toc)

### [126] [Dynamic Flow, Static Graph: KV Cache Reuse for Efficient LLM Serving on Mobile NPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34727)
*Zhengxiang Huang,Shengheng Chen,Chaoyue Niu,Yujie Sun,Zhaode Wang,Zeyu Zhao,Chengfei Lv,Fan Wu,Guihai Chen*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 提出移动端NPU专用的KV缓存重用机制，通过计算-存储协同设计、图内重计算映射和交叉图调度优化，实现KV缓存复用，显著降低首 token 生成时间（TTFT）40-60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的KV缓存设计主要针对云端GPU优化，假设动态执行环境和充足内存带宽，但移动NPU的静态编译要求和严重的内存/带宽限制使得这些设计无法直接应用于本地设备，导致延迟高、效率低。

Method: 提出计算-存储协同设计，包括：1) 图内机制将选择性KV重计算映射到静态NPU图；2) 交叉图调度器优化分块合并并最小化填充；3) 分层KV管理器采用树-哈希语义混合结构；4) 成本感知预取和驱逐策略；5) 双维流水线重叠KV加载、重排和存储与NPU执行，隐藏数据移动延迟。

Result: 实验显示，该设计将首token生成时间（TTFT）相比无重用和仅前缀缓存分别减少40-60%，在代表性的本地设备工作负载和LLM上验证有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究为移动端大语言模型本地服务提供了关键的架构解决方案，克服了传统云端优化的局限性，实现了更低延迟和更高的效率，为本地智能应用的部署奠定基础。

Abstract: On-device large language model (LLM) serving is a cornerstone of local-first personal intelligence, offering users data sovereignty, strong privacy guarantees, and freedom from cloud API latency and cost. Although KV caching is widely used to reduce latency in long-context inference, existing designs were primarily optimized for cloud GPUs with dynamic execution environments and abundant memory bandwidth. These architectural assumptions do not hold on mobile NPUs, where computation graphs must be statically compiled and both memory capacity and I/O bandwidth are severely constrained. In this work, we present a compute-storage co-design for mobile-centric prefix and non-prefix KV reuse. We first propose an intra-graph mechanism that maps selective KV recomputation onto static NPU graphs, reconciling algorithmic dynamicity with NPU staticity. We further develop an inter-graph scheduler to optimize chunk merging and minimize padding with dynamic programming. To address mobile bandwidth limitations, we introduce a hierarchical KV manager featuring a tree-hash-semantic hybrid structure, along with cost-aware prefetching and eviction policies. We also build a two-dimensional pipeline that overlaps KV loading, rerotation, and storage with NPU execution, hiding data-movement latency. Experiments across representative on-device workloads and LLMs show that our design reduces time-to-first-token (TTFT) by $40-60\%$ compared with no reuse and prefix-only caching.

</details>


### [127] [Planarian: Managing Agent State with Statepoints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35366)
*Jinnan Guo,Hao Mark Chen,Kapil Vaswani,Andrew Paverd,Peter Pietzuch*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: Planarian 提出了“代理状态点”（agent statepoints）抽象及三个核心管理原语（snapshot、rollback、fork），用于统一、高效地管理本地和远程环境状态，使代理能够安全地撤销错误并并行探索替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 代理在执行任务时会持续修改本地环境和远程服务状态。当前框架缺乏统一的抽象和机制来一致、高效地管理这些本地和远程状态，导致代理和用户难以安全地撤销探索性操作或从错误中恢复。

Method: Planarian 引入了“代理状态点”抽象，并提供三个核心原语：(i) `snapshot`：结合增量本地快照与远程补偿动作记录，创建无需外部服务支持检查点的跨环境状态点；(ii) `rollback`：通过恢复本地检查点并重放补偿动作，将环境恢复到先前状态；(iii) `fork`：从状态点分叉出多个隔离分支，支持并行探索。

Result: 实验结果显示，Planarian 能使代理撤销错误并并行探索替代方案，将任务质量提升高达 15 倍；同时，用户从错误操作中恢复时仅需付出 3% 的性能开销。
